`Agent/Core/DriverRiskScout-Core.ps1`:

```ps1
<#
.SYNOPSIS
    DriverRiskScout 1.1.5 - defensive Windows kernel-driver exposure and risk assessment.

.DESCRIPTION
    Read-only assessment focused on Windows kernel drivers. It inventories loaded,
    installed and DriverStore .sys files; hashes and verifies signatures; correlates
    exact hashes with LOLDrivers and Microsoft's vulnerable-driver blocklist; performs
    static PE/import/IOCTL/device-string heuristics; correlates recent Sysmon, Code
    Integrity and service-install events; detects HVCI/VBS/WDAC/ASR protections;
    identifies stale DriverStore copies; supports baseline/diff; optionally enriches
    CVEs with NVD; optionally runs bundled YARA rules; and writes CSV/JSON/HTML/Wazuh
    outputs.

    It does NOT send IOCTLs, exploit drivers, terminate processes, load/unload drivers,
    disable security controls or modify the assessed system.

    Device ACL probing is opt-in because opening a device handle can trigger a driver's
    IRP_MJ_CREATE path. Even when enabled, no DeviceIoControl/IOCTL is sent.
#>

[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [string]$OutputDirectory = (Join-Path (Get-Location) ("DriverRiskScout-{0}" -f (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd-HHmmss"))),
    [string]$LolDriversJson,
    [string]$MicrosoftBlocklistXml,
    [string]$ThreatProfileJson,
    [switch]$NoNetwork,
    [switch]$IncludeStopped,
    [switch]$SkipDriverStore,
    [switch]$SkipSystemDriversDirectory,
    [switch]$SkipEventLogs,
    [ValidateRange(1,3650)][int]$LookbackDays = 30,
    [string]$CompareBaseline,
    [switch]$EnableNvdLookup,
    [switch]$EnableYara,
    [string]$YaraExecutable,
    [switch]$EnableDeviceAclProbe,
    [switch]$EnableOnlineCertificateRevocation,
    [ValidateRange(1,500)][int]$MaxDeviceAclProbes = 50,
    [ValidateRange(1,32)][int]$MaxParallelism = 1,
    [string]$PersistentAnalysisCacheDirectory,
    [string]$WazuhOutputPath
)

Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

# Runtime requirement is validated before any scan work.

$ToolName = 'DriverRiskScout'
$ToolVersion = '2.2.0-compat-core'
$LolDriversApi = 'https://www.loldrivers.io/api/drivers.json'
$MicrosoftBlocklistDownload = 'https://aka.ms/VulnerableDriverBlockList'
$NvdApi = 'https://services.nvd.nist.gov/rest/json/cves/2.0'

function Write-Section { param([string]$Text) Write-Host ''; Write-Host ("=== {0} ===" -f $Text) -ForegroundColor Cyan }
function Get-Prop {
    param($Object,[string]$Name,$Default=$null)
    if($null -eq $Object){return $Default}

    # PowerShell 7 ConvertFrom-Json -AsHashtable is required for JSON objects
    # that contain keys differing only by case (for example init/INIT).
    # Support IDictionary objects as well as PSCustomObject transparently.
    if($Object -is [System.Collections.IDictionary]){
        try{
            foreach($k in $Object.Keys){
                if(([string]$k) -ceq $Name){ return $Object[$k] }
            }
            foreach($k in $Object.Keys){
                if(([string]$k) -ieq $Name){ return $Object[$k] }
            }
        }catch{}
        return $Default
    }

    try{
        $p=$Object.PSObject.Properties[$Name]
        if($null -ne $p){return $p.Value}
    }catch{}
    return $Default
}
function Html { param([object]$Text) if($null -eq $Text){return ''}; return [System.Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode([string]$Text) }
function Normalize-PathKey { param([string]$Path) if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Path)){return $null}; try{return ([IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Path)).TrimEnd('\').ToLowerInvariant()}catch{return $Path.TrimEnd('\').ToLowerInvariant()} }
function Get-RegistryDword { param([string]$Path,[string]$Name) try{$x=Get-ItemProperty -Path $Path -Name $Name -ErrorAction Stop; return [int]$x.$Name}catch{return $null} }

function Assert-PowerShell7 {
    if($PSVersionTable.PSVersion.Major -lt 7){
        throw "DriverRiskScout 1.1.5 requires PowerShell 7.x. Start it with pwsh.exe. Detected: $($PSVersionTable.PSVersion)"
    }
}
function Get-CountSafe {
    param([AllowNull()]$Value)
    if($null -eq $Value){ return 0 }
    return @($Value).Count
}


function ConvertFrom-JsonCompat {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Json)
    Assert-PowerShell7
    # -AsHashtable preserves JSON keys that differ only by case (e.g. init/INIT).
    return ($Json | ConvertFrom-Json -AsHashtable -Depth 100)
}

function Resolve-DriverImagePath {
    param([string]$PathName)
    if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($PathName)){return $null}
    $p=$PathName.Trim()
    if($p.StartsWith('"')){ $m=[regex]::Match($p,'^"([^\"]+)"'); if($m.Success){$p=$m.Groups[1].Value} }
    else { $m=[regex]::Match($p,'^(.*?\.sys)(?:\s|$)','IgnoreCase'); if($m.Success){$p=$m.Groups[1].Value} }
    $p=[Environment]::ExpandEnvironmentVariables($p)
    if($p -match '^\\\?\?\\'){$p=$p.Substring(4)}
    if($p -match '^\\SystemRoot\\'){$p=Join-Path $env:SystemRoot $p.Substring('\SystemRoot\'.Length)}
    elseif($p -match '^SystemRoot\\'){$p=Join-Path $env:SystemRoot $p.Substring('SystemRoot\'.Length)}
    elseif($p -match '^\\System32\\'){$p=Join-Path $env:SystemRoot $p.TrimStart('\')}
    elseif($p -match '^System32\\'){$p=Join-Path $env:SystemRoot $p}
    try{return [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($p)}catch{return $p}
}

function Get-ProtectionState {
    $hvci=Get-RegistryDword 'HKLM:\SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\DeviceGuard\Scenarios\HypervisorEnforcedCodeIntegrity' 'Enabled'
    $vbs=Get-RegistryDword 'HKLM:\SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\DeviceGuard' 'EnableVirtualizationBasedSecurity'
    $vuln=Get-RegistryDword 'HKLM:\SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\CI\Config' 'VulnerableDriverBlocklistEnable'
    $dg=$null; try{$dg=Get-CimInstance Win32_DeviceGuard -Namespace root\Microsoft\Windows\DeviceGuard -ErrorAction Stop}catch{}
    $vbsRuntime='Unknown'; $ci='Unknown'; $umci='Unknown'; $services=''
    if($dg){
        try{$vbsRuntime=switch([int]$dg.VirtualizationBasedSecurityStatus){0{'Not enabled'}1{'Enabled but not running'}2{'Running'}default{'Unknown'}}}catch{}
        try{$ci=[string]$dg.CodeIntegrityPolicyEnforcementStatus}catch{}
        try{$umci=[string]$dg.UsermodeCodeIntegrityPolicyEnforcementStatus}catch{}
        try{$services=(@($dg.SecurityServicesRunning) -join ',')}catch{}
    }
    $secureBoot='Unknown'; try{$secureBoot=[string](Confirm-SecureBootUEFI -ErrorAction Stop)}catch{}
    $asr='Unknown'; $asrState='Not configured/unknown'; $defender='Unknown'; $tamper='Unknown'
    try{
        $mp=Get-MpPreference -ErrorAction Stop
        $ids=@($mp.AttackSurfaceReductionRules_Ids); $actions=@($mp.AttackSurfaceReductionRules_Actions)
        $target='56a863a9-875e-4185-98a7-b882c64b5ce5'
        for($i=0;$i -lt $ids.Count;$i++){ if(([string]$ids[$i]).ToLowerInvariant() -eq $target){ $asr=[string]$actions[$i]; $asrState=switch([int]$actions[$i]){0{'Disabled'}1{'Block'}2{'Audit'}6{'Warn'}default{'Configured: '+[string]$actions[$i]}}; break } }
    }catch{}
    try{$mps=Get-MpComputerStatus -ErrorAction Stop; $defender=[string]$mps.AntivirusEnabled; $tamper=[string](Get-Prop $mps 'IsTamperProtected' 'Unknown')}catch{}
    $activePolicies=@(); try{$activePolicies=@(Get-ChildItem "$env:SystemRoot\System32\CodeIntegrity\CiPolicies\Active" -Filter '*.cip' -File -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -ExpandProperty FullName)}catch{}
    [PSCustomObject]@{
        HVCIRegistryEnabled=($hvci -eq 1); HVCIRaw=$hvci; VBSConfigured=($vbs -eq 1); VBSRuntimeStatus=$vbsRuntime;
        VulnerableDriverBlocklistEnabled=($vuln -eq 1); VulnerableDriverBlocklistRaw=$vuln;
        SecureBoot=$secureBoot; CodeIntegrityPolicyStatus=$ci; UserModeCodeIntegrityPolicyStatus=$umci;
        SecurityServicesRunning=$services; ActiveWdacPolicyCount=$activePolicies.Count; ActiveWdacPolicies=($activePolicies -join ';');
        DefenderAntivirusEnabled=$defender; DefenderTamperProtection=$tamper; VulnerableSignedDriverASRAction=$asr; VulnerableSignedDriverASRState=$asrState
    }
}

function Get-LolDriversData {
    param([string]$LocalPath,[switch]$Offline)
    if($LocalPath){
        if(-not (Test-Path $LocalPath -PathType Leaf)){throw "LOLDrivers JSON not found: $LocalPath"}
        $t=Get-Content $LocalPath -Raw
    }
    elseif(-not $Offline){
        try{[Net.ServicePointManager]::SecurityProtocol=[Net.SecurityProtocolType]::Tls12}catch{}
        $t=(Invoke-WebRequest $LolDriversApi -UseBasicParsing).Content
    }
    else{return @()}

    $converted=ConvertFrom-JsonCompat -Json $t
    return @($converted)
}

function New-LolDriversIndex {
    param([array]$Database)
    $sha=@{};$name=@{}
    foreach($e in $Database){ foreach($s in @(Get-Prop $e 'KnownVulnerableSamples' @())){ if($null -eq $s){continue}; $h=[string](Get-Prop $s 'SHA256' ''); $n=[string](Get-Prop $s 'Filename' ''); if($h){$k=$h.ToLowerInvariant(); if(-not $sha.ContainsKey($k)){$sha[$k]=New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList};[void]$sha[$k].Add([pscustomobject]@{Entry=$e;Sample=$s})}; if($n){$k=$n.ToLowerInvariant();if(-not $name.ContainsKey($k)){$name[$k]=New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList};[void]$name[$k].Add([pscustomobject]@{Entry=$e;Sample=$s})} } }
    [pscustomobject]@{BySha256=$sha;ByFilename=$name}
}

function Get-ThreatProfiles {
    param([string]$ExplicitPath)
    $path=$ExplicitPath
    if(-not $path){$path=Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'profiles\known-byovd.json'}
    if(-not (Test-Path $path -PathType Leaf)){return @()}
    try{return @((ConvertFrom-JsonCompat -Json (Get-Content $path -Raw)))}catch{Write-Warning ("Threat profile JSON could not be parsed: {0}" -f $_.Exception.Message);return @()}
}

function New-ThreatProfileIndex {
    param([array]$Profiles)
    $sha=@{}
    foreach($p in $Profiles){foreach($h in @(Get-Prop $p 'SHA256' @())){if(-not $h){continue};$k=([string]$h).ToLowerInvariant();if(-not $sha.ContainsKey($k)){$sha[$k]=New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList};[void]$sha[$k].Add($p)}}
    [pscustomobject]@{BySha256=$sha;Profiles=$Profiles}
}

function Get-ThreatProfileMatches {
    param($Index,[string]$Sha256,[string]$FileName,[string]$OriginalFilename,[array]$DeviceNames,[array]$IoctlCandidates,[array]$Imports)
    $matches=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
    $seen=@{}
    $ioHex=@($IoctlCandidates|ForEach-Object{([string](Get-Prop $_ 'Hex' '')).ToLowerInvariant()})
    $devNorm=@($DeviceNames|ForEach-Object{([string]$_).Trim().TrimStart('\\.').ToLowerInvariant()})
    $imp=@($Imports|ForEach-Object{([string]$_).ToLowerInvariant()})
    if($Sha256 -and $Index.BySha256.ContainsKey($Sha256.ToLowerInvariant())){
        foreach($p in @($Index.BySha256[$Sha256.ToLowerInvariant()])){$id=[string](Get-Prop $p 'ProfileId' '');$seen[$id]=$true;[void]$matches.Add([pscustomobject]@{Profile=$p;Confidence='Exact SHA256';Exact=$true;Evidence='sha256'})}
    }
    foreach($p in @($Index.Profiles)){
        $id=[string](Get-Prop $p 'ProfileId' '');if($seen.ContainsKey($id)){continue}
        $signals=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
        $names=@((Get-Prop $p 'DriverNames' @())|ForEach-Object{([string]$_).ToLowerInvariant()})
        if(($FileName -and $names -contains $FileName.ToLowerInvariant()) -or ($OriginalFilename -and $names -contains $OriginalFilename.ToLowerInvariant())){[void]$signals.Add('filename')}
        $expectedDevices=@((Get-Prop $p 'Devices' @())|ForEach-Object{([string]$_).Trim().TrimStart('\\.').ToLowerInvariant()})
        if(@($expectedDevices|Where-Object{$devNorm -contains $_}).Count){[void]$signals.Add('device')}
        $expectedIoctls=@((Get-Prop $p 'Ioctls' @())|ForEach-Object{([string]$_).ToLowerInvariant()})
        if($expectedIoctls.Count -and @($expectedIoctls|Where-Object{$ioHex -contains $_}).Count){[void]$signals.Add('ioctl')}
        $expectedImports=@((Get-Prop $p 'Imports' @())|ForEach-Object{([string]$_).ToLowerInvariant()})
        $importHits=@($expectedImports|Where-Object{$imp -contains $_})
        if($importHits.Count -ge [Math]::Min(2,[Math]::Max(1,$expectedImports.Count))){[void]$signals.Add('imports')}
        if($signals.Count -ge 3){[void]$matches.Add([pscustomobject]@{Profile=$p;Confidence='Heuristic';Exact=$false;Evidence=($signals -join '+')})}
    }
    return $matches.ToArray()
}

function Get-CvesFromEntry {
    param($Entry)
    $t=@([string](Get-Prop $Entry 'Description' ''),[string](Get-Prop $Entry 'Usecase' '')) + @((Get-Prop $Entry 'Resources' @())|ForEach-Object{[string]$_})
    return @(([regex]::Matches(($t -join ' '),'CVE-\d{4}-\d{4,}','IgnoreCase')|ForEach-Object{$_.Value.ToUpperInvariant()})|Sort-Object -Unique)
}
function Get-EntryResources { param($Entry) return @((Get-Prop $Entry 'Resources' @())|ForEach-Object{[string]$_}|Where-Object{$_}) }
function Get-PrimitiveClassFromText {
    param([string]$Text)
    $t=$Text.ToLowerInvariant()
    if($t -match 'terminate|process kill|kill process|edr kill'){return 'Process termination'}
    if($t -match 'physical memory|kernel memory|arbitrary write|arbitrary read|read/write'){return 'Kernel/physical memory access'}
    if($t -match 'elevat|privilege'){return 'Privilege escalation'}
    if($t -match 'signature enforcement|code integrity|\bdse\b'){return 'Code-integrity bypass'}
    if($t -match 'disable|bypass|tamper'){return 'Security-control bypass/tamper'}
    return 'See intelligence source'
}

function Get-MicrosoftBlocklistIndex {
    param([string]$ExplicitXml,[switch]$Offline,[string]$WorkingDirectory)
    $xmlFiles=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string];$source='Unavailable'
    if($ExplicitXml){if(-not (Test-Path $ExplicitXml -PathType Leaf)){throw "Blocklist XML not found: $ExplicitXml"};[void]$xmlFiles.Add((Resolve-Path $ExplicitXml).Path);$source='Explicit XML'}
    else{
        foreach($c in @("$env:SystemRoot\schemas\CodeIntegrity\ExamplePolicies\RecommendedDriverBlock_Enforced.xml","$env:SystemRoot\schemas\CodeIntegrity\ExamplePolicies\RecommendedDriverBlock_Audit.xml")){if(Test-Path $c){[void]$xmlFiles.Add($c)}}
        if($xmlFiles.Count){$source='Local Windows example policy'}
        elseif(-not $Offline){$z=Join-Path $WorkingDirectory 'VulnerableDriverBlockList.zip';$x=Join-Path $WorkingDirectory 'MSBlocklist';Invoke-WebRequest $MicrosoftBlocklistDownload -OutFile $z -UseBasicParsing;Expand-Archive $z $x -Force;foreach($f in Get-ChildItem $x -Filter '*.xml' -Recurse -File){[void]$xmlFiles.Add($f.FullName)};$source='Microsoft current package'}
    }
    $sha=@{};$fn=@{};$rules=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
    foreach($f in $xmlFiles){
        try{
            $text=Get-Content $f -Raw
            foreach($m in [regex]::Matches($text,'(?i)\bHash\s*=\s*"([0-9a-f]{64})"')){$h=$m.Groups[1].Value.ToLowerInvariant();if(-not $sha.ContainsKey($h)){$sha[$h]=New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList};[void]$sha[$h].Add($f)}
            [xml]$xml=$text
            $nodes=@($xml.SelectNodes('//*[local-name()="FileAttrib"]'))
            foreach($n in $nodes){
                $o=[pscustomobject]@{FileName=[string]$n.FileName;OriginalFileName=[string]$n.OriginalFileName;InternalName=[string]$n.InternalName;ProductName=[string]$n.ProductName;MinimumFileVersion=[string]$n.MinimumFileVersion;MaximumFileVersion=[string]$n.MaximumFileVersion;Source=$f}
                [void]$rules.Add($o)
                foreach($candidate in @($o.FileName,$o.OriginalFileName)){if($candidate){$k=$candidate.ToLowerInvariant();if(-not $fn.ContainsKey($k)){$fn[$k]=New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList};[void]$fn[$k].Add($o)}}
            }
        }catch{Write-Verbose "Blocklist parse failed for ${f}: $($_.Exception.Message)"}
    }
    [pscustomobject]@{BySha256=$sha;ByFilename=$fn;Rules=$rules.ToArray();XmlFiles=$xmlFiles.ToArray();Source=$source;ExactSha256Rules=$sha.Count;FileAttributeRules=$rules.Count}
}

function Read-U16 {param([byte[]]$B,[int]$O) if($O+2 -gt $B.Length){return 0};return [BitConverter]::ToUInt16($B,$O)}
function Read-U32 {param([byte[]]$B,[int]$O) if($O+4 -gt $B.Length){return 0};return [BitConverter]::ToUInt32($B,$O)}
function Read-U64 {param([byte[]]$B,[int]$O) if($O+8 -gt $B.Length){return [uint64]0};return [BitConverter]::ToUInt64($B,$O)}
function Read-AsciiZ {param([byte[]]$B,[int]$O,[int]$Max=512) $sb=New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder;for($i=$O;$i -lt $B.Length -and $i -lt ($O+$Max);$i++){if($B[$i] -eq 0){break};if($B[$i] -lt 32 -or $B[$i] -gt 126){break};[void]$sb.Append([char]$B[$i])};return $sb.ToString()}


function Get-CachedPeStaticAnalysis {
    param([string]$Path,[string]$SHA256,[string]$CacheDirectory)
    if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($CacheDirectory) -or [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($SHA256)){
        return [pscustomobject]@{Analysis=(Get-PeStaticAnalysis $Path);CacheHit=$false}
    }
    if(-not (Test-Path $CacheDirectory)){New-Item -ItemType Directory $CacheDirectory -Force|Out-Null}
    $cf=Join-Path $CacheDirectory ($SHA256.ToLowerInvariant()+'.json')
    if(Test-Path $cf -PathType Leaf){
        try{
            $cached=Get-Content $cf -Raw|ConvertFrom-Json -Depth 30
            return [pscustomobject]@{Analysis=$cached;CacheHit=$true}
        }catch{}
    }
    $analysis=Get-PeStaticAnalysis $Path
    try{$analysis|ConvertTo-Json -Depth 30|Set-Content $cf -Encoding UTF8}catch{}
    return [pscustomobject]@{Analysis=$analysis;CacheHit=$false}
}

function Get-PeStaticAnalysis {
    param([string]$Path)
    $imports=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string];$dlls=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string];$deviceStrings=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string];$ioctls=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
    $pdb='';$timestamp=$null;$machine='';$parseError='';$arch='Unknown'
    try{
        [byte[]]$b=[IO.File]::ReadAllBytes($Path);if($b.Length -lt 256 -or $b[0] -ne 0x4d -or $b[1] -ne 0x5a){throw 'Not a PE file'}
        $pe=[int](Read-U32 $b 0x3c);if($pe+256 -gt $b.Length -or $b[$pe] -ne 0x50 -or $b[$pe+1] -ne 0x45){throw 'Invalid PE signature'}
        $machineVal=Read-U16 $b ($pe+4);$machine=('0x{0:X4}' -f $machineVal);switch($machineVal){0x8664{$arch='x64'}0x014c{$arch='x86'}0xaa64{$arch='ARM64'}}
        $num=Read-U16 $b ($pe+6);$ts=Read-U32 $b ($pe+8);try{$timestamp=[DateTimeOffset]::FromUnixTimeSeconds([int64]$ts).UtcDateTime}catch{}
        $soh=Read-U16 $b ($pe+20);$opt=$pe+24;$magic=Read-U16 $b $opt;$is64=($magic -eq 0x20b);$dd=$opt+$(if($is64){112}else{96});$impRva=Read-U32 $b ($dd+8);$dbgRva=Read-U32 $b ($dd+48);$dbgSize=Read-U32 $b ($dd+52)
        $secOff=$opt+$soh;$secs=@()
        for($s=0;$s -lt $num;$s++){ $o=$secOff+($s*40);if($o+40 -gt $b.Length){break};$n=(Read-AsciiZ $b $o 8);$vs=Read-U32 $b ($o+8);$va=Read-U32 $b ($o+12);$raw=Read-U32 $b ($o+16);$ptr=Read-U32 $b ($o+20);$secs += [pscustomobject]@{Name=$n;VA=[uint32]$va;VS=[uint32]$vs;Raw=[uint32]$raw;Ptr=[uint32]$ptr} }
        function RvaToOff([uint32]$r){foreach($s in $secs){$size=[Math]::Max([uint32]$s.VS,[uint32]$s.Raw);if($r -ge $s.VA -and $r -lt ($s.VA+$size)){return [int]($s.Ptr+($r-$s.VA))}};return -1}
        if($impRva){$io=RvaToOff $impRva;if($io -ge 0){for($d=0;$d -lt 2048;$d++){ $o=$io+($d*20);if($o+20 -gt $b.Length){break};$oft=Read-U32 $b $o;$nameRva=Read-U32 $b ($o+12);$ft=Read-U32 $b ($o+16);if(($oft+$nameRva+$ft) -eq 0){break};$no=RvaToOff $nameRva;if($no -ge 0){$dn=Read-AsciiZ $b $no 260;if($dn){[void]$dlls.Add($dn)}};$tr=if($oft){$oft}else{$ft};$to=RvaToOff $tr;if($to -lt 0){continue};$step=if($is64){8}else{4};$ordMask=if($is64){[uint64]0x8000000000000000}else{[uint64]0x80000000};for($t=0;$t -lt 8192;$t++){ $q=$to+($t*$step);if($q+$step -gt $b.Length){break};$v=if($is64){Read-U64 $b $q}else{[uint64](Read-U32 $b $q)};if($v -eq 0){break};if(($v -band $ordMask) -ne 0){continue};$nr=[uint32]($v -band 0x7fffffff);if($is64){$nr=[uint32]($v -band 0x7fffffff)};$nn=RvaToOff $nr;if($nn -ge 0){$fn=Read-AsciiZ $b ($nn+2) 512;if($fn){[void]$imports.Add($fn)}} } }}}
        # PDB path from CodeView records or fallback raw RSDS search.
        $idx=-1;for($i=0;$i -lt $b.Length-4;$i++){if($b[$i] -eq 0x52 -and $b[$i+1] -eq 0x53 -and $b[$i+2] -eq 0x44 -and $b[$i+3] -eq 0x53){$idx=$i;break}}
        if($idx -ge 0 -and $idx+24 -lt $b.Length){$pdb=Read-AsciiZ $b ($idx+24) 1024}
        # Extract printable ASCII and UTF-16 strings, retaining only device/interface indicators.
        $ascii=[Text.Encoding]::ASCII.GetString($b)
        foreach($m in [regex]::Matches($ascii,'[ -~]{5,}')){ $v=$m.Value;if($v -match '(?i)\\Device\\|\\DosDevices\\|\\Global\?\?\\|\\\\\.\\|\\BaseNamedObjects\\'){if(-not ($deviceStrings.Contains($v))){[void]$deviceStrings.Add($v)}} }
        try{$uni=[Text.Encoding]::Unicode.GetString($b);foreach($m in [regex]::Matches($uni,'[ -~]{5,}')){$v=$m.Value;if($v -match '(?i)\\Device\\|\\DosDevices\\|\\Global\?\?\\|\\\\\.\\|\\BaseNamedObjects\\'){if(-not ($deviceStrings.Contains($v))){[void]$deviceStrings.Add($v)}}}}catch{}
        # IOCTL heuristic: immediate 32-bit values used by CMP instructions in executable sections.
        foreach($s in $secs|Where-Object{$_.Name -match '^\.text|CODE'}){ $start=[int]$s.Ptr;$end=[Math]::Min($b.Length,$start+[int]$s.Raw);for($i=$start;$i -lt ($end-6);$i++){ $val=$null;if($b[$i] -eq 0x3d){$val=Read-U32 $b ($i+1)}elseif($b[$i] -eq 0x81 -and (($b[$i+1] -band 0x38) -eq 0x38)){$val=Read-U32 $b ($i+2)};if($null -ne $val){$dev=($val -shr 16) -band 0xffff;$func=($val -shr 2) -band 0xfff;$method=$val -band 3;$access=($val -shr 14) -band 3;$plausible=(($dev -ge 1 -and $dev -le 0x5f) -or ($dev -ge 0x8000));if($plausible -and $func -ge 0x800){$hex=('0x{0:X8}' -f $val);if(-not (@($ioctls|Where-Object{$_.Hex -eq $hex}).Count)){[void]$ioctls.Add([pscustomobject]@{Hex=$hex;DeviceType=('0x{0:X4}' -f $dev);Function=('0x{0:X3}' -f $func);Method=$method;Access=$access;Confidence='Heuristic CMP immediate'})}} } } }
    }catch{$parseError=$_.Exception.Message}
    $danger=@{
      'Process termination'=@('ZwTerminateProcess','NtTerminateProcess');
      'Process manipulation'=@('PsLookupProcessByProcessId','ZwOpenProcess','ObOpenObjectByPointer','PsReferencePrimaryToken');
      'Kernel/physical memory'=@('MmMapIoSpace','MmMapIoSpaceEx','MmCopyMemory','MmMapLockedPagesSpecifyCache','MmGetPhysicalAddress','ZwMapViewOfSection');
      'User-buffer handling'=@('ProbeForRead','ProbeForWrite','MmProbeAndLockPages');
      'Device exposure'=@('IoCreateDevice','IoCreateDeviceSecure','IoCreateSymbolicLink','WdfDeviceCreateSymbolicLink');
      'Privilege/security'=@('SeSinglePrivilegeCheck','SePrivilegeCheck','ZwAdjustPrivilegesToken')
    }
    $caps=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string];$hits=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
    foreach($k in $danger.Keys){$found=@();foreach($n in $danger[$k]){if($imports -contains $n){$found+=$n}};if($found.Count){[void]$caps.Add($k);$hits.AddRange([string[]]$found)}}
    [pscustomobject]@{Architecture=$arch;Machine=$machine;PeTimestampUtc=$timestamp;PdbPath=$pdb;ImportedDlls=@($dlls|Sort-Object -Unique);Imports=@($imports|Sort-Object -Unique);DangerousImports=@($hits|Sort-Object -Unique);HeuristicCapabilities=@($caps|Sort-Object -Unique);DeviceStrings=@($deviceStrings|Select-Object -First 100);IoctlCandidates=@($ioctls|Select-Object -First 100);ParseError=$parseError}
}

function Get-FileMetadata {
    param([string]$Path)
    $h='';$he='';try{$h=(Get-FileHash $Path -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()}catch{$he=$_.Exception.Message}
    $sigStatus='';$signer='';$issuer='';$thumb='';$serial='';$nb=$null;$na=$null;$chain='';$whql=$false
    try{$sig=Get-AuthenticodeSignature $Path;$sigStatus=[string]$sig.Status;if($sig.SignerCertificate){$c=$sig.SignerCertificate;$signer=[string]$c.Subject;$issuer=[string]$c.Issuer;$thumb=[string]$c.Thumbprint;$serial=[string]$c.SerialNumber;$nb=$c.NotBefore;$na=$c.NotAfter;$whql=($signer -match 'Hardware Compatibility Publisher|Windows Hardware') -or ($issuer -match 'Microsoft');try{$ch=New-Object System.Security.Cryptography.X509Certificates.X509Chain;$ch.ChainPolicy.RevocationMode=if($EnableOnlineCertificateRevocation -and -not $NoNetwork){[System.Security.Cryptography.X509Certificates.X509RevocationMode]::Online}else{[System.Security.Cryptography.X509Certificates.X509RevocationMode]::NoCheck};$ok=$ch.Build($c);$chain=if($ok){'Valid'}else{(@($ch.ChainStatus|ForEach-Object{$_.Status}) -join ',')}}catch{}}}catch{}
    $co='';$fv='';$pv='';$pr='';$orig='';$desc='';$lw=$null;try{$it=Get-Item $Path;$v=$it.VersionInfo;$co=[string]$v.CompanyName;$fv=[string]$v.FileVersion;$pv=[string]$v.ProductVersion;$pr=[string]$v.ProductName;$orig=[string]$v.OriginalFilename;$desc=[string]$v.FileDescription;$lw=$it.LastWriteTimeUtc}catch{}
    [pscustomobject]@{SHA256=$h;HashError=$he;SignatureStatus=$sigStatus;Signer=$signer;CertificateIssuer=$issuer;CertificateThumbprint=$thumb;CertificateSerial=$serial;CertificateNotBefore=$nb;CertificateNotAfter=$na;CertificateChain=$chain;WHQLHint=$whql;Company=$co;FileVersion=$fv;ProductVersion=$pv;Product=$pr;OriginalFilename=$orig;Description=$desc;LastWriteTimeUtc=$lw}
}

function Get-EventDataMap {param($Event)$r=@{};try{[xml]$x=$Event.ToXml();foreach($d in @($x.Event.EventData.Data)){$n=[string]$d.Name;if($n){$r[$n]=[string]$d.'#text'}}}catch{};return $r}
function Get-SysmonDriverLoadEvents {param([datetime]$StartTime)$r=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object];try{foreach($e in @(Get-WinEvent -FilterHashtable @{LogName='Microsoft-Windows-Sysmon/Operational';Id=6;StartTime=$StartTime} -ErrorAction Stop)){$d=Get-EventDataMap $e;$hs=[string]$(if($d.ContainsKey('Hashes')){$d['Hashes']}else{''});$m=[regex]::Match($hs,'(?i)SHA256=([0-9A-F]{64})');$sha=if($m.Success){$m.Groups[1].Value.ToLowerInvariant()}else{''};[void]$r.Add([pscustomobject]@{TimeCreated=$e.TimeCreated;ImageLoaded=$(if($d.ContainsKey('ImageLoaded')){$d['ImageLoaded']}else{''});SHA256=$sha;Signed=$(if($d.ContainsKey('Signed')){$d['Signed']}else{''});Signature=$(if($d.ContainsKey('Signature')){$d['Signature']}else{''});SignatureStatus=$(if($d.ContainsKey('SignatureStatus')){$d['SignatureStatus']}else{''})})}}catch{};return $r.ToArray()}
function Get-CodeIntegrityEvents {param([datetime]$StartTime)$r=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object];try{foreach($e in @(Get-WinEvent -FilterHashtable @{LogName='Microsoft-Windows-CodeIntegrity/Operational';Id=@(3033,3076,3077,3089,3099);StartTime=$StartTime} -ErrorAction Stop)){$d=Get-EventDataMap $e;$p='';foreach($k in @('FileName','FilePath','File Name','File Path','ImageName','Image')){if($d.ContainsKey($k) -and $d[$k]){$p=[string]$d[$k];break}};[void]$r.Add([pscustomobject]@{TimeCreated=$e.TimeCreated;EventId=$e.Id;FilePath=$p;Message=[string]$e.Message})}}catch{};return $r.ToArray()}
function Get-DriverInstallEvents {param([datetime]$StartTime)$r=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object];try{foreach($e in @(Get-WinEvent -FilterHashtable @{LogName='System';ProviderName='Service Control Manager';Id=7045;StartTime=$StartTime} -ErrorAction Stop)){if([string]$e.Message -match '(?i)\.sys'){[void]$r.Add([pscustomobject]@{TimeCreated=$e.TimeCreated;EventId=$e.Id;Message=[string]$e.Message})}}}catch{};return $r.ToArray()}

function Get-DosDeviceNames {
    $code=@'
using System; using System.Text; using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public static class DRSQueryDosDevice { [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError=true)] public static extern uint QueryDosDevice(string lpDeviceName, StringBuilder lpTargetPath, int ucchMax); }
'@
    try{if(-not ('DRSQueryDosDevice' -as [type])){Add-Type $code -ErrorAction Stop};$sb=New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder 1048576;$n=[DRSQueryDosDevice]::QueryDosDevice($null,$sb,$sb.Capacity);if($n -eq 0){return @()};return @($sb.ToString().Split([char]0)|Where-Object{$_})}catch{return @()}
}

function Get-DeviceAclProbe {
    param([string]$DosName)
    $code=@'
using System; using System.Runtime.InteropServices; using Microsoft.Win32.SafeHandles;
public class DRSDeviceAclResult { public bool Opened; public int Win32Error; public string Sddl; }
public static class DRSDeviceAcl {
 const uint READ_CONTROL=0x00020000, FILE_SHARE_READ=1, FILE_SHARE_WRITE=2, FILE_SHARE_DELETE=4, OPEN_EXISTING=3, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL=0x80, DACL_SECURITY_INFORMATION=4;
 enum SE_OBJECT_TYPE { SE_UNKNOWN_OBJECT_TYPE=0, SE_FILE_OBJECT=1, SE_SERVICE=2, SE_PRINTER=3, SE_REGISTRY_KEY=4, SE_LMSHARE=5, SE_KERNEL_OBJECT=6 }
 [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError=true)] static extern SafeFileHandle CreateFileW(string name,uint access,uint share,IntPtr sa,uint creation,uint flags,IntPtr template);
 [DllImport("advapi32.dll", SetLastError=true)] static extern uint GetSecurityInfo(IntPtr handle,SE_OBJECT_TYPE type,uint si,out IntPtr owner,out IntPtr group,out IntPtr dacl,out IntPtr sacl,out IntPtr sd);
 [DllImport("advapi32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError=true)] static extern bool ConvertSecurityDescriptorToStringSecurityDescriptorW(IntPtr sd,uint rev,uint info,out IntPtr str,out uint len);
 [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern IntPtr LocalFree(IntPtr p);
 public static DRSDeviceAclResult Probe(string path){var r=new DRSDeviceAclResult(); using(var h=CreateFileW(path,READ_CONTROL,FILE_SHARE_READ|FILE_SHARE_WRITE|FILE_SHARE_DELETE,IntPtr.Zero,OPEN_EXISTING,FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL,IntPtr.Zero)){ if(h.IsInvalid){r.Win32Error=Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();return r;} r.Opened=true;IntPtr o,g,d,s,sd;uint e=GetSecurityInfo(h.DangerousGetHandle(),SE_OBJECT_TYPE.SE_KERNEL_OBJECT,DACL_SECURITY_INFORMATION,out o,out g,out d,out s,out sd);if(e!=0){r.Win32Error=(int)e;return r;}IntPtr p;uint l;if(ConvertSecurityDescriptorToStringSecurityDescriptorW(sd,1,DACL_SECURITY_INFORMATION,out p,out l)){r.Sddl=Marshal.PtrToStringUni(p);LocalFree(p);} if(sd!=IntPtr.Zero)LocalFree(sd); } return r; }
}
'@
    try{if(-not ('DRSDeviceAcl' -as [type])){Add-Type $code -ErrorAction Stop};$r=[DRSDeviceAcl]::Probe("\\.\$DosName");$broad=$false;if($r.Sddl -match '\(A;[^\)]*;;;(WD|AU|BU|AN)\)'){$broad=$true};return [pscustomobject]@{Device="\\.\$DosName";Opened=$r.Opened;Win32Error=$r.Win32Error;SDDL=$r.Sddl;BroadPrincipalAllow=$broad}}catch{return [pscustomobject]@{Device="\\.\$DosName";Opened=$false;Win32Error=-1;SDDL='';BroadPrincipalAllow=$false}}
}

function Get-DeviceNamesFromStrings {
    param([array]$Strings)
    $r=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
    foreach($s in $Strings){$v=[string]$s;foreach($pat in @('(?i)\\DosDevices\\([A-Za-z0-9_.{}#$-]{1,128})','(?i)\\\?\?\\([A-Za-z0-9_.{}#$-]{1,128})','(?i)\\\\\.\\([A-Za-z0-9_.{}#$-]{1,128})')){foreach($m in [regex]::Matches($v,$pat)){if($m.Groups[1].Value -and -not ($r.Contains($m.Groups[1].Value))){[void]$r.Add($m.Groups[1].Value)}}}}
    return $r.ToArray()
}

function Get-NvdEnrichment {
    param([string]$Cve,[string]$CacheDir)
    $cf=Join-Path $CacheDir ($Cve+'.json')
    try{if(Test-Path $cf){$j=ConvertFrom-JsonCompat -Json (Get-Content $cf -Raw)}else{if($NoNetwork){return $null};$u=$NvdApi+'?cveId='+[uri]::EscapeDataString($Cve);$j=Invoke-RestMethod $u -UseBasicParsing;($j|ConvertTo-Json -Depth 20)|Set-Content $cf -Encoding UTF8;Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 650};$c=$j.vulnerabilities[0].cve;$desc='';foreach($d in @($c.descriptions)){if($d.lang -eq 'en'){$desc=$d.value;break}};$sev='';$score='';foreach($p in @('cvssMetricV40','cvssMetricV31','cvssMetricV30','cvssMetricV2')){$arr=Get-Prop $c.metrics $p @();if(@($arr).Count){$data=Get-Prop $arr[0] 'cvssData' $null;if($data){$sev=[string](Get-Prop $data 'baseSeverity' '');$score=[string](Get-Prop $data 'baseScore' '')};break}};return [pscustomobject]@{CVE=$Cve;Severity=$sev;Score=$score;Description=$desc;Published=[string]$c.published;LastModified=[string]$c.lastModified}}catch{return $null}
}

function Invoke-YaraOnFile {
    param([string]$Path,[string]$Exe,[string]$Rules)
    try{$o=& $Exe $Rules $Path 2>$null;return @(($o|ForEach-Object{([string]$_ -split '\s+')[0]})|Where-Object{$_}|Sort-Object -Unique)}catch{return @()}
}

function Add-CandidatePath {
    param([hashtable]$Candidates,[string]$Path,[string]$Source)
    if(-not $Path -or -not (Test-Path $Path -PathType Leaf)){return};$k=Normalize-PathKey $Path;if(-not $k){return};if(-not $Candidates.ContainsKey($k)){$Candidates[$k]=[pscustomobject]@{Path=(Resolve-Path $Path).Path;Sources=New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList}};if(-not ($Candidates[$k].Sources -contains $Source)){[void]$Candidates[$k].Sources.Add($Source)}
}

function Test-VersionRule {
    param([string]$Version,$Rule)
    if(-not $Version){return $false};try{$v=[version](([regex]::Match($Version,'\d+(\.\d+){1,3}')).Value);if(-not $v){return $false};$min=$null;$max=$null;if($Rule.MinimumFileVersion){$min=[version]$Rule.MinimumFileVersion};if($Rule.MaximumFileVersion){$max=[version]$Rule.MaximumFileVersion};if($min -and $v -lt $min){return $false};if($max -and $v -gt $max){return $false};return $true}catch{return $false}
}

function Get-Score {
    param($R)
    $s=0;if($R.LolExact){$s+=55};if($R.MicrosoftExact){$s+=50};if($R.ThreatProfileExact){$s+=45};if($R.ThreatProfileHeuristic){$s+=12};if($R.Running){$s+=15}elseif($R.RecentLoad){$s+=8};if($R.BroadDeviceAcl){$s+=12};if($R.DevicePresent){$s+=5};if($R.DangerousImportCount -ge 3){$s+=8}elseif($R.DangerousImportCount){$s+=4};if($R.IoctlCandidateCount){$s+=4};if($R.SignatureStatus -and $R.SignatureStatus -ne 'Valid'){$s+=6};if($R.DriverAgeYears -ge 5){$s+=3};if(-not $R.HVCI){$s+=3};if(-not $R.MSBlockEnabled){$s+=2};if($R.RecentInstall){$s+=3};if($s -gt 100){$s=100};return $s
}
function Get-Priority {param([int]$S)if($S -ge 90){'Critical'}elseif($S -ge 75){'High'}elseif($S -ge 50){'Medium'}elseif($S -ge 25){'Low'}else{'Info'}}

function Compare-BaselineData {
    param([array]$Current,[string]$Path)
    $r=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object];if(-not $Path -or -not (Test-Path $Path)){return @()};try{$old=@((ConvertFrom-JsonCompat -Json (Get-Content $Path -Raw)).Files)}catch{return @()};$om=@{};$cm=@{};foreach($x in $old){$om[[string]$x.PathKey]=$x};foreach($x in $Current){$cm[[string]$x.PathKey]=$x};foreach($k in $cm.Keys){if(-not $om.ContainsKey($k)){[void]$r.Add([pscustomobject]@{Change='New';Path=$cm[$k].Path;OldSHA256='';NewSHA256=$cm[$k].SHA256})}elseif([string]$om[$k].SHA256 -ne [string]$cm[$k].SHA256){[void]$r.Add([pscustomobject]@{Change='Changed';Path=$cm[$k].Path;OldSHA256=$om[$k].SHA256;NewSHA256=$cm[$k].SHA256})}};foreach($k in $om.Keys){if(-not $cm.ContainsKey($k)){[void]$r.Add([pscustomobject]@{Change='Removed';Path=$om[$k].Path;OldSHA256=$om[$k].SHA256;NewSHA256=''})}};return $r.ToArray()
}

function New-HtmlReport {
    param($HostInfo,$Protection,[array]$Inventory,[array]$Findings,[array]$Stale,[array]$Diff,[string]$Path)
    $crit=@($Findings|Where-Object Priority -eq 'Critical').Count;$hi=@($Findings|Where-Object Priority -eq 'High').Count;$med=@($Findings|Where-Object Priority -eq 'Medium').Count;$sus=@($Findings|Where-Object Classification -eq 'Suspicious').Count
    $rows=New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder;foreach($f in @($Findings|Sort-Object RemediationScore -Descending|Select-Object -First 250)){[void]$rows.AppendLine("<tr><td>$(Html $f.Priority)</td><td>$($f.RemediationScore)</td><td>$(Html $f.Classification)</td><td>$(Html $f.FileName)</td><td>$(Html $f.State)</td><td>$(Html $f.Company)</td><td>$(Html $f.ThreatProfileProjects)</td><td>$(Html $f.PrimitiveClass)</td><td>$(Html $f.CVEs)</td><td><code>$(Html $f.SHA256)</code></td></tr>")}
    $html=@"
<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>DriverRiskScout</title><style>body{font-family:Segoe UI,Arial,sans-serif;margin:28px;background:#f5f7fa;color:#1f2937}.cards{display:flex;gap:14px;flex-wrap:wrap}.card{background:#fff;padding:16px 20px;border-radius:10px;box-shadow:0 1px 5px #0002;min-width:150px}.n{font-size:30px;font-weight:700}table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;background:#fff;margin-top:18px}th,td{padding:9px;border-bottom:1px solid #e5e7eb;text-align:left;font-size:13px}th{position:sticky;top:0;background:#111827;color:#fff}code{font-size:11px}.muted{color:#6b7280}.good{color:#087f5b}.bad{color:#b42318}</style></head><body>
<h1>DriverRiskScout 1.1.5</h1><p class="muted">Read-only Windows kernel-driver exposure assessment</p>
<h2>Host</h2><p><b>$(Html $HostInfo.ComputerName)</b> — $(Html $HostInfo.OSCaption) $(Html $HostInfo.OSVersion), build $(Html $HostInfo.BuildNumber)</p>
<div class="cards"><div class="card"><div class="n">$crit</div>Critical</div><div class="card"><div class="n">$hi</div>High</div><div class="card"><div class="n">$med</div>Medium</div><div class="card"><div class="n">$sus</div>Suspicious</div><div class="card"><div class="n">$($Inventory.Count)</div>Files</div></div>
<h2>Protection</h2><p>HVCI: <b>$(Html $Protection.HVCIRegistryEnabled)</b> &nbsp; VBS: <b>$(Html $Protection.VBSRuntimeStatus)</b> &nbsp; Vulnerable-driver blocklist: <b>$(Html $Protection.VulnerableDriverBlocklistEnabled)</b> &nbsp; Secure Boot: <b>$(Html $Protection.SecureBoot)</b> &nbsp; WDAC policies: <b>$($Protection.ActiveWdacPolicyCount)</b></p>
<h2>Prioritized findings</h2><table><thead><tr><th>Priority</th><th>Score</th><th>Class</th><th>Driver</th><th>State</th><th>Vendor</th><th>Threat profile</th><th>Capability</th><th>CVE</th><th>SHA256</th></tr></thead><tbody>$rows</tbody></table>
<p class="muted">The score is a remediation/exposure priority, not proof of exploitability. Static IOCTL/import matches are heuristics.</p>
<h3>DriverStore stale candidates</h3><p>$($Stale.Count) older duplicate/version candidates.</p><h3>Baseline changes</h3><p>$($Diff.Count) changes compared with the supplied baseline.</p>
</body></html>
"@
    Set-Content $Path $html -Encoding UTF8
}

Write-Section "$ToolName $ToolVersion"
Write-Host 'Read-only assessment. No IOCTLs, exploitation, process termination, driver loading or security-control changes are performed.'
Assert-PowerShell7
Write-Host ("PowerShell runtime: {0}" -f $PSVersionTable.PSVersion) -ForegroundColor DarkGray

if($EnableDeviceAclProbe){Write-Warning 'Device ACL probe is enabled. It opens READ_CONTROL handles to statically discovered DOS device names; this can invoke IRP_MJ_CREATE. No IOCTL is sent.'}

New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $OutputDirectory -Force|Out-Null;$cache=Join-Path $OutputDirectory '_cache';New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $cache -Force|Out-Null;$nvdCache=Join-Path $cache 'nvd';New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $nvdCache -Force|Out-Null
$os=Get-CimInstance Win32_OperatingSystem;$protection=Get-ProtectionState;$hotfixes=@();try{$hotfixes=@(Get-HotFix -ErrorAction Stop|Sort-Object InstalledOn -Descending|Select-Object -First 100 HotFixID,Description,InstalledBy,InstalledOn)}catch{};$hostInfo=[pscustomobject]@{ComputerName=$env:COMPUTERNAME;OSCaption=[string]$os.Caption;OSVersion=[string]$os.Version;BuildNumber=[string]$os.BuildNumber;ScanTime=(Get-Date).ToString('o');LatestHotFix=$(if((Get-CountSafe $hotfixes) -gt 0){[string]$hotfixes[0].HotFixID}else{''});LatestHotFixDate=$(if((Get-CountSafe $hotfixes) -gt 0){[string]$hotfixes[0].InstalledOn}else{''})}
Write-Section 'Threat intelligence'
$lol=@()
try{
    $lol=Get-LolDriversData $LolDriversJson -Offline:$NoNetwork
    Write-Host ("LOLDrivers entries loaded: {0}" -f (Get-CountSafe $lol))
}catch{
    Write-Warning ("LOLDrivers load failed: {0}" -f $_.Exception.Message)
    $lol=@()
}
$lolIndex=New-LolDriversIndex -Database $lol

try{
    $ms=Get-MicrosoftBlocklistIndex $MicrosoftBlocklistXml -Offline:$NoNetwork -WorkingDirectory $cache
    Write-Host ("Microsoft blocklist source: {0}; exact hashes: {1}; file rules: {2}" -f $ms.Source,$ms.ExactSha256Rules,$ms.FileAttributeRules)
}catch{
    Write-Warning ("Microsoft blocklist load failed: {0}" -f $_.Exception.Message)
    $ms=[pscustomobject]@{BySha256=@{};ByFilename=@{};Rules=@();XmlFiles=@();Source='Unavailable';ExactSha256Rules=0;FileAttributeRules=0}
}

$threatProfiles=Get-ThreatProfiles $ThreatProfileJson
$threatIndex=New-ThreatProfileIndex -Profiles $threatProfiles
Write-Host ("Bundled/custom BYOVD detection profiles: {0}" -f (Get-CountSafe $threatProfiles))

$services=@(Get-CimInstance Win32_SystemDriver);$serviceByPath=@{};foreach($s in $services){$p=Resolve-DriverImagePath ([string]$s.PathName);if($p){$k=Normalize-PathKey $p;if(-not $serviceByPath.ContainsKey($k)){$serviceByPath[$k]=New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList};[void]$serviceByPath[$k].Add($s)}}
$candidates=@{};foreach($s in $services){if($IncludeStopped -or [string]$s.State -eq 'Running'){Add-CandidatePath $candidates (Resolve-DriverImagePath ([string]$s.PathName)) $(if([string]$s.State -eq 'Running'){'RunningService'}else{'StoppedService'})}}
if(-not $SkipSystemDriversDirectory){foreach($f in @(Get-ChildItem "$env:SystemRoot\System32\drivers" -Filter '*.sys' -File -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)){Add-CandidatePath $candidates $f.FullName 'System32DriversDirectory'}}
if(-not $SkipDriverStore){$ds="$env:SystemRoot\System32\DriverStore\FileRepository";if(Test-Path $ds){foreach($f in @(Get-ChildItem $ds -Filter '*.sys' -File -Recurse -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)){Add-CandidatePath $candidates $f.FullName 'DriverStore'}}}

$start=(Get-Date).AddDays(-$LookbackDays);$sysmon=@();$ci=@();$inst=@();if(-not $SkipEventLogs){$sysmon=Get-SysmonDriverLoadEvents $start;$ci=Get-CodeIntegrityEvents $start;$inst=Get-DriverInstallEvents $start}
$dosDevices=Get-DosDeviceNames
$yaraRules=Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'rules\DriverRiskScout.yar';if($EnableYara -and -not $YaraExecutable){foreach($n in @('yara64.exe','yara.exe')){$c=Get-Command $n -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue;if($c){$YaraExecutable=$c.Source;break}}}


# Optional bounded PowerShell 7 parallel pre-analysis. Only file metadata/signature/PE
# analysis is parallelized; correlation and scoring remain deterministic/serial.
$preAnalysis=@{}
if($MaxParallelism -gt 1 -and $candidates.Count -gt 1){
    try{
        $defReadU16=${function:Read-U16}.ToString()
        $defReadU32=${function:Read-U32}.ToString()
        $defReadU64=${function:Read-U64}.ToString()
        $defReadAsciiZ=${function:Read-AsciiZ}.ToString()
        $defPe=${function:Get-PeStaticAnalysis}.ToString()
        $defMeta=${function:Get-FileMetadata}.ToString()
        $defCache=${function:Get-CachedPeStaticAnalysis}.ToString()
        $paths=@($candidates.Values|ForEach-Object{$_.Path})
        Write-Host ("Parallel file analysis: {0} worker(s)" -f $MaxParallelism) -ForegroundColor DarkGray
        $parallelResults=@($paths|ForEach-Object -Parallel {
            Invoke-Expression ("function Read-U16 { " + $using:defReadU16 + " }")
            Invoke-Expression ("function Read-U32 { " + $using:defReadU32 + " }")
            Invoke-Expression ("function Read-U64 { " + $using:defReadU64 + " }")
            Invoke-Expression ("function Read-AsciiZ { " + $using:defReadAsciiZ + " }")
            Invoke-Expression ("function Get-PeStaticAnalysis { " + $using:defPe + " }")
            Invoke-Expression ("function Get-FileMetadata { " + $using:defMeta + " }")
            Invoke-Expression ("function Get-CachedPeStaticAnalysis { " + $using:defCache + " }")
            $script:EnableOnlineCertificateRevocation=[bool]$using:EnableOnlineCertificateRevocation
            $script:NoNetwork=[bool]$using:NoNetwork
            $p=[string]$_
            $m=Get-FileMetadata $p
            $cp=Get-CachedPeStaticAnalysis $p $m.SHA256 ([string]$using:PersistentAnalysisCacheDirectory)
            [pscustomobject]@{Path=$p;Meta=$m;Pe=$cp.Analysis;CacheHit=$cp.CacheHit}
        } -ThrottleLimit $MaxParallelism)
        foreach($r in $parallelResults){
            try{$preAnalysis[(Normalize-PathKey ([string]$r.Path))]=$r}catch{}
        }
    }catch{
        Write-Warning ("Parallel pre-analysis failed; falling back to serial analysis: {0}" -f $_.Exception.Message)
        $preAnalysis=@{}
    }
}

$inventory=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object];$findings=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object];$deviceFindings=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object];$ioctlFindings=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object];$profileFindings=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object];$probeCount=0;$i=0
foreach($c in $candidates.Values){
    $i++;Write-Progress -Activity 'DriverRiskScout static analysis' -Status "$i / $($candidates.Count) $($c.Path)" -PercentComplete (($i/[Math]::Max(1,$candidates.Count))*100)
    $key=Normalize-PathKey $c.Path;$cachedPe=$null
    if($preAnalysis.ContainsKey($key)){$pa=$preAnalysis[$key];$meta=$pa.Meta;$pe=$pa.Pe;$cachedPe=[pscustomobject]@{CacheHit=$pa.CacheHit}}
    else{$meta=Get-FileMetadata $c.Path;$cachedPe=Get-CachedPeStaticAnalysis $c.Path $meta.SHA256 $PersistentAnalysisCacheDirectory;$pe=$cachedPe.Analysis};$svcs=@();if($serviceByPath.ContainsKey($key)){$svcs=@($serviceByPath[$key])};$running=(@($svcs|Where-Object State -eq 'Running').Count -gt 0);$state=if($running){'Running'}elseif((Get-CountSafe $svcs) -gt 0){'Registered/Stopped'}elseif($c.Sources -contains 'DriverStore'){'DriverStore only'}else{'File only'}
    $recent=@($sysmon|Where-Object{($_.SHA256 -and $meta.SHA256 -and $_.SHA256 -eq $meta.SHA256) -or ($_.ImageLoaded -and (Normalize-PathKey $_.ImageLoaded) -eq $key)});$recentInstall=$false;foreach($e in $inst){if([string]$e.Message -match [regex]::Escape([IO.Path]::GetFileName($c.Path))){$recentInstall=$true;break}}
    $lolMatches=@();if($meta.SHA256 -and $lolIndex.BySha256.ContainsKey($meta.SHA256)){$lolMatches=@($lolIndex.BySha256[$meta.SHA256])};$lolExact=((Get-CountSafe $lolMatches) -gt 0);$msExact=($meta.SHA256 -and $ms.BySha256.ContainsKey($meta.SHA256));$fname=[IO.Path]::GetFileName($c.Path);$msHints=@();if($ms.ByFilename.ContainsKey($fname.ToLowerInvariant())){$msHints=@($ms.ByFilename[$fname.ToLowerInvariant()]|Where-Object{Test-VersionRule $meta.FileVersion $_})}
    $devNames=Get-DeviceNamesFromStrings $pe.DeviceStrings;$present=@($devNames|Where-Object{$dosDevices -contains $_});$broad=$false;$aclSummary=@();foreach($dn in $devNames){$isPresent=($present -contains $dn);$pr=$null;if($EnableDeviceAclProbe -and $isPresent -and $probeCount -lt $MaxDeviceAclProbes){$probeCount++;$pr=Get-DeviceAclProbe $dn;if($pr.BroadPrincipalAllow){$broad=$true};$aclSummary+="$($pr.Device): broad=$($pr.BroadPrincipalAllow) error=$($pr.Win32Error)"};[void]$deviceFindings.Add([pscustomobject]@{Driver=$c.Path;DeviceName=$dn;DosPath="\\.\$dn";Present=$isPresent;AclProbeEnabled=$EnableDeviceAclProbe;Opened=$(if($pr){$pr.Opened}else{$null});Win32Error=$(if($pr){$pr.Win32Error}else{$null});BroadPrincipalAllow=$(if($pr){$pr.BroadPrincipalAllow}else{$false});SDDL=$(if($pr){$pr.SDDL}else{''})})};foreach($ic in $pe.IoctlCandidates){[void]$ioctlFindings.Add([pscustomobject]@{Driver=$c.Path;FileName=$fname;Hex=$ic.Hex;DeviceType=$ic.DeviceType;Function=$ic.Function;Method=$ic.Method;Access=$ic.Access;Confidence=$ic.Confidence;KnownVulnerableHash=$lolExact;MicrosoftBlocklistHash=$msExact;Running=$running})}
    $profileMatches=Get-ThreatProfileMatches $threatIndex $meta.SHA256 $fname $meta.OriginalFilename $devNames $pe.IoctlCandidates $pe.Imports
    $profileExact=(@($profileMatches|Where-Object Exact).Count -gt 0);$profileHeuristic=(@($profileMatches|Where-Object{-not $_.Exact}).Count -gt 0);$profilePrimary=@($profileMatches|Sort-Object @{Expression='Exact';Descending=$true}|Select-Object -First 1)
    foreach($pm in $profileMatches){$pp=$pm.Profile;[void]$profileFindings.Add([pscustomobject]@{Project=[string](Get-Prop $pp 'Project' '');ProfileId=[string](Get-Prop $pp 'ProfileId' '');Confidence=$pm.Confidence;Evidence=$pm.Evidence;FileName=$fname;Path=$c.Path;SHA256=$meta.SHA256;State=$state;Running=$running;Devices=(@((Get-Prop $pp 'Devices' @())) -join ',');Ioctls=(@((Get-Prop $pp 'Ioctls' @())) -join ',');Capability=[string](Get-Prop $pp 'Capability' '');RiskContext=[string](Get-Prop $pp 'RiskContext' '');Source=[string](Get-Prop $pp 'Source' '')})}
    $yara=@();if($EnableYara -and $YaraExecutable -and (Test-Path $yaraRules)){$yara=Invoke-YaraOnFile $c.Path $YaraExecutable $yaraRules}
    $age=0;if($pe.PeTimestampUtc){$age=[Math]::Round(((Get-Date).ToUniversalTime()-[datetime]$pe.PeTimestampUtc).TotalDays/365.25,1)}
    $inv=[pscustomobject]@{Path=$c.Path;PathKey=$key;FileName=$fname;Sources=($c.Sources -join ',');State=$state;DriverServices=(@($svcs|ForEach-Object{$_.Name}) -join ',');Running=$running;SHA256=$meta.SHA256;SignatureStatus=$meta.SignatureStatus;Signer=$meta.Signer;CertificateIssuer=$meta.CertificateIssuer;CertificateThumbprint=$meta.CertificateThumbprint;CertificateNotBefore=$meta.CertificateNotBefore;CertificateNotAfter=$meta.CertificateNotAfter;CertificateChain=$meta.CertificateChain;WHQLHint=$meta.WHQLHint;Company=$meta.Company;FileVersion=$meta.FileVersion;ProductVersion=$meta.ProductVersion;Product=$meta.Product;OriginalFilename=$meta.OriginalFilename;Description=$meta.Description;Architecture=$pe.Architecture;PeTimestampUtc=$pe.PeTimestampUtc;DriverAgeYears=$age;PdbPath=$pe.PdbPath;ImportedDlls=($pe.ImportedDlls -join ',');DangerousImports=($pe.DangerousImports -join ',');HeuristicCapabilities=($pe.HeuristicCapabilities -join ',');DangerousImportCount=$pe.DangerousImports.Count;DeviceStrings=($pe.DeviceStrings -join ' | ');DeviceNames=($devNames -join ',');DevicePresent=($present.Count -gt 0);PresentDevices=($present -join ',');BroadDeviceAcl=$broad;DeviceAclSummary=($aclSummary -join ' | ');IoctlCandidates=(@($pe.IoctlCandidates|ForEach-Object{$_.Hex}) -join ',');IoctlCandidateCount=$pe.IoctlCandidates.Count;YaraRules=($yara -join ',');RecentSysmonLoad=((Get-CountSafe $recent) -gt 0);RecentLoadCount=(Get-CountSafe $recent);RecentInstall=$recentInstall;LolDriversExact=$lolExact;MicrosoftBlocklistExact=$msExact;MicrosoftVersionHint=((Get-CountSafe $msHints) -gt 0);ThreatProfileExact=$profileExact;ThreatProfileHeuristic=$profileHeuristic;ThreatProfiles=(@($profileMatches|ForEach-Object{[string](Get-Prop $_.Profile 'Project' '')})|Sort-Object -Unique) -join ',';ThreatProfileEvidence=(@($profileMatches|ForEach-Object{"$([string](Get-Prop $_.Profile 'ProfileId' '')):$($_.Confidence):$($_.Evidence)"}) -join ' | ');StaticParseError=$pe.ParseError;PeAnalysisCacheHit=$cachedPe.CacheHit}
    [void]$inventory.Add($inv)
    $class='Unknown';if($lolExact -or $msExact -or $profileExact){$class='Known'}elseif($profileHeuristic -or $pe.DangerousImports.Count -or $pe.IoctlCandidates.Count -or $present.Count -or (Get-CountSafe $yara)){$class='Suspicious'}
    $entryList=if($lolExact){$lolMatches}else{@([pscustomobject]@{Entry=$null;Sample=$null})}
    foreach($lm in $entryList){
        $entry=$lm.Entry
        $cves=if($entry){Get-CvesFromEntry $entry}else{@()}
        $use=if($entry){[string](Get-Prop $entry 'Usecase' '')}elseif((Get-CountSafe $profilePrimary)){'Curated BYOVD research profile match'}else{''}
        $desc=if($entry){[string](Get-Prop $entry 'Description' '')}elseif((Get-CountSafe $profilePrimary)){[string](Get-Prop $profilePrimary[0].Profile 'RiskContext' '')}else{''}
        $resources=if($entry){Get-EntryResources $entry}elseif((Get-CountSafe $profilePrimary)){@([string](Get-Prop $profilePrimary[0].Profile 'Source' ''))}else{@()}
        $primitive=if($entry){Get-PrimitiveClassFromText ($use+' '+$desc)}elseif((Get-CountSafe $profilePrimary)){[string](Get-Prop $profilePrimary[0].Profile 'Capability' 'Unknown')}elseif($pe.HeuristicCapabilities.Count){$pe.HeuristicCapabilities -join '; '}else{'Unknown'}
        $r=[pscustomobject]@{Classification=$class;LolExact=$lolExact;MicrosoftExact=$msExact;ThreatProfileExact=$profileExact;ThreatProfileHeuristic=$profileHeuristic;Running=$running;RecentLoad=((Get-CountSafe $recent) -gt 0);RecentInstall=$recentInstall;BroadDeviceAcl=$broad;DevicePresent=($present.Count -gt 0);DangerousImportCount=$pe.DangerousImports.Count;IoctlCandidateCount=$pe.IoctlCandidates.Count;SignatureStatus=$meta.SignatureStatus;DriverAgeYears=$age;HVCI=$protection.HVCIRegistryEnabled;MSBlockEnabled=$protection.VulnerableDriverBlocklistEnabled}
        $score=Get-Score $r;$priority=Get-Priority $score
        $nvd=@();if($EnableNvdLookup){foreach($cv in $cves){$en=Get-NvdEnrichment $cv $nvdCache;if($en){$nvd+=$en}}}
        $profileProjects=(@($profileMatches|ForEach-Object{[string](Get-Prop $_.Profile 'Project' '')})|Sort-Object -Unique) -join ','
        $profileIds=(@($profileMatches|ForEach-Object{[string](Get-Prop $_.Profile 'ProfileId' '')})|Sort-Object -Unique) -join ','
        $profileConfidence=(@($profileMatches|ForEach-Object{$_.Confidence})|Sort-Object -Unique) -join ','
        $finding=[pscustomobject]@{Priority=$priority;RemediationScore=$score;Classification=$class;FileName=$fname;Path=$c.Path;State=$state;DriverServices=(@($svcs|ForEach-Object{$_.Name}) -join ',');Running=$running;RecentLoad=((Get-CountSafe $recent) -gt 0);RecentInstall=$recentInstall;SHA256=$meta.SHA256;Company=$meta.Company;FileVersion=$meta.FileVersion;Signer=$meta.Signer;SignatureStatus=$meta.SignatureStatus;WHQLHint=$meta.WHQLHint;DriverAgeYears=$age;LOLDriversEntryId=$(if($entry){[string](Get-Prop $entry 'Id' '')}else{''});Category=$(if($entry){[string](Get-Prop $entry 'Category' '')}else{''});Verified=$(if($entry){[string](Get-Prop $entry 'Verified' '')}else{''});Usecase=$use;PrimitiveClass=$primitive;CVEs=($cves -join ',');AdvisoryResources=($resources -join ' | ');ThreatProfileProjects=$profileProjects;ThreatProfileIds=$profileIds;ThreatProfileConfidence=$profileConfidence;NvdSeverity=(@($nvd|ForEach-Object{"$($_.CVE):$($_.Severity)/$($_.Score)"}) -join ',');NvdDescriptions=(@($nvd|ForEach-Object{"$($_.CVE): $($_.Description)"}) -join ' | ');MicrosoftBlocklistExact=$msExact;MicrosoftVersionHint=((Get-CountSafe $msHints) -gt 0);DangerousImports=($pe.DangerousImports -join ',');HeuristicCapabilities=($pe.HeuristicCapabilities -join ',');IoctlCandidates=(@($pe.IoctlCandidates|ForEach-Object{$_.Hex}) -join ',');DeviceNames=($devNames -join ',');PresentDevices=($present -join ',');BroadDeviceAcl=$broad;YaraRules=($yara -join ',');PatchAssessment=$(if($lolExact){'Exact known-vulnerable sample hash present'}elseif($msExact){'Exact Microsoft blocklist hash present'}elseif($profileExact){'Exact curated BYOVD research-profile hash present'}elseif($profileHeuristic){'Heuristic BYOVD research-profile similarity; validate manually'}elseif((Get-CountSafe $msHints)){'Microsoft filename/version rule hint; validate manually'}else{'No exact public-hash confirmation'});RecommendedAction=$(if($class -eq 'Known'){'Treat as high-priority driver exposure: validate business need and vendor-fixed version; update/remove; verify WDAC/HVCI/blocklist enforcement; investigate unexpected loading.'}elseif($class -eq 'Suspicious'){'Manual driver review: validate device ACL, IOCTL dispatch, imported APIs, vendor provenance and update status. Threat-profile heuristic matches are not proof of exploitability.'}else{'Inventory/baseline only.'})}
        if($class -ne 'Unknown' -or $score -ge 25){[void]$findings.Add($finding)}
    }
}
Write-Progress -Activity 'DriverRiskScout static analysis' -Completed

# DriverStore stale/version candidates.
$stale=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object];$dsItems=@($inventory|Where-Object{$_.Sources -match 'DriverStore'});foreach($g in @($dsItems|Group-Object {([string]$(if($_.OriginalFilename){$_.OriginalFilename}else{$_.FileName})+'|'+[string]$_.Company).ToLowerInvariant()})){if($g.Count -lt 2){continue};$parsed=@();foreach($x in $g.Group){try{$vv=[version](([regex]::Match([string]$x.FileVersion,'\d+(\.\d+){1,3}')).Value);$parsed+=[pscustomobject]@{Item=$x;V=$vv}}catch{}};if($parsed.Count -lt 2){continue};$max=($parsed|Sort-Object V -Descending|Select-Object -First 1).V;foreach($p in $parsed){if($p.V -lt $max){[void]$stale.Add([pscustomobject]@{FileName=$p.Item.FileName;Path=$p.Item.Path;FileVersion=$p.Item.FileVersion;NewestSeenVersion=[string]$max;Company=$p.Item.Company;SHA256=$p.Item.SHA256;KnownVulnerable=$p.Item.LolDriversExact;MicrosoftBlocked=$p.Item.MicrosoftBlocklistExact})}}}

$baselineObj=[pscustomobject]@{Tool=$ToolName;Version=$ToolVersion;ComputerName=$env:COMPUTERNAME;Created=(Get-Date).ToString('o');Files=@($inventory|Select-Object Path,PathKey,SHA256,FileVersion,Company,State)};$baselinePath=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'baseline.json';$baselineObj|ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8|Set-Content $baselinePath -Encoding UTF8;$diff=Compare-BaselineData ($inventory.ToArray()) $CompareBaseline

# Outputs
$inventoryCsv=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'driver-inventory.csv';$findingsCsv=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'prioritized-findings.csv';$deviceCsv=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'device-interface-findings.csv';$ioctlCsv=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'ioctl-candidates.csv';$staleCsv=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'driverstore-stale.csv';$diffCsv=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'baseline-diff.csv';$sysmonCsv=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'sysmon-driver-load-events.csv';$ciCsv=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'codeintegrity-events.csv';$installCsv=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'driver-install-events.csv';$hotfixCsv=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'hotfixes.csv';$profileCsv=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'byovd-profile-matches.csv';$reportJson=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'report.json';$wazuh=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'wazuh-driver-findings.ndjson';$html=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'DriverRiskScout-report.html'
$inventory.ToArray()|Export-Csv $inventoryCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8;@($findings|Sort-Object RemediationScore -Descending)|Export-Csv $findingsCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8;$deviceFindings.ToArray()|Export-Csv $deviceCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8;$ioctlFindings.ToArray()|Export-Csv $ioctlCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8;$stale.ToArray()|Export-Csv $staleCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8;@($diff)|Export-Csv $diffCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8;@($sysmon)|Export-Csv $sysmonCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8;@($ci)|Export-Csv $ciCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8;@($inst)|Export-Csv $installCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8;@($hotfixes)|Export-Csv $hotfixCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8;$profileFindings.ToArray()|Export-Csv $profileCsv -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
$report=[pscustomobject]@{Tool=[pscustomobject]@{Name=$ToolName;Version=$ToolVersion;Mode='Read-only defensive assessment'};Host=$hostInfo;Protection=$protection;HotFixes=@($hotfixes);ThreatIntel=[pscustomobject]@{LolDriversEntries=(Get-CountSafe $lol);MicrosoftBlocklistSource=$ms.Source;MicrosoftExactHashes=$ms.ExactSha256Rules;MicrosoftFileRules=$ms.FileAttributeRules;CuratedThreatProfiles=(Get-CountSafe $threatProfiles)};Statistics=[pscustomobject]@{Files=$inventory.Count;Known=@($findings|Where-Object Classification -eq 'Known').Count;Suspicious=@($findings|Where-Object Classification -eq 'Suspicious').Count;Critical=@($findings|Where-Object Priority -eq 'Critical').Count;High=@($findings|Where-Object Priority -eq 'High').Count;DeviceInterfaces=$deviceFindings.Count;IoctlCandidates=$ioctlFindings.Count;StaleDriverStore=$stale.Count;BaselineChanges=(Get-CountSafe $diff);ThreatProfileMatches=$profileFindings.Count;ThreatProfileExact=@($profileFindings|Where-Object Confidence -eq 'Exact SHA256').Count};Findings=@($findings|Sort-Object RemediationScore -Descending);StaleDriverStore=$stale.ToArray();BaselineDiff=@($diff)};$report|ConvertTo-Json -Depth 14|Set-Content $reportJson -Encoding UTF8
# Wazuh NDJSON output. Always emit a summary event so Wazuh can distinguish
# "scan completed with no findings" from "scanner did not run".
Remove-Item $wazuh -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
$wazuhSummary=[ordered]@{driver_risk_scout=[ordered]@{
    type='summary';
    computer=$env:COMPUTERNAME;
    version=$ToolVersion;
    files=$inventory.Count;
    known=$report.Statistics.Known;
    suspicious=$report.Statistics.Suspicious;
    critical=$report.Statistics.Critical;
    high=$report.Statistics.High;
    threat_profile_matches=$report.Statistics.ThreatProfileMatches;
    hvci_enabled=$protection.HVCIRegistryEnabled;
    vulnerable_driver_blocklist_enabled=$protection.VulnerableDriverBlocklistEnabled;
    secure_boot=$protection.SecureBoot;
    wdac_policy_count=$protection.ActiveWdacPolicyCount;
    timestamp=(Get-Date).ToString('o')
}}

foreach($f in $findings){
    $o=[ordered]@{driver_risk_scout=[ordered]@{
        type='finding';
        computer=$env:COMPUTERNAME;
        version=$ToolVersion;
        priority=$f.Priority;
        score=$f.RemediationScore;
        classification=$f.Classification;
        driver=$f.FileName;
        path=$f.Path;

        running=$f.Running;

        threat_profile=$f.ThreatProfileProjects;
        threat_profile_confidence=$f.ThreatProfileConfidence;

        microsoft_blocklist_exact=$f.MicrosoftBlocklistExact;
        broad_device_acl=$f.BroadDeviceAcl;
        ioctl_candidates=$f.IoctlCandidates;
        dangerous_imports=$f.DangerousImports;
        recommended_action=$f.RecommendedAction;
        timestamp=(Get-Date).ToString('o')
    }}
    Add-Content $wazuh (($o|ConvertTo-Json -Compress -Depth 6)) -Encoding UTF8
}
if($WazuhOutputPath){
    $parent=Split-Path $WazuhOutputPath -Parent
    if($parent -and -not (Test-Path $parent)){New-Item -ItemType Directory $parent -Force|Out-Null}
    Copy-Item $wazuh $WazuhOutputPath -Force
}
New-HtmlReport $hostInfo $protection ($inventory.ToArray()) ($findings.ToArray()) ($stale.ToArray()) @($diff) $html

Write-Section 'Summary';Write-Host "Files inventoried: $($inventory.Count)";Write-Host "Known findings:    $(@($findings|Where-Object Classification -eq 'Known').Count)";Write-Host "Suspicious:        $(@($findings|Where-Object Classification -eq 'Suspicious').Count)";Write-Host "Critical/High:     $(@($findings|Where-Object{$_.Priority -in @('Critical','High')}).Count)";Write-Host "Threat profiles:   $($profileFindings.Count) match(es)";Write-Host "Stale DriverStore: $($stale.Count)";Write-Host "Baseline changes:  $((Get-CountSafe $diff))";Write-Host "HTML report:       $html";Write-Host "Output directory:  $OutputDirectory"

```
