```json
    "Source": "https://git.redteamfortress.com/j3h4ck/PPLShade"
  },
  {
    "ProfileId": "pplshade-mtxc9cb",
    "Project": "PPLShade",
    "DriverNames": ["mtxC9CB.sys"],
    "SHA256": ["0414c0d5bb6ddbcc84b3d59ce411acf1ed8b17d17054c6192e0a7594b5146d60"],
    "Devices": ["DosMtxVxd"],
    "Ioctls": [],
    "Imports": ["MmMapIoSpace"],
    "Capability": "Physical memory mapping / PPL tamper enabler",
    "RiskContext": "Driver listed by PPLShade research as providing physical-memory mapping used for kernel-memory manipulation.",
    "Source": "https://git.redteamfortress.com/j3h4ck/PPLShade"
  }
]

```

`Agent/Core/rules/DriverRiskScout.yar`:

```yar
/* DriverRiskScout defensive YARA heuristics.
   These rules identify review-worthy Windows kernel-driver characteristics.
   A match is NOT proof of a vulnerability or exploitability. */

rule DRS_Process_Termination_APIs
{
    meta:
        description = "Kernel driver imports/strings associated with process termination"
        purpose = "defensive triage"
    strings:
        $a1 = "ZwTerminateProcess" ascii wide
        $a2 = "PsLookupProcessByProcessId" ascii wide
        $a3 = "ObOpenObjectByPointer" ascii wide
    condition:
        uint16(0) == 0x5A4D and 2 of ($a*)
}

rule DRS_Kernel_Physical_Memory_APIs
{
    meta:
        description = "Kernel driver strings associated with kernel/physical memory mapping"
        purpose = "defensive triage"
    strings:
        $m1 = "MmMapIoSpace" ascii wide
        $m2 = "MmMapIoSpaceEx" ascii wide
        $m3 = "MmMapLockedPagesSpecifyCache" ascii wide
        $m4 = "MmCopyMemory" ascii wide
        $m5 = "MmMapLockedPages" ascii wide
    condition:
        uint16(0) == 0x5A4D and 2 of ($m*)
}

rule DRS_Device_Exposure_APIs
{
    meta:
        description = "Kernel driver exposes a device/symbolic-link interface"
        purpose = "defensive triage"
    strings:
        $d1 = "IoCreateDevice" ascii wide
        $d2 = "IoCreateDeviceSecure" ascii wide
        $d3 = "IoCreateSymbolicLink" ascii wide
        $d4 = "\\DosDevices\\" ascii wide
        $d5 = "\\Device\\" ascii wide
    condition:
        uint16(0) == 0x5A4D and (($d1 or $d2) and 1 of ($d3,$d4,$d5))
}

rule DRS_User_Buffer_Handling_APIs
{
    meta:
        description = "Kernel driver contains user-buffer validation/mapping APIs"
        purpose = "defensive triage"
    strings:
        $b1 = "ProbeForRead" ascii wide
        $b2 = "ProbeForWrite" ascii wide
        $b3 = "MmProbeAndLockPages" ascii wide
        $b4 = "MmGetSystemAddressForMdlSafe" ascii wide
    condition:
        uint16(0) == 0x5A4D and 2 of ($b*)
}

```

`Agent/DriverRiskScout.ps1`:

```ps1
<#
.SYNOPSIS
  DriverRiskScout 2.3.5 - Windows driver exposure, security-product and policy assessment agent.

.DESCRIPTION
  Runs the proven read-only kernel-driver core, enriches it with AV/EDR inventory,
  App Control/CiTool policy state, PnP provenance, explainable multi-score risk,
  first/last-seen history, protection regression detection, Wazuh change events,
  local HTML/JSON/CSV reporting, offline spool, and optional client->collector upload.

  No IOCTL exploitation, security-product tampering, driver loading/unloading,
  process termination or policy modification is performed.
#>

[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [string]$ConfigPath = (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'config\config.json'),
    [ValidateSet('Local','Agent')][string]$Mode,
    [string]$OutputDirectory,
    [switch]$NoNetwork,
    [switch]$EnableDeviceAclProbe,
    [switch]$EnableExploitIntel,
    [switch]$GenerateSigmaRules,
    [string]$ExploitDbPath
)

Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'

if($PSVersionTable.PSVersion.Major -lt 7){
    throw "DriverRiskScout 2.3.5 requires PowerShell 7.x (pwsh.exe). Detected: $($PSVersionTable.PSVersion)"
}

$ToolVersion='2.3.5'
$moduleDir=Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'Modules'
foreach($m in @('Common','SecurityProducts','AppControl','Provenance','State','CollectorClient','Wazuh','Reporting','Timeline','Exploitability','ExploitIntel','Sigma','Investigation','LabValidation')){
    Import-Module (Join-Path $moduleDir "$m.psm1") -Force -DisableNameChecking
}
Import-Module (Join-Path $moduleDir 'RiskEngine.psm1') -Force -DisableNameChecking

$config=Get-DRSConfig $ConfigPath
if($Mode){$config.Mode=$Mode}
if($EnableDeviceAclProbe){$config.Scan.EnableDeviceAclProbe=$true}
if($EnableExploitIntel){$config.Scan.EnableExploitIntel=$true}
if($GenerateSigmaRules){$config.Scan.GenerateSigmaRules=$true}
if($ExploitDbPath){$config.Scan.ExploitDbPath=$ExploitDbPath}

$reportsRoot=Initialize-DRSDirectory ([string]$config.Output.Root)
$stateRoot=Initialize-DRSDirectory ([string]$config.State.Root)
$spoolRoot=Initialize-DRSDirectory ([string]$config.State.SpoolRoot)
$cacheRoot=Initialize-DRSDirectory ([string]$config.State.CacheRoot)
$identity=Get-DRSAgentIdentity -StateRoot $stateRoot -Config $config
Remove-DRSOldReports -Root $reportsRoot -KeepDays ([int]$config.Output.KeepReportsDays)

$scanId=[guid]::NewGuid().ToString()
if(-not $OutputDirectory){$OutputDirectory=Join-Path $reportsRoot ("{0}-{1}" -f (Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss'),$env:COMPUTERNAME)}
$null=Initialize-DRSDirectory $OutputDirectory
$logPath=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'DriverRiskScout.log.jsonl'
Write-DRSLog -LogPath $logPath -Message "DriverRiskScout $ToolVersion starting" -Data @{scan_id=$scanId;mode=$config.Mode;pwsh=[string]$PSVersionTable.PSVersion}

try{
    $collectorDeliveryFailed=$false
    if([bool]$config.Collector.Enabled -and [bool]$config.Collector.RetrySpoolBeforeScan){
        $spoolResults=@(Send-DRSPendingSpool -Config $config -SpoolRoot $spoolRoot -LogPath $logPath)
        if($spoolResults.Count){Write-DRSLog -LogPath $logPath -Message "Retried pending collector spool" -Data @{items=$spoolResults.Count}}
    }

    $previousBaseline=Join-Path $stateRoot 'baseline.json'
    $core=Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'Core\DriverRiskScout-Core.ps1'
    $coreArgs=@{
        OutputDirectory=$OutputDirectory
        LookbackDays=[int]$config.Scan.LookbackDays
        IncludeStopped=[bool]$config.Scan.IncludeStopped
        SkipDriverStore=(-not [bool]$config.Scan.DriverStore)
        SkipEventLogs=(-not [bool]$config.Scan.EventLogs)
        EnableNvdLookup=[bool]$config.Scan.EnableNvdLookup
        EnableYara=[bool]$config.Scan.EnableYara
        EnableDeviceAclProbe=[bool]$config.Scan.EnableDeviceAclProbe
        EnableOnlineCertificateRevocation=[bool]$config.Scan.EnableOnlineCertificateRevocation
        MaxDeviceAclProbes=[int]$config.Scan.MaxDeviceAclProbes
        MaxParallelism=[int]$config.Performance.MaxParallelism
        NoNetwork=$NoNetwork
        PersistentAnalysisCacheDirectory=(Join-Path $cacheRoot 'pe')
    }
    if([string]$config.Scan.YaraExecutable){$coreArgs.YaraExecutable=[string]$config.Scan.YaraExecutable}
    if(Test-Path $previousBaseline -PathType Leaf){$coreArgs.CompareBaseline=$previousBaseline}

    Write-DRSLog -LogPath $logPath -Message 'Running kernel-driver core assessment'
    & $core @coreArgs

    $coreReportPath=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'report.json'
    if(-not (Test-Path $coreReportPath)){throw 'Core report.json was not produced.'}
    $coreReport=Get-Content $coreReportPath -Raw|ConvertFrom-Json -Depth 50
    $inventory=@(Import-Csv (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'driver-inventory.csv'))
    $coreFindings=@($coreReport.Findings)
    $ciEvents=@()
    $ciPath=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'codeintegrity-events.csv'
    if(Test-Path $ciPath){$ciEvents=@(Import-Csv $ciPath)}
    $driverInstallEvents=@()
    foreach($candidate in @('driver-install-events.csv','service-install-events.csv')){
        $ep=Join-Path $OutputDirectory $candidate
        if(Test-Path $ep){$driverInstallEvents+=@(Import-Csv $ep)}
    }
    $sysmonDriverEvents=@()
    foreach($candidate in @('sysmon-driver-load-events.csv','sysmon-events.csv')){
        $ep=Join-Path $OutputDirectory $candidate
        if(Test-Path $ep){$sysmonDriverEvents+=@(Import-Csv $ep)}
    }

    # Security product inventory
    Write-DRSLog -LogPath $logPath -Message 'Collecting AV/EDR inventory'
    $security=Get-DRSSecurityProducts -ProfilePath (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'profiles\security-products.json') -DriverInventory $inventory

    # App Control / CiTool
    Write-DRSLog -LogPath $logPath -Message 'Collecting App Control policy state'
    $appControl=Get-DRSAppControlPolicies

    # PnP provenance
    Write-DRSLog -LogPath $logPath -Message 'Resolving driver provenance'
    $provIndex=Get-DRSDriverProvenanceIndex
    $prov=@(Add-DRSProvenance -Inventory $inventory -Index $provIndex)
    $prov|Export-Csv (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'driver-provenance.csv') -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
    $provByPath=@{};foreach($p in $prov){$provByPath[[string]$p.Path]=$p}

    # Explainable scores
    $enrichedFindings=@(Add-DRSExplainableRisk -Findings $coreFindings -Protection $coreReport.Protection -AppControl $appControl -CodeIntegrityEvents $ciEvents -ProvenanceByPath $provByPath)

    # Exploitability / reachability / privilege-impact assessment.
    # Detection-only: no driver is loaded, exploited or sent IOCTLs.
    $exploitability=@(Add-DRSExploitabilityAssessment -Findings $enrichedFindings -Protection $coreReport.Protection -AppControl $appControl -PolicyPath (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'profiles\exploitability-policy.json'))
    $exploitByPath=@{}
    foreach($e in $exploitability){$exploitByPath[([string]$e.Path).ToLowerInvariant()]=$e}
    foreach($f in $enrichedFindings){
        $ek=([string]$f.Path).ToLowerInvariant()
        if($exploitByPath.ContainsKey($ek)){
            $e=$exploitByPath[$ek]
            foreach($p in $e.PSObject.Properties){
                if($p.Name -in @('FileName','Path','SHA256')){continue}
                $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName $p.Name -NotePropertyValue $p.Value -Force
            }
        }
    }

    # Exploit Intelligence: bundled user-supplied CVE/PoC research mappings.
    # This is evidence enrichment only; no PoC is downloaded, built or executed.
    $exploitIntel=@()
    $exploitInventoryLeads=@()
    if([bool]$config.Scan.EnableExploitIntel){
        $dbPath=Resolve-DRSExploitDbPath -Path ([string]$config.Scan.ExploitDbPath) -AgentRoot $PSScriptRoot
        $findingCount=@($enrichedFindings).Count
        $inventoryCount=@($inventory).Count
        Write-DRSLog -LogPath $logPath -Message 'Collecting exploit intelligence' -Data @{database=$dbPath;online=[bool]$config.Scan.EnableExploitIntelOnline;findings=$findingCount;inventory=$inventoryCount}

        # A clean host may legitimately have zero prioritized findings. In that case
        # skip finding enrichment, but still correlate the full driver inventory with
        # the research database so research leads are not lost.
        if($findingCount -gt 0){
            $exploitIntel=@(Add-DRSExploitIntel -Findings @($enrichedFindings) -DatabasePath $dbPath -CacheRoot $cacheRoot `
                -EnableOnline ([bool]$config.Scan.EnableExploitIntelOnline) -NoNetwork:$NoNetwork)
        } else {
            $exploitIntel=@()
            Write-DRSLog -LogPath $logPath -Message 'Exploit intelligence finding enrichment skipped: no prioritized findings'
        }

        $exploitInventoryLeads=@(Get-DRSExploitIntelInventoryLeads -Inventory @($inventory) -DatabasePath $dbPath)
        $exploitInventoryLeads|Export-Csv (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'exploit-intel-inventory-leads.csv') -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
        ConvertTo-Json -InputObject @($exploitInventoryLeads) -Depth 20|Set-Content (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'exploit-intel-inventory-leads.json') -Encoding UTF8

        $intelByPath=@{}
        foreach($ei in $exploitIntel){$intelByPath[([string]$ei.Path).ToLowerInvariant()]=$ei}
        foreach($f in $enrichedFindings){
            $key=([string]$f.Path).ToLowerInvariant()
            if($intelByPath.ContainsKey($key)){
                $ei=$intelByPath[$key]
                $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelMatchCount -NotePropertyValue ([int]$ei.MatchCount) -Force
                $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelMatches -NotePropertyValue @($ei.Matches) -Force
                $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelCves -NotePropertyValue @($ei.CVEs) -Force
                $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelReferences -NotePropertyValue @($ei.References) -Force
                $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelTypes -NotePropertyValue @($ei.ExploitTypes) -Force
                $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelMaxCVSS -NotePropertyValue ([double]$ei.MaxCVSS) -Force
                $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName HasPublicPoCReference -NotePropertyValue ([bool]$ei.HasPublicPoCReference) -Force
                $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelSourceVerifiedFlag -NotePropertyValue ([bool]$ei.SourceVerifiedFlag) -Force
                $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ResearchMaturityScore -NotePropertyValue ([int]$ei.ResearchMaturityScore) -Force
                $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelSummary -NotePropertyValue ([string]$ei.Summary) -Force

                # Merge CVEs without discarding CVEs already found by other sources.
                $merged=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
                foreach($raw in @($f.CVEs,$f.ExploitIntelCves)){
                    foreach($part in @($raw)){
                        foreach($piece in ([string]$part -split '[,;|\s]+')){
                            $cv=$piece.Trim().ToUpperInvariant()
                            if($cv -match '^CVE-\d{4}-\d{4,}$' -and -not $merged.Contains($cv)){$merged.Add($cv)}
                        }
                    }
                }
                $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName CVEList -NotePropertyValue $merged.ToArray() -Force
            }
        }
        $exploitIntel|ConvertTo-Json -Depth 30|Set-Content (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'exploit-intel.json') -Encoding UTF8
    } else {
        foreach($f in $enrichedFindings){
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelMatchCount -NotePropertyValue 0 -Force
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelMatches -NotePropertyValue @() -Force
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelCves -NotePropertyValue @() -Force
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelReferences -NotePropertyValue @() -Force
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelTypes -NotePropertyValue @() -Force
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelMaxCVSS -NotePropertyValue 0 -Force
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName HasPublicPoCReference -NotePropertyValue $false -Force
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelSourceVerifiedFlag -NotePropertyValue $false -Force
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ResearchMaturityScore -NotePropertyValue 0 -Force
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName ExploitIntelSummary -NotePropertyValue 'Exploit intelligence disabled' -Force
        }
    }

    # First/last seen + change detection
    $history=Update-DRSFileHistory -Inventory $inventory -HistoryPath (Join-Path $stateRoot 'file-history.json') -SuppressInitialChangeEvents ([bool]$config.State.SuppressInitialChangeEvents)
    $protectionChanges=@(Compare-DRSProtectionState -Current $coreReport.Protection -StatePath (Join-Path $stateRoot 'protection-state.json'))
    $securityProductChanges=@(Compare-DRSSecurityProductState -Products $security.Products -StatePath (Join-Path $stateRoot 'security-products-state.json') -SuppressInitialChangeEvents ([bool]$config.State.SuppressInitialChangeEvents))
    $timeline=@(New-DRSDriverTimeline -OutputDirectory $OutputDirectory)
    $timeline|Export-Csv (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'driver-timeline.csv') -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
    $securityProductChanges|Export-Csv (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'security-product-changes.csv') -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
    foreach($f in $enrichedFindings){
        $key=([string]$f.Path).ToLowerInvariant()
        $histKey=$null
        foreach($hk in $history.History.Keys){if(([string]$hk) -eq $key){$histKey=$hk;break}}
        if($histKey){
            $h=$history.History[$histKey]
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName FirstSeen -NotePropertyValue ([string]$h.first_seen) -Force
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName LastSeen -NotePropertyValue ([string]$h.last_seen) -Force
        }
    }
    # Vulnerability Investigation Console records: source evidence, external research links
    # and safe read-only validation commands.
    $investigations=@(New-DRSInvestigationRecords -Findings $enrichedFindings -Protection $coreReport.Protection -AppControl $appControl)
    $investigationByPath=@{}
    foreach($i in $investigations){$investigationByPath[([string]$i.Path).ToLowerInvariant()]=$i}
    foreach($f in $enrichedFindings){
        $ik=([string]$f.Path).ToLowerInvariant()
        if($investigationByPath.ContainsKey($ik)){
            $i=$investigationByPath[$ik]
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName MatchEvidence -NotePropertyValue @($i.MatchEvidence) -Force
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName InvestigationSources -NotePropertyValue @($i.Sources) -Force
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName SafeValidationCommands -NotePropertyValue @($i.SafeValidationCommands) -Force
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName CVEList -NotePropertyValue @($i.CVEList) -Force
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName InvestigationConclusion -NotePropertyValue ([string]$i.Conclusion) -Force
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName InvestigationSafetyNote -NotePropertyValue ([string]$i.SafetyNote) -Force
        }
    }

    # Lab Validation Mode: correlate existing Code Integrity / SCM / Sysmon telemetry.
    # It never creates or starts a driver service and never sends IOCTLs.
    $labValidation=@(New-DRSLabValidationRecords -Findings $enrichedFindings -Protection $coreReport.Protection -AppControl $appControl `
        -CodeIntegrityEvents $ciEvents -DriverInstallEvents $driverInstallEvents -SysmonDriverEvents $sysmonDriverEvents)
    $labByPath=@{}
    foreach($l in $labValidation){$labByPath[([string]$l.Path).ToLowerInvariant()]=$l}
    foreach($f in $enrichedFindings){
        $lk=([string]$f.Path).ToLowerInvariant()
        if($labByPath.ContainsKey($lk)){
            $l=$labByPath[$lk]
            foreach($p in $l.PSObject.Properties){
                if($p.Name -in @('FileName','Path')){continue}
                $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName $p.Name -NotePropertyValue $p.Value -Force
            }
        }
    }
    $labValidation|Export-Csv (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'lab-validation.csv') -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8

    foreach($f in $enrichedFindings){
        if($null -eq $f.PSObject.Properties['InvestigationPage']){
            $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName InvestigationPage -NotePropertyValue '' -Force
        }
    }
    if([bool]$config.Investigation.GenerateDetailPages){
        $investigationDir=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'investigations'
        New-DRSInvestigationPages -Findings $enrichedFindings -Directory $investigationDir -ToolVersion $ToolVersion -Protection $coreReport.Protection -AppControl $appControl
    }

    $enrichedFindings|Export-Csv (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'prioritized-findings-v2.csv') -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
    $exploitability|Export-Csv (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'exploitability-assessment.csv') -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
    ConvertTo-Json -InputObject @($investigations) -Depth 30|Set-Content (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'investigations.json') -Encoding UTF8
    $security.Products|Export-Csv (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'security-products.csv') -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
    @($appControl.Policies)|Export-Csv (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'app-control-policies.csv') -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
    $history.Changes|Export-Csv (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'driver-changes.csv') -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
    $protectionChanges|Export-Csv (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'protection-changes.csv') -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8

    # Persist newest baseline for next run
    Copy-Item (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'baseline.json') $previousBaseline -Force

    $sigmaResult=$null
    if([bool]$config.Scan.GenerateSigmaRules){
        Write-DRSLog -LogPath $logPath -Message 'Generating Sigma detection rules'
        $sigmaResult=New-DRSSigmaRules -Findings $enrichedFindings -OutputDirectory $OutputDirectory -ToolVersion $ToolVersion
    }

    $medium=@($enrichedFindings|Where-Object Priority -eq 'Medium').Count
    $high=@($enrichedFindings|Where-Object Priority -eq 'High').Count
    $critical=@($enrichedFindings|Where-Object Priority -eq 'Critical').Count
    $known=@($enrichedFindings|Where-Object Classification -eq 'Known').Count
    $suspicious=@($enrichedFindings|Where-Object Classification -eq 'Suspicious').Count

    $report=[ordered]@{
        schema_version='2.3'
        scan_id=$scanId
        created_at=(Get-Date).ToString('o')
        tool=[ordered]@{name='DriverRiskScout';version=$ToolVersion;mode=[string]$config.Mode}
        host=[ordered]@{
            agent_id=[string]$identity.AgentId
            computer_name=[string]$coreReport.Host.ComputerName
            domain=[string]$identity.Domain
            site=[string]$identity.Site
            tags=@($identity.Tags)
            os_caption=[string]$coreReport.Host.OSCaption
            os_version=[string]$coreReport.Host.OSVersion
            build_number=[string]$coreReport.Host.BuildNumber
            latest_hotfix=[string]$coreReport.Host.LatestHotFix
            latest_hotfix_date=[string]$coreReport.Host.LatestHotFixDate
        }
        statistics=[ordered]@{
            files=$inventory.Count;known=$known;suspicious=$suspicious;critical=$critical;high=$high;medium=$medium;
            stale_driverstore=[int]$coreReport.Statistics.StaleDriverStore;
            exploitability_high=@($enrichedFindings|Where-Object {$_.CurrentExploitabilityScore -ge 70}).Count;
            privilege_escalation_high=@($enrichedFindings|Where-Object {$_.PrivilegeEscalationScore -ge 70}).Count;
            exploit_intel_matches=@($enrichedFindings|Where-Object {$_.ExploitIntelMatchCount -gt 0}).Count;
            public_poc_references=@($enrichedFindings|Where-Object {$_.HasPublicPoCReference}).Count;
            exploit_intel_inventory_leads=@($exploitInventoryLeads).Count;
            driver_changes=@($history.Changes).Count;protection_changes=$protectionChanges.Count;security_product_changes=$securityProductChanges.Count;timeline_events=$timeline.Count
        }
        protection=$coreReport.Protection
        app_control=[ordered]@{source=$appControl.Source;policy_count=$appControl.PolicyCount;enforced_count=$appControl.EnforcedCount;policies=$appControl.Policies}
        security_products=[ordered]@{products=$security.Products;defender=$security.Defender;security_center=$security.SecurityCenterProducts}
        findings=$enrichedFindings
        changes=[ordered]@{driver_changes=$history.Changes;protection_changes=$protectionChanges;security_product_changes=$securityProductChanges}
        threat_intel=$coreReport.ThreatIntel
        stale_driverstore=$coreReport.StaleDriverStore
        provenance_summary=[ordered]@{bound_files=@($prov|Where-Object CurrentlyBound).Count;unbound_files=@($prov|Where-Object{-not $_.CurrentlyBound}).Count};timeline_summary=[ordered]@{events=$timeline.Count;file='driver-timeline.csv'}
        source_report=[ordered]@{core_version=[string]$coreReport.Tool.Version;core_report='report.json'}
        exploit_intel=[ordered]@{
            enabled=[bool]$config.Scan.EnableExploitIntel
            online=[bool]$config.Scan.EnableExploitIntelOnline
            database=[string]$config.Scan.ExploitDbPath
            findings_with_matches=@($enrichedFindings|Where-Object {$_.ExploitIntelMatchCount -gt 0}).Count
            public_poc_references=@($enrichedFindings|Where-Object {$_.HasPublicPoCReference}).Count
            max_research_maturity=$(if($enrichedFindings.Count){(@($enrichedFindings|Measure-Object ResearchMaturityScore -Maximum).Maximum)}else{0})
            inventory_research_leads=@($exploitInventoryLeads).Count
            inventory_research_lead_file='exploit-intel-inventory-leads.csv'
            inventory_leads=@($exploitInventoryLeads|Select-Object -First 200)
        }
        sigma_rules=$(if($sigmaResult){[ordered]@{generated=$sigmaResult.RulesGenerated;directory=$sigmaResult.OutputDirectory;rules=$sigmaResult.Rules}}else{$null})
    }

    $reportPath=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'report-v2.json'
    $report|ConvertTo-Json -Depth 60|Set-Content $reportPath -Encoding UTF8

    if([bool]$config.Output.Html){
        New-DRSV2HtmlReport -Report $report -Path (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'DriverRiskScout-v2-report.html')
    }

    $wazuhEvents=@(New-DRSWazuhEvents -ReportV2 $report)
    if([bool]$config.Wazuh.Enabled){
        Write-DRSWazuhNdjson -Events $wazuhEvents -Path ([string]$config.Wazuh.OutputPath)
        Write-DRSWazuhNdjson -Events $wazuhEvents -Path (Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'wazuh-v2.ndjson')
    }

    # Agent/collector payload
    $payload=[ordered]@{
        schema_version='2.3'
        report=$report
        wazuh_events=$wazuhEvents
    }
    if([bool]$config.Output.IncludeFullInventoryInAgentPayload){$payload.inventory=$inventory}
    $payload.provenance=$prov

    if(([string]$config.Mode -eq 'Agent') -or [bool]$config.Collector.Enabled){
        $send=Send-DRSCollectorPayload -Config $config -Payload $payload -SpoolRoot $spoolRoot -LogPath $logPath
        Write-DRSLog -LogPath $logPath -Message 'Collector submission completed' -Data @{sent=$send.Sent;skipped=$send.Skipped;reason=$send.Reason}
        if([bool]$config.Collector.Enabled -and -not $send.Sent){$collectorDeliveryFailed=$true}
    }

    Write-DRSLog -LogPath $logPath -Message 'Scan completed' -Data @{files=$inventory.Count;known=$known;critical=$critical;high=$high;medium=$medium}
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "=== DriverRiskScout 2.3.5 Summary ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host "Host:              $($report.host.computer_name)"
    Write-Host "Files:             $($report.statistics.files)"
    Write-Host "Known:             $known"
    Write-Host "Critical / High:   $critical / $high"
    Write-Host "Medium:            $medium"
    Write-Host "Exploitability >=70:$(@($enrichedFindings|Where-Object {$_.CurrentExploitabilityScore -ge 70}).Count)"
    Write-Host "PrivEsc score >=70:$(@($enrichedFindings|Where-Object {$_.PrivilegeEscalationScore -ge 70}).Count)"
    Write-Host "Exploit Intel:     $(@($enrichedFindings|Where-Object {$_.ExploitIntelMatchCount -gt 0}).Count) finding(s)"
    Write-Host "Public PoC refs:   $(@($enrichedFindings|Where-Object {$_.HasPublicPoCReference}).Count)"
    Write-Host "Research leads:    $(@($exploitInventoryLeads).Count) inventory mapping(s)"
    Write-Host "Sigma rules:       $(if($sigmaResult){$sigmaResult.RulesGenerated}else{0})"
    Write-Host "AV/EDR products:   $(@($security.Products|Where-Object Active).Count) active"
    Write-Host "App Control:       $($appControl.EnforcedCount) enforced policy/policies"
    Write-Host "Driver changes:    $(@($history.Changes).Count)"
    Write-Host "Protection changes:$($protectionChanges.Count)"
    Write-Host "Security changes:  $($securityProductChanges.Count)"
    Write-Host "Output:            $OutputDirectory"

    if($collectorDeliveryFailed){exit [int]$config.ExitCodes.Partial}
    if($critical -gt 0){exit [int]$config.ExitCodes.Critical}
    if($high -gt 0){exit [int]$config.ExitCodes.High}
    if($medium -gt 0){exit [int]$config.ExitCodes.Medium}
    exit 0
}catch{
    try{Write-DRSLog -LogPath $logPath -Level ERROR -Message 'DriverRiskScout failed' -Data @{error=$_.Exception.Message;stack=$_.ScriptStackTrace}}catch{}
    Write-Error $_
    exit [int]$config.ExitCodes.Error
}
```

`Agent/Install-Agent.ps1`:

```ps1
[CmdletBinding(SupportsShouldProcess)]
param(
  [string]$InstallPath='C:\Program Files\DriverRiskScout',
  [string]$DataPath='C:\ProgramData\DriverRiskScout',
  [string]$CollectorUrl='',
  [string]$ApiKey='',
  [string]$ScheduleTime='03:15',
  [switch]$NoScheduledTask
)
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
if(-not ([Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal]::new([Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent())).IsInRole([Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)){
  throw 'Run this installer from an elevated PowerShell 7 session.'
}
$source=$PSScriptRoot
if($PSCmdlet.ShouldProcess($InstallPath,'Install DriverRiskScout agent')){
  New-Item -ItemType Directory $InstallPath -Force|Out-Null
  New-Item -ItemType Directory $DataPath -Force|Out-Null
  Copy-Item "$source\*" $InstallPath -Recurse -Force
  $cfgPath=Join-Path $InstallPath 'config\config.json'
  if(-not (Test-Path $cfgPath)){Copy-Item (Join-Path $InstallPath 'config\config.example.json') $cfgPath}
  if($CollectorUrl){
    $cfg=Get-Content $cfgPath -Raw|ConvertFrom-Json -AsHashtable -Depth 30
    $cfg.Mode='Agent';$cfg.Collector.Enabled=$true;$cfg.Collector.Url=$CollectorUrl
    $cfg|ConvertTo-Json -Depth 30|Set-Content $cfgPath -Encoding UTF8
  }
  if($ApiKey){[Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable('DRS_COLLECTOR_API_KEY',$ApiKey,'Machine')}
  if(-not $NoScheduledTask){
    $parts=$ScheduleTime.Split(':');$hour=[int]$parts[0];$minute=[int]$parts[1]
    $action=New-ScheduledTaskAction -Execute 'pwsh.exe' -Argument ('-NoLogo -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "{0}" -ConfigPath "{1}"' -f (Join-Path $InstallPath 'DriverRiskScout.ps1'),$cfgPath)
    $trigger=New-ScheduledTaskTrigger -Daily -At ([datetime]::Today.AddHours($hour).AddMinutes($minute))
    $principal=New-ScheduledTaskPrincipal -UserId 'SYSTEM' -LogonType ServiceAccount -RunLevel Highest
    $settings=New-ScheduledTaskSettingsSet -StartWhenAvailable -MultipleInstances IgnoreNew -ExecutionTimeLimit (New-TimeSpan -Hours 2)
    Register-ScheduledTask -TaskName 'DriverRiskScout Daily' -Action $action -Trigger $trigger -Principal $principal -Settings $settings -Force|Out-Null
  }
  Write-Host "Installed to $InstallPath" -ForegroundColor Green
  Write-Host "Configuration: $cfgPath"
}
```

`Agent/Modules/AppControl.psm1`:

```psm1
Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0

function Get-DRSAppControlPolicies {
    $policies=@();$source='Unavailable';$error=''
    $ci=Get-Command CiTool.exe -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if($ci){
        try{
            $raw=& $ci.Source -lp -json 2>$null
            if($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0 -and $raw){
                $o=($raw -join [Environment]::NewLine)|ConvertFrom-Json -Depth 30
                $policies=@($o.Policies)
                $source='CiTool -lp -json'
            }
        }catch{$error=$_.Exception.Message}
    }
    if($policies.Count -eq 0){
        try{
            $dir="$env:SystemRoot\System32\CodeIntegrity\CiPolicies\Active"
            $files=@(Get-ChildItem $dir -Filter '*.cip' -File -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
            $policies=@($files|ForEach-Object{[pscustomobject]@{
                PolicyID=$_.BaseName;FriendlyName=$_.Name;IsEnforced='Unknown';IsAuthorized='Unknown';
                IsSigned='Unknown';PlatformPolicy='Unknown';HasFileOnDisk=$true;Path=$_.FullName
            }})
            if($policies.Count){$source='Active CIP files'}
        }catch{$error=$_.Exception.Message}
    }
    return [pscustomobject]@{
        Source=$source
        Error=$error
        PolicyCount=$policies.Count
        EnforcedCount=@($policies|Where-Object{[string]$_.IsEnforced -match 'True|1'}).Count
        Policies=$policies
    }
}

function Get-DRSDriverPolicyAssessment {
    param($Finding,$Protection,$AppControl,[array]$CodeIntegrityEvents)

    if([bool]$Finding.Running){
        return [pscustomobject]@{Status='Observed loaded';Confidence='High';Reason='Driver is currently registered as running.'}
    }
    if([bool]$Finding.RecentLoad){
        return [pscustomobject]@{Status='Observed loaded recently';Confidence='High';Reason='Recent driver-load telemetry matched this file.'}
    }

    $path=[string]$Finding.Path
    $ciMatch=@($CodeIntegrityEvents|Where-Object{[string]$_.Message -like "*$path*" -or ([string]$_.Message -like "*$([IO.Path]::GetFileName($path))*")})
    if($ciMatch.Count -gt 0){
        return [pscustomobject]@{Status='Code Integrity event observed';Confidence='Medium';Reason='Code Integrity telemetry references this driver; inspect event IDs/messages for allow/block semantics.'}
    }

    if([bool]$Finding.MicrosoftBlocklistExact -and [bool]$Protection.VulnerableDriverBlocklistEnabled){
        return [pscustomobject]@{Status='Likely constrained by policy';Confidence='Medium';Reason='Exact Microsoft blocklist hash plus local vulnerable-driver blocklist enabled. No active load was observed.'}
    }

    if([bool]$Protection.HVCIRegistryEnabled -and [int]$AppControl.EnforcedCount -gt 0){
        return [pscustomobject]@{Status='Policy protections present';Confidence='Low';Reason='HVCI/App Control protections are active, but no exact policy decision for this driver was observed.'}
    }

    return [pscustomobject]@{Status='Unknown';Confidence='Low';Reason='No observed load or definitive policy decision was available.'}
}
```

`Agent/Modules/CollectorClient.psm1`:

```psm1
Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0

function Get-DRSCollectorApiKey {
    param($Config)
    $envName=[string]$Config.Collector.ApiKeyEnvironmentVariable
    if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($envName)){return ''}
    $v=[string][Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable($envName,'Process')
    if(-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($v)){return $v}
    $v=[string][Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable($envName,'Machine')
    if(-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($v)){return $v}
    return [string][Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable($envName,'User')
}

function Send-DRSCollectorPayload {
    param($Config,$Payload,[string]$SpoolRoot,[string]$LogPath)

    if(-not [bool]$Config.Collector.Enabled){return [pscustomobject]@{Sent=$false;Skipped=$true;Reason='Collector disabled'}}
    $url=([string]$Config.Collector.Url).TrimEnd('/') + '/api/v1/ingest'
    $key=Get-DRSCollectorApiKey $Config
    if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($key)){throw "Collector is enabled but API key environment variable '$($Config.Collector.ApiKeyEnvironmentVariable)' is empty."}

    $headers=@{'X-DRS-API-Key'=$key}
    $body=$Payload|ConvertTo-Json -Depth 60 -Compress
    try{
        $args=@{
            Uri=$url;Method='Post';Headers=$headers;ContentType='application/json';Body=$body;
            TimeoutSec=[int]$Config.Collector.TimeoutSeconds;ErrorAction='Stop'
        }
        if([bool]$Config.Collector.SkipCertificateCheck){$args.SkipCertificateCheck=$true}
        $r=Invoke-RestMethod @args
        return [pscustomobject]@{Sent=$true;Skipped=$false;Reason='';Response=$r}
    }catch{
        if(-not (Test-Path $SpoolRoot)){New-Item -ItemType Directory $SpoolRoot -Force|Out-Null}
        $name='pending-{0}-{1}.json' -f (Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss'),([guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
        $path=Join-Path $SpoolRoot $name
        $body|Set-Content $path -Encoding UTF8
        if($LogPath){Write-DRSLog -LogPath $LogPath -Level WARN -Message "Collector upload failed; payload spooled to $path" -Data @{error=$_.Exception.Message}}
        return [pscustomobject]@{Sent=$false;Skipped=$false;Reason=$_.Exception.Message;SpoolPath=$path}
    }
}

function Send-DRSPendingSpool {
    param($Config,[string]$SpoolRoot,[string]$LogPath)
    if(-not [bool]$Config.Collector.Enabled -or -not (Test-Path $SpoolRoot)){return @()}
    $key=Get-DRSCollectorApiKey $Config
    if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($key)){return @()}
    $url=([string]$Config.Collector.Url).TrimEnd('/') + '/api/v1/ingest'
    $headers=@{'X-DRS-API-Key'=$key}
    $results=@()
    foreach($f in @(Get-ChildItem $SpoolRoot -Filter 'pending-*.json' -File -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue|Sort-Object CreationTime)){
        try{
            $args=@{Uri=$url;Method='Post';Headers=$headers;ContentType='application/json';Body=(Get-Content $f.FullName -Raw);TimeoutSec=[int]$Config.Collector.TimeoutSeconds;ErrorAction='Stop'}
            if([bool]$Config.Collector.SkipCertificateCheck){$args.SkipCertificateCheck=$true}
            $null=Invoke-RestMethod @args
            Remove-Item $f.FullName -Force
            $results += [pscustomobject]@{File=$f.Name;Sent=$true}
        }catch{
            $results += [pscustomobject]@{File=$f.Name;Sent=$false;Error=$_.Exception.Message}
            break
        }
    }
    return $results
}
```

`Agent/Modules/Common.psm1`:

```psm1
Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0

function Get-DRSProp {
    param($Object,[string]$Name,$Default=$null)
    if($null -eq $Object){ return $Default }
    if($Object -is [System.Collections.IDictionary]){
        foreach($k in $Object.Keys){ if(([string]$k) -ceq $Name){ return $Object[$k] } }
        foreach($k in $Object.Keys){ if(([string]$k) -ieq $Name){ return $Object[$k] } }
        return $Default
    }
    try {
        $p = $Object.PSObject.Properties[$Name]
        if($null -ne $p){ return $p.Value }
    } catch {}
    return $Default
}

function Get-DRSCount {
    param([AllowNull()]$Value)
    if($null -eq $Value){ return 0 }
    return @($Value).Count
}

function ConvertTo-DRSHashtable {
    param($Object)
    if($null -eq $Object){ return $null }
    if($Object -is [System.Collections.IDictionary]){
        $h = [ordered]@{}
        foreach($k in $Object.Keys){ $h[[string]$k] = ConvertTo-DRSHashtable $Object[$k] }
        return $h
    }
    if($Object -is [pscustomobject]){
        $h = [ordered]@{}
        foreach($p in $Object.PSObject.Properties){ $h[$p.Name] = ConvertTo-DRSHashtable $p.Value }
        return $h
    }
    if(($Object -is [System.Collections.IEnumerable]) -and -not ($Object -is [string])){
        return @($Object | ForEach-Object { ConvertTo-DRSHashtable $_ })
    }
    return $Object
}

function Merge-DRSHashtable {
    param([System.Collections.IDictionary]$Base,[System.Collections.IDictionary]$Override)
    $result = [ordered]@{}
    foreach($k in $Base.Keys){ $result[$k] = $Base[$k] }
    foreach($k in $Override.Keys){
        if($result.Contains($k) -and $result[$k] -is [System.Collections.IDictionary] -and $Override[$k] -is [System.Collections.IDictionary]){
            $result[$k] = Merge-DRSHashtable (ConvertTo-DRSHashtable $result[$k]) (ConvertTo-DRSHashtable $Override[$k])
        } else {
            $result[$k] = $Override[$k]
        }
    }
    return $result
}

function Get-DRSDefaultConfig {
    return [ordered]@{
        Version = 1
        Mode = 'Local'
        Identity = [ordered]@{ Site = ''; Tags = @() }
        Scan = [ordered]@{
            IncludeStopped = $true
            DriverStore = $true
            EventLogs = $true
            LookbackDays = 30
            EnableNvdLookup = $false
            EnableYara = $false
            YaraExecutable = ''
            EnableDeviceAclProbe = $false
            MaxDeviceAclProbes = 25
            EnableOnlineCertificateRevocation = $false
            EnableExploitIntel = $true
            EnableExploitIntelOnline = $false
            ExploitDbPath = 'profiles\exploit-db.json'
            GenerateSigmaRules = $true
        }
        Output = [ordered]@{
            Root = 'C:\ProgramData\DriverRiskScout\Reports'
            KeepReportsDays = 30
            IncludeFullInventoryInAgentPayload = $true
            Html = $true
            Json = $true
            Csv = $true
        }
        State = [ordered]@{
            Root = 'C:\ProgramData\DriverRiskScout\State'
            CacheRoot = 'C:\ProgramData\DriverRiskScout\Cache'
            SpoolRoot = 'C:\ProgramData\DriverRiskScout\Spool'
            SuppressInitialChangeEvents = $true
        }
        Collector = [ordered]@{
            Enabled = $false
            Url = 'https://drs-server.example.local:8443'
            ApiKeyEnvironmentVariable = 'DRS_COLLECTOR_API_KEY'
            SkipCertificateCheck = $false
            TimeoutSeconds = 30
            RetrySpoolBeforeScan = $true
        }
        Investigation = [ordered]@{
            GenerateDetailPages = $true
            IncludeGitHubSearchLinks = $true
            IncludeSafeValidationCommands = $true
        }
        Wazuh = [ordered]@{
            Enabled = $true
            OutputPath = 'C:\ProgramData\DriverRiskScout\wazuh-driver-findings.ndjson'
        }
        Performance = [ordered]@{
            MaxParallelism = 4
            IncrementalState = $true
        }
        ExitCodes = [ordered]@{
            Medium = 10
            High = 20
            Critical = 30
            Partial = 50
            Error = 90
        }
    }
}

function Get-DRSConfig {
    param([string]$Path)
    $base = Get-DRSDefaultConfig
    if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Path) -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Path -PathType Leaf)){
        return $base
    }
    $raw = Get-Content -LiteralPath $Path -Raw
    $custom = $raw | ConvertFrom-Json -AsHashtable -Depth 50
    return Merge-DRSHashtable $base $custom
}

function Initialize-DRSDirectory {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$Path)
    if(-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Path)){
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $Path -Force | Out-Null
    }
    return (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $Path).Path
}

function Write-DRSLog {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$LogPath,
        [ValidateSet('DEBUG','INFO','WARN','ERROR')][string]$Level='INFO',
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$Message,
        [hashtable]$Data
    )
    $record = [ordered]@{
        timestamp = (Get-Date).ToString('o')
        level = $Level
        message = $Message
    }
    if($Data){ $record.data = $Data }
    $line = $record | ConvertTo-Json -Compress -Depth 8
    Add-Content -LiteralPath $LogPath -Value $line -Encoding UTF8
    $fg = switch($Level){ 'ERROR'{'Red'} 'WARN'{'Yellow'} 'DEBUG'{'DarkGray'} default{'Gray'} }
    Write-Host ("[{0}] {1}" -f $Level,$Message) -ForegroundColor $fg
}

function Get-DRSAgentIdentity {
    param([string]$StateRoot,$Config)
    $path=Join-Path $StateRoot 'agent-id.txt'
    $id=''
    if(Test-Path $path -PathType Leaf){
        try{$id=(Get-Content $path -Raw).Trim()}catch{}
    }
    if($id -notmatch '^[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}$'){
        $id=[guid]::NewGuid().ToString()
        Set-Content $path $id -Encoding ASCII
    }
    $domain=''
    try{$domain=[string](Get-CimInstance Win32_ComputerSystem -ErrorAction Stop).Domain}catch{}
    return [pscustomobject]@{
        AgentId=$id
        ComputerName=$env:COMPUTERNAME
        Domain=$domain
        Site=[string]$Config.Identity.Site
        Tags=@($Config.Identity.Tags|ForEach-Object{[string]$_})
    }
}

function Remove-DRSOldReports {
    param([string]$Root,[int]$KeepDays)
    if(-not (Test-Path $Root)){ return }
    $cutoff=(Get-Date).AddDays(-[Math]::Max(1,$KeepDays))
    Get-ChildItem $Root -Directory -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Where-Object LastWriteTime -lt $cutoff |
        Remove-Item -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
}
```

`Agent/Modules/ExploitIntel.psm1`:

```psm1
Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0

function Get-DRSExploitProperty {
    param($Object,[string]$Name,$Default=$null)
    if($null -eq $Object){ return $Default }

    # ConvertFrom-Json -AsHashtable returns Hashtable/OrderedHashtable records.
    # Resolve dictionary keys explicitly and case-sensitively first so JSON keys
    # such as "init" and "INIT" remain distinct.
    if($Object -is [System.Collections.IDictionary]){
        foreach($key in $Object.Keys){
            if([string]::Equals([string]$key,$Name,[System.StringComparison]::Ordinal)){
                return $Object[$key]
            }
        }

        # Optional case-insensitive fallback for research DBs that vary only in
        # conventional field casing. Ambiguous case-colliding keys are not guessed.
        $ciMatches=@(
            foreach($key in $Object.Keys){
                if([string]::Equals([string]$key,$Name,[System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)){
                    $key
                }
            }
        )
        if($ciMatches.Count -eq 1){
            return $Object[$ciMatches[0]]
        }
        return $Default
    }

    $p=$Object.PSObject.Properties[$Name]
    if($null -eq $p){ return $Default }
    return $p.Value
}


function Resolve-DRSExploitDbPath {
    param([string]$Path,[string]$AgentRoot)
    if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Path)){
        return (Join-Path $AgentRoot 'profiles\exploit-db.json')
    }
    if([System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($Path)){ return $Path }
    return (Join-Path $AgentRoot $Path)
}

function Import-DRSExploitDatabase {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$Path)

    if(-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Path -PathType Leaf)){
        throw "Exploit intelligence database not found: $Path"
    }

    $records=@(Get-Content -LiteralPath $Path -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json -AsHashtable -Depth 30)
    $out=[System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
    $seen=@{}

    foreach($r in $records){
        if($null -eq $r){continue}
        $cve=([string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $r 'CVE' '')).Trim().ToUpperInvariant()
        if($cve -and $cve -notmatch '^CVE-\d{4}-\d{4,}$'){continue}
        $drivers=@((Get-DRSExploitProperty $r 'AffectedDrivers' @())|ForEach-Object{([string]$_).Trim()}|Where-Object{$_})
        $key=if($cve){$cve}else{([string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $r 'Url' '')).Trim()}
        if($key -and $seen.ContainsKey($key)){continue}
        if($key){$seen[$key]=$true}

        $out.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            CVE=$cve
            Url=[string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $r 'Url' '')
            Type=[string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $r 'Type' '')
            SourceVerifiedFlag=[bool](Get-DRSExploitProperty $r 'Verified' $false)
            Date=[string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $r 'Date' '')
            Description=[string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $r 'Description' '')
            CVSS=$(if($null -eq (Get-DRSExploitProperty $r 'CVSS' 0)){0}else{[double](Get-DRSExploitProperty $r 'CVSS' 0)})
            AffectedDrivers=$drivers
            Tags=@((Get-DRSExploitProperty $r 'Tags' @())|ForEach-Object{[string]$_})
            Source='Bundled exploit-db.json'
            SourceTrust='User-supplied research data; not independently verified by DriverRiskScout'
            Applicability='Affected driver filename mapping only; affected version/build is not provided by this database'
        })|Out-Null
    }
    return $out.ToArray()
}

function Get-DRSPropertyValue {
    param($Object,[string]$Name)
    if($null -eq $Object){return $null}
    $prop=$Object.PSObject.Properties[$Name]
    if($null -eq $prop){return $null}
    return $prop.Value
}

function Get-DRSExistingCves {
    param($Finding)
    $values=[System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
    foreach($raw in @((Get-DRSPropertyValue $Finding 'CVEs'),(Get-DRSPropertyValue $Finding 'CVEList'))){
        if($null -eq $raw){continue}
        $parts=if($raw -is [System.Collections.IEnumerable] -and $raw -isnot [string]){@($raw)}else{([string]$raw -split '[,;|\s]+')}
        foreach($p in $parts){
            $c=([string]$p).Trim().ToUpperInvariant()
            if($c -match '^CVE-\d{4}-\d{4,}$' -and -not $values.Contains($c)){$values.Add($c)}
        }
    }
    return $values.ToArray()
}

function Get-DRSResearchMaturityScore {
    param([array]$ResearchRecords)
    if(-not $ResearchRecords -or $ResearchRecords.Count -eq 0){return 0}
    $score=0
    $score+=30 # exact affected-driver-name mapping from the local database
    if(@($ResearchRecords|Where-Object {[bool](Get-DRSExploitProperty $_ 'SourceVerifiedFlag' $false)}).Count){$score+=20}
    if(@($ResearchRecords|Where-Object {[double](Get-DRSExploitProperty $_ 'CVSS' 0) -ge 7.0}).Count){$score+=15}
    if(@($ResearchRecords|Where-Object {[string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $_ 'Type' '') -match '(?i)Privilege|Elevation|LPE'}).Count){$score+=15}
    if(@($ResearchRecords|Where-Object {-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $_ 'CVE' ''))}).Count){$score+=10}
    if(@($ResearchRecords|Where-Object {[string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $_ 'Url' '') -match '^https?://'}).Count){$score+=10}
    return [Math]::Min(100,$score)
}

function Get-DRSNvdCve {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$Cve,
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$CacheRoot,
        [switch]$NoNetwork
    )
    if($NoNetwork){return $null}
    $dir=Join-Path $CacheRoot 'exploit-intel\nvd'
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dir -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue|Out-Null
    $safe=$Cve -replace '[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]','_'
    $cache=Join-Path $dir "$safe.json"
    if(Test-Path $cache){
        try{
            $item=Get-Item $cache
            if($item.LastWriteTimeUtc -gt (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().AddHours(-24)){
                return (Get-Content $cache -Raw|ConvertFrom-Json -Depth 30)
            }
        }catch{}
    }
    try{
        $uri="https://services.nvd.nist.gov/rest/json/cves/2.0?cveId=$([uri]::EscapeDataString($Cve))"
        $data=Invoke-RestMethod -Uri $uri -TimeoutSec 12 -ErrorAction Stop
        $data|ConvertTo-Json -Depth 30|Set-Content $cache -Encoding UTF8
        return $data
    }catch{
        return $null
    }
}

function Add-DRSExploitIntel {
    param(
        [AllowNull()][AllowEmptyCollection()][array]$Findings=@(),
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$DatabasePath,
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$CacheRoot,
        [bool]$EnableOnline=$false,
        [switch]$NoNetwork
    )

    $db=@(Import-DRSExploitDatabase -Path $DatabasePath)
    $byDriver=@{}
    $byCve=@{}
    foreach($entry in $db){
        if((Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'CVE' '')){$byCve[(Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'CVE' '')]=$entry}
        foreach($driver in @((Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'AffectedDrivers' @()))){
            $k=([string]$driver).ToLowerInvariant()
            if(-not $byDriver.ContainsKey($k)){$byDriver[$k]=[System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()}
            $byDriver[$k].Add($entry)|Out-Null
        }
    }

    foreach($f in @($Findings)){
        $intelRecords=[System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
        $name=([string]$f.FileName).ToLowerInvariant()

        if($byDriver.ContainsKey($name)){
            foreach($entry in $byDriver[$name]){$intelRecords.Add($entry)|Out-Null}
        }

        foreach($cve in @(Get-DRSExistingCves $f)){
            if($byCve.ContainsKey($cve)){
                $entry=$byCve[$cve]
                if(-not @($intelRecords|Where-Object CVE -eq (Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'CVE' '')).Count){$intelRecords.Add($entry)|Out-Null}
            }
        }

        $cves=[System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
        $refs=[System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
        $types=[System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
        $maxCvss=0.0
        $sourceVerified=$false

        foreach($entry in $intelRecords){
            $entryCve=[string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'CVE' '')
            $entryUrl=[string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'Url' '')
            $entryType=[string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'Type' '')
            $entryCvss=[double](Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'CVSS' 0)
            $entryVerified=[bool](Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'SourceVerifiedFlag' $false)
            if($entryCve -and -not $cves.Contains($entryCve)){$cves.Add($entryCve)}
            if($entryUrl -and -not $refs.Contains($entryUrl)){$refs.Add($entryUrl)}
            if($entryType -and -not $types.Contains($entryType)){$types.Add($entryType)}
            if($entryCvss -gt $maxCvss){$maxCvss=$entryCvss}
            if($entryVerified){$sourceVerified=$true}
        }

        foreach($existing in @(Get-DRSExistingCves $f)){
            if(-not $cves.Contains($existing)){$cves.Add($existing)}
        }

        $nvdRecords=[System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
        if($EnableOnline -and -not $NoNetwork){
            foreach($cve in $cves){
                $nvd=Get-DRSNvdCve -Cve $cve -CacheRoot $CacheRoot
                if($nvd){
                    foreach($wrapper in @($nvd.vulnerabilities)){
                        if($wrapper.cve){
                            $nvdRecords.Add([pscustomobject]@{
                                CVE=[string]$wrapper.cve.id
                                Status=[string]$wrapper.cve.vulnStatus
                                Published=[string]$wrapper.cve.published
                                Modified=[string]$wrapper.cve.lastModified
                            })|Out-Null
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        $researchScore=Get-DRSResearchMaturityScore -ResearchRecords $intelRecords.ToArray()
        $hasPublicRef=@($intelRecords|Where-Object {$_.Url -match '^https?://'}).Count -gt 0

        [pscustomobject]@{
            FileName=[string]$f.FileName
            Path=[string]$f.Path
            SHA256=[string]$f.SHA256
            MatchCount=$intelRecords.Count
            Matches=$intelRecords.ToArray()
            CVEs=$cves.ToArray()
            References=$refs.ToArray()
            ExploitTypes=$types.ToArray()
            MaxCVSS=[Math]::Round($maxCvss,1)
            HasPublicPoCReference=$hasPublicRef
            SourceVerifiedFlag=$sourceVerified
            ResearchMaturityScore=$researchScore
            OnlineNvd=$nvdRecords.ToArray()
            Summary=$(if($intelRecords.Count){"$($intelRecords.Count) local exploit-intel mapping(s); research maturity $researchScore/100"}else{'No local exploit-intel mapping'})
        }
    }
}


function Get-DRSExploitIntelInventoryLeads {
    param(
        [AllowNull()][AllowEmptyCollection()][array]$Inventory=@(),
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$DatabasePath
    )

    $db=@(Import-DRSExploitDatabase -Path $DatabasePath)
    $byDriver=@{}
    foreach($entry in $db){
        foreach($driver in @((Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'AffectedDrivers' @()))){
            $k=([string]$driver).ToLowerInvariant()
            if(-not $byDriver.ContainsKey($k)){$byDriver[$k]=[System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()}
            $byDriver[$k].Add($entry)|Out-Null
        }
    }

    $seen=@{}
    foreach($item in @($Inventory)){
        $name=([string]$item.FileName).ToLowerInvariant()
        if(-not $byDriver.ContainsKey($name)){continue}
        foreach($entry in $byDriver[$name]){
            $key=(([string]$item.Path).ToLowerInvariant() + '|' + [string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'CVE' ''))
            if($seen.ContainsKey($key)){continue}
            $seen[$key]=$true
            [pscustomobject]@{
                FileName=[string]$item.FileName
                Path=[string]$item.Path
                SHA256=[string]$item.SHA256
                Company=[string]$item.Company
                FileVersion=[string]$item.FileVersion
                State=[string]$item.State
                Running=[string]$item.Running
                CVE=[string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'CVE' '')
                Type=[string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'Type' '')
                CVSS=[double](Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'CVSS' 0)
                Description=[string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'Description' '')
                SourceVerifiedFlag=[bool](Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'SourceVerifiedFlag' $false)
                Reference=[string](Get-DRSExploitProperty $entry 'Url' '')
                Applicability='Research lead only: filename mapping matched; affected version/build is not established'
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`Agent/Modules/Exploitability.psm1`:

```psm1
Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0

function Get-DRSBool {
    param($Value)
    if($Value -is [bool]){ return $Value }
    return ([string]$Value -match '^(?i:true|1|yes)$')
}

function Get-DRSExploitabilityLevel {
    param([int]$Score,$Policy)
    $t=$Policy.Thresholds
    if($Score -ge [int]$t.Critical){return 'Critical'}
    if($Score -ge [int]$t.High){return 'High'}
    if($Score -ge [int]$t.Elevated){return 'Elevated'}
    if($Score -ge [int]$t.Guarded){return 'Guarded'}
    return 'Low'
}

function Get-DRSPrimitiveAssessment {
    param($Finding,$Policy)

    $primitive=[string]$Finding.PrimitiveClass
    $heur=[string]$Finding.HeuristicCapabilities
    $source='Unknown'
    $score=0
    $impact='Unknown'
    $privBase=0
    $evidence='No dangerous primitive was established by the current evidence.'

    if(-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($primitive) -and $primitive -notmatch '^(Unknown|See intelligence source)$'){
        foreach($rule in @($Policy.PrimitiveWeights)){
            if($primitive -match [regex]::Escape([string]$rule.Pattern)){
                $score=[int]$rule.Score
                $impact=[string]$rule.PrivilegeImpact
                $privBase=[int]$rule.PrivilegeBaseScore
                $source='Threat intelligence / curated profile'
                $evidence="Primitive classification from intelligence/profile: $primitive"
                break
            }
        }
        if($score -eq 0){
            $source='Threat intelligence / curated profile'
            $impact=$primitive
            $score=8
            $evidence="Non-generic primitive classification present: $primitive"
        }
    } elseif(-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($heur)){
        $source='Static heuristic'
        $score=6
        $impact='Potential privileged kernel capability; manual validation required'
        $privBase=10
        $evidence="Static heuristic capabilities: $heur"
    } elseif(-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string]$Finding.IoctlCandidates)){
        $source='Static heuristic'
        $score=4
        $impact='Unknown IOCTL attack surface; semantics not established'
        $privBase=5
        $evidence='Private IOCTL candidates were detected, but their semantics were not proven.'
    }

    [pscustomobject]@{
        Primitive=$(if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($primitive)){'Unknown'}else{$primitive})
        Source=$source
        Score=$score
        PrivilegeImpact=$impact
        PrivilegeBaseScore=$privBase
        Evidence=$evidence
    }
}

function Get-DRSReachabilityAssessment {
    param($Finding)

    $running=Get-DRSBool $Finding.Running
    $recent=Get-DRSBool $Finding.RecentLoad
    $broad=Get-DRSBool $Finding.BroadDeviceAcl
    $present=-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string]$Finding.PresentDevices)
    $names=-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string]$Finding.DeviceNames)

    if(-not $running -and -not $recent){
        return [pscustomobject]@{
            Level='Not currently active'
            LowPrivilegeAccess='Not established'
            Confidence='High'
            Reason='Driver is not currently running and no recent load was observed. A static device name does not prove current reachability.'
        }
    }
    if($broad){
        return [pscustomobject]@{
            Level='Broad device access indicated'
            LowPrivilegeAccess='Indicated'
            Confidence='High'
            Reason='A present device object was observed and the optional READ_CONTROL ACL probe indicated a broad allow principal.'
        }
    }
    if($present){
        return [pscustomobject]@{
            Level='Device present'
            LowPrivilegeAccess='Unknown'
            Confidence='Medium'
            Reason='A DOS device name is present, but low-privileged access was not established.'
        }
    }
    if($names){
        return [pscustomobject]@{
            Level='Static device reference only'
            LowPrivilegeAccess='Unknown'
            Confidence='Low'
            Reason='Device-name strings were found statically, but a corresponding live DOS device was not observed.'
        }
    }
    return [pscustomobject]@{
        Level='Loaded without observed user-mode interface'
        LowPrivilegeAccess='Unknown'
        Confidence='Low'
        Reason='The driver is/was loaded, but DriverRiskScout did not observe a device interface. Other communication paths may exist.'
    }
}

function Get-DRSVulnerabilityConfidence {
    param($Finding)

    $known=([string]$Finding.Classification -eq 'Known')
    $verified=([string]$Finding.Verified -match '^(?i:true|1|yes)$')
    $msExact=Get-DRSBool $Finding.MicrosoftBlocklistExact
    $msHint=Get-DRSBool $Finding.MicrosoftVersionHint
    $profileExact=([string]$Finding.ThreatProfileConfidence -match '(?i)Exact')
    $profileHeur=([string]$Finding.ThreatProfileConfidence -match '(?i)Heuristic')

    if($known -and $verified){return [pscustomobject]@{Level='Very High';Score=95;Reason='Exact known-driver intelligence plus verified source metadata.'}}
    if($known -or $msExact -or $profileExact){return [pscustomobject]@{Level='High';Score=85;Reason='Exact known/blocklist/profile evidence is present.'}}
    if($msHint){return [pscustomobject]@{Level='Medium';Score=60;Reason='Filename/version policy hint requires manual validation.'}}
    if($profileHeur -or [string]$Finding.Classification -eq 'Suspicious'){return [pscustomobject]@{Level='Low-Medium';Score=40;Reason='Heuristic evidence only; not a vulnerability confirmation.'}}
    return [pscustomobject]@{Level='Low';Score=10;Reason='No public exact vulnerability evidence was established.'}
}

function Add-DRSExploitabilityAssessment {
    param(
        [array]$Findings,
        $Protection,
        $AppControl,
        [string]$PolicyPath
    )

    $policy=Get-Content -LiteralPath $PolicyPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json -AsHashtable -Depth 20
    $w=$policy.Weights

    foreach($f in @($Findings)){
        $why=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
        $score=0
        $potential=0

        $known=([string]$f.Classification -eq 'Known')
        $verified=([string]$f.Verified -match '^(?i:true|1|yes)$')
        $msExact=Get-DRSBool $f.MicrosoftBlocklistExact
        $msHint=Get-DRSBool $f.MicrosoftVersionHint
        $profileExact=([string]$f.ThreatProfileConfidence -match '(?i)Exact')
        $profileHeur=([string]$f.ThreatProfileConfidence -match '(?i)Heuristic')
        $running=Get-DRSBool $f.Running
        $recent=Get-DRSBool $f.RecentLoad
        $broad=Get-DRSBool $f.BroadDeviceAcl
        $present=-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string]$f.PresentDevices)
        $deviceNames=-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string]$f.DeviceNames)
        $ioctl=-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string]$f.IoctlCandidates)
        $imports=-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string]$f.DangerousImports)

        if($known){
            $score+=[int]$w.ExactKnownHash;$potential+=[int]$w.ExactKnownHash
            $why.Add("+$($w.ExactKnownHash) exact known/blocklist intelligence classification")
        } elseif([string]$f.Classification -eq 'Suspicious'){
            $score+=[int]$w.StaticSuspicious;$potential+=[int]$w.StaticSuspicious
            $why.Add("+$($w.StaticSuspicious) suspicious static/heuristic classification")
        }
        if($verified){
            $score+=[int]$w.VerifiedThreatIntel;$potential+=[int]$w.VerifiedThreatIntel
            $why.Add("+$($w.VerifiedThreatIntel) threat-intelligence source marked verified")
        }
        if($msExact){
            $score+=[int]$w.MicrosoftBlocklistExact;$potential+=[int]$w.MicrosoftBlocklistExact
            $why.Add("+$($w.MicrosoftBlocklistExact) exact Microsoft vulnerable-driver blocklist match")
        } elseif($msHint){
            $score+=[int]$w.MicrosoftVersionHint;$potential+=[int]$w.MicrosoftVersionHint
            $why.Add("+$($w.MicrosoftVersionHint) Microsoft filename/version policy hint")
        }
        if($profileExact -and -not $known){
            $score+=[int]$w.ExactThreatProfile;$potential+=[int]$w.ExactThreatProfile
            $why.Add("+$($w.ExactThreatProfile) exact curated research-profile match")
        } elseif($profileHeur){
            $score+=[int]$w.HeuristicThreatProfile;$potential+=[int]$w.HeuristicThreatProfile
            $why.Add("+$($w.HeuristicThreatProfile) heuristic research-profile similarity")
        }

        $primitive=Get-DRSPrimitiveAssessment -Finding $f -Policy $policy
        if([int]$primitive.Score -gt 0){
            $score+=[int]$primitive.Score;$potential+=[int]$primitive.Score
            $why.Add("+$($primitive.Score) primitive evidence: $($primitive.Primitive)")
        }

        if($running){
            $score+=[int]$w.Running
            $why.Add("+$($w.Running) currently running")
        } elseif($recent){
            $score+=[int]$w.RecentLoad
            $why.Add("+$($w.RecentLoad) recently loaded")
        } elseif([string]$f.State -eq 'Registered/Stopped'){
            $score+=[int]$w.RegisteredStopped
            $why.Add("+$($w.RegisteredStopped) registered kernel service, currently stopped")
        }

        # Potential-if-loaded assumes runtime only; it does not invent a permissive ACL.
        $potential+=[int]$w.Running

        if($broad){
            $score+=[int]$w.BroadDeviceAcl;$potential+=[int]$w.BroadDeviceAcl
            $why.Add("+$($w.BroadDeviceAcl) broad device ACL indicated by optional probe")
        } elseif($present){
            $score+=[int]$w.PresentDevice;$potential+=[int]$w.PresentDevice
            $why.Add("+$($w.PresentDevice) live DOS device observed; low-privilege ACL unknown")
        } elseif($deviceNames){
            $score+=[int]$w.DeviceNameStaticOnly;$potential+=[int]$w.DeviceNameStaticOnly
            $why.Add("+$($w.DeviceNameStaticOnly) static device-name reference only")
        }

        if($ioctl){
            $score+=[int]$w.IoctlCandidates;$potential+=[int]$w.IoctlCandidates
            $why.Add("+$($w.IoctlCandidates) private IOCTL candidates; semantics unproven")
        }
        if($imports){
            $score+=[int]$w.DangerousImports;$potential+=[int]$w.DangerousImports
            $why.Add("+$($w.DangerousImports) sensitive kernel imports; legitimate use remains possible")
        }

        $policyAssessment=[string]$f.PolicyAssessment
        if(Get-DRSBool $Protection.HVCIRegistryEnabled){
            $score=[Math]::Max(0,$score-[int]$w.HVCIEnabledReduction)
            $why.Add("-$($w.HVCIEnabledReduction) HVCI configured/enabled")
        }
        if(Get-DRSBool $Protection.VulnerableDriverBlocklistEnabled){
            $score=[Math]::Max(0,$score-[int]$w.DriverBlocklistEnabledReduction)
            $why.Add("-$($w.DriverBlocklistEnabledReduction) vulnerable-driver blocklist enabled")
        }
        if($policyAssessment -eq 'Likely constrained by policy'){
            $score=[Math]::Max(0,$score-[int]$w.PolicyLikelyConstrainedReduction)
            $why.Add("-$($w.PolicyLikelyConstrainedReduction) local policy assessment indicates likely constraint")
        }

        $score=[Math]::Min(100,[int]$score)
        $potential=[Math]::Min(100,[int]$potential)

        $reach=Get-DRSReachabilityAssessment -Finding $f
        $vuln=Get-DRSVulnerabilityConfidence -Finding $f

        $priv=[int]$primitive.PrivilegeBaseScore
        if($known){$priv+=20}
        if($running){$priv+=10}
        if($present){$priv+=5}
        if($broad){$priv+=15}
        $priv=[Math]::Min(100,$priv)

        $peAssessment='Not demonstrated by current evidence'
        if($primitive.Primitive -eq 'Privilege escalation' -and $known){
            $peAssessment='Threat intelligence indicates a privilege-escalation primitive'
        } elseif($primitive.Primitive -eq 'Kernel/physical memory access' -and $known){
            $peAssessment='Potential privilege escalation via known kernel/physical-memory primitive'
        } elseif($primitive.Source -eq 'Static heuristic'){
            $peAssessment='Heuristic only - privilege escalation not established'
        } elseif($known -and $primitive.Primitive -match '^(Unknown|See intelligence source)$'){
            $peAssessment='Known vulnerable sample, but privilege-escalation primitive is not established by current metadata'
        }

        [pscustomobject]@{
            FileName=[string]$f.FileName
            Path=[string]$f.Path
            SHA256=[string]$f.SHA256
            CurrentExploitabilityScore=$score
            CurrentExploitabilityLevel=(Get-DRSExploitabilityLevel $score $policy)
            PotentialIfLoadedScore=$potential
            PotentialIfLoadedLevel=(Get-DRSExploitabilityLevel $potential $policy)
            VulnerabilityConfidence=$vuln.Level
            VulnerabilityConfidenceScore=$vuln.Score
            VulnerabilityReason=$vuln.Reason
            Reachability=$reach.Level
            LowPrivilegeAccess=$reach.LowPrivilegeAccess
            ReachabilityConfidence=$reach.Confidence
            ReachabilityReason=$reach.Reason
            Primitive=$primitive.Primitive
            PrimitiveEvidenceSource=$primitive.Source
            PrimitiveEvidence=$primitive.Evidence
            PrivilegeImpact=$primitive.PrivilegeImpact
            PrivilegeEscalationScore=$priv
            PrivilegeEscalationAssessment=$peAssessment
            ExploitabilityWhy=($why -join '; ')
        }
    }
}
```
