`Agent/Modules/Investigation.psm1`:

```psm1
Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0

function ConvertTo-DRSUriComponent {
    param([string]$Value)
    return [System.Uri]::EscapeDataString([string]$Value)
}

function ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml {
    param($Value)
    if($null -eq $Value){return ''}
    return [System.Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode([string]$Value)
}

function Get-DRSInvestigationProperty {
    param($Object,[string]$Name,$Default=$null)
    if($null -eq $Object){return $Default}
    $p=$Object.PSObject.Properties[$Name]
    if($null -eq $p){return $Default}
    return $p.Value
}

function Get-DRSCves {
    param($Finding)
    $out=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
    foreach($raw in @(
        (Get-DRSInvestigationProperty $Finding 'CVEs' ''),
        (Get-DRSInvestigationProperty $Finding 'CVEList' @()),
        (Get-DRSInvestigationProperty $Finding 'ExploitIntelCves' @())
    )){
        $parts=if($raw -is [System.Collections.IEnumerable] -and $raw -isnot [string]){@($raw)}else{([string]$raw -split '[,;|\s]+')}
        foreach($piece in $parts){
            $v=([string]$piece).Trim().ToUpperInvariant()
            if($v -match '^CVE-\d{4}-\d{4,}$' -and -not $out.Contains($v)){$out.Add($v)}
        }
    }
    return $out.ToArray()
}

function Get-DRSMatchEvidence {
    param($Finding)
    $items=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]

    if(-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string]$Finding.LOLDriversEntryId)){
        $items.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Type='Exact SHA256'
            Source='LOLDrivers'
            Confidence='Very High'
            Evidence=("Exact local SHA256 associated with LOLDrivers entry {0}" -f [string]$Finding.LOLDriversEntryId)
        })|Out-Null
    }
    if([bool]$Finding.MicrosoftBlocklistExact){
        $items.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Type='Exact SHA256'
            Source='Microsoft vulnerable driver block rules'
            Confidence='Very High'
            Evidence='Exact SHA256 matched the locally loaded Microsoft blocklist policy.'
        })|Out-Null
    } elseif([bool]$Finding.MicrosoftVersionHint){
        $items.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Type='Filename/version hint'
            Source='Microsoft vulnerable driver block rules'
            Confidence='Medium'
            Evidence='Filename/version policy hint matched. This is not an exact-hash confirmation.'
        })|Out-Null
    }
    if([string]$Finding.ThreatProfileConfidence -match '(?i)Exact'){
        $items.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Type='Exact SHA256'
            Source='Bundled/curated BYOVD research profile'
            Confidence='High'
            Evidence=([string]$Finding.ThreatProfileProjects)
        })|Out-Null
    } elseif([string]$Finding.ThreatProfileConfidence -match '(?i)Heuristic'){
        $items.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Type='Heuristic similarity'
            Source='Bundled/curated BYOVD research profile'
            Confidence='Low-Medium'
            Evidence=([string]$Finding.ThreatProfileProjects)
        })|Out-Null
    }
    foreach($x in @((Get-DRSInvestigationProperty $Finding 'ExploitIntelMatches' @()))){
        $confidence=if([bool]$x.SourceVerifiedFlag){'Source marks verified'}else{'Source-provided'}
        $items.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Type='Affected-driver research mapping'
            Source='Bundled exploit-db.json'
            Confidence=$confidence
            Evidence=("CVE={0}; Type={1}; CVSS={2}; filename mapping matched; affected version/build not established by supplied database" -f [string]$x.CVE,[string]$x.Type,[string]$x.CVSS)
        })|Out-Null
    }

    if($items.Count -eq 0){
        $items.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Type='Heuristic/local evidence'
            Source='DriverRiskScout'
            Confidence='Low'
            Evidence='No exact public-hash source was established by the current scan.'
        })|Out-Null
    }
    return $items.ToArray()
}

function Get-DRSSourceLinks {
    param($Finding)

    $links=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
    $sha=[string]$Finding.SHA256
    $name=[string]$Finding.FileName
    $entry=[string]$Finding.LOLDriversEntryId
    $cves=@(Get-DRSCves $Finding)

    if(-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($entry)){
        $links.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Name='LOLDrivers'
            Kind='Threat intelligence'
            Trust='Driver-specific community intelligence'
            Url=("https://www.loldrivers.io/drivers/{0}/" -f (ConvertTo-DRSUriComponent $entry))
            Query='Exact LOLDrivers entry'
        })|Out-Null
    }

    if(-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($sha)){
        $links.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Name='GitHub - exact SHA256 search'
            Kind='Research / code search'
            Trust='Secondary research source'
            Url=("https://github.com/search?q={0}&type=code" -f (ConvertTo-DRSUriComponent ('"' + $sha + '"')))
            Query=$sha
        })|Out-Null
    }

    if(-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($name)){
        $links.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Name='GitHub - driver filename search'
            Kind='Research / code search'
            Trust='Secondary research source'
            Url=("https://github.com/search?q={0}&type=code" -f (ConvertTo-DRSUriComponent ('"' + $name + '"')))
            Query=$name
        })|Out-Null
        $links.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Name='GitHub - repositories'
            Kind='Research repositories'
            Trust='Secondary research source'
            Url=("https://github.com/search?q={0}&type=repositories" -f (ConvertTo-DRSUriComponent $name))
            Query=$name
        })|Out-Null
    }

    foreach($cve in $cves){
        $q=ConvertTo-DRSUriComponent $cve
        $links.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Name=("NVD - {0}" -f $cve)
            Kind='Vulnerability database'
            Trust='Authoritative enrichment'
            Url=("https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/{0}" -f $q)
            Query=$cve
        })|Out-Null
        $links.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Name=("CVE.org - {0}" -f $cve)
            Kind='CVE record'
            Trust='Primary CVE record'
            Url=("https://www.cve.org/CVERecord?id={0}" -f $q)
            Query=$cve
        })|Out-Null
        $links.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Name=("GitHub Advisory Database - {0}" -f $cve)
            Kind='Security advisory'
            Trust='Secondary advisory database'
            Url=("https://github.com/advisories?query={0}" -f $q)
            Query=$cve
        })|Out-Null
        $links.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Name=("GitHub - {0} research" -f $cve)
            Kind='Research search'
            Trust='Secondary research source'
            Url=("https://github.com/search?q={0}&type=repositories" -f $q)
            Query=$cve
        })|Out-Null
    }

    foreach($x in @((Get-DRSInvestigationProperty $Finding 'ExploitIntelMatches' @()))){
        $url=[string]$x.Url
        if($url -match '^https?://'){
            $links.Add([pscustomobject]@{
                Name=$(if([string]$x.CVE){"Research reference - $([string]$x.CVE)"}else{'Research reference'})
                Kind=$(if([string]$x.Type){[string]$x.Type}else{'Exploit research'})
                Trust='User-supplied research URL; not independently verified by DriverRiskScout'
                Url=$url
                Query=([string]$x.CVE)
            })|Out-Null
        }
    }

    $links.Add([pscustomobject]@{
        Name='CISA Known Exploited Vulnerabilities'
        Kind='Exploited-in-the-wild catalog'
        Trust='Authoritative prioritization source'
        Url='https://www.cisa.gov/known-exploited-vulnerabilities-catalog'
        Query=($cves -join ',')
    })|Out-Null

    $links.Add([pscustomobject]@{
        Name='Microsoft vulnerable driver block rules'
        Kind='Platform protection'
        Trust='Primary vendor/platform source'
        Url='https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/security/application-security/application-control/app-control-for-business/design/microsoft-recommended-driver-block-rules'
        Query=$name
    })|Out-Null

    # Preserve URLs supplied by upstream intelligence, normally vendor or research advisories.
    foreach($u in ([string]$Finding.AdvisoryResources -split '\s*\|\s*')){
        $url=$u.Trim()
        if($url -match '^https?://'){
            $host=''
            try{$host=([uri]$url).Host}catch{}
            $links.Add([pscustomobject]@{
                Name=$(if($host){("Advisory - " + $host)}else{'Advisory resource'})
                Kind='Upstream advisory'
                Trust=$(if($host -match 'microsoft|asus|intel|amd|nvidia|dell|hp|lenovo|cve\.org|nist\.gov|cisa\.gov'){'Primary/authoritative source'}else{'Referenced research/advisory'})
                Url=$url
                Query=$name
            })|Out-Null
        }
    }

    # Stable de-duplication by URL.
    $seen=@{}
    return @($links | Where-Object {
        $k=[string]$_.Url
        if($seen.ContainsKey($k)){$false}else{$seen[$k]=$true;$true}
    })
}

function Get-DRSSafeValidationCommands {
    param($Finding)

    $commands=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
    $path=[string]$Finding.Path
    $pathPs=$path.Replace("'","''")
    $leaf=[string]$Finding.FileName
    $leafPs=$leaf.Replace("'","''")

    $commands.Add([pscustomobject]@{
        Title='Datei vorhanden?'
        Purpose='Verifiziert nur die lokale Existenz der bereits gefundenen Datei.'
        Command=("Test-Path -LiteralPath '{0}'" -f $pathPs)
    })|Out-Null

    $commands.Add([pscustomobject]@{
        Title='SHA256 unabhängig bestätigen'
        Purpose='Prüft, ob die aktuell vorhandene Datei noch exakt denselben Hash besitzt.'
        Command=("Get-FileHash -LiteralPath '{0}' -Algorithm SHA256 | Format-List *" -f $pathPs)
    })|Out-Null

    $commands.Add([pscustomobject]@{
        Title='Authenticode-Signatur prüfen'
        Purpose='Zeigt Signaturstatus, Signer und Zertifikatsinformationen.'
        Command=("Get-AuthenticodeSignature -LiteralPath '{0}' | Format-List *" -f $pathPs)
    })|Out-Null

    $commands.Add([pscustomobject]@{
        Title='Kernel-Service nach Dateiname suchen'
        Purpose='Read-only Abfrage; startet oder erstellt keinen Dienst.'
        Command=("Get-CimInstance Win32_SystemDriver | Where-Object {{ `$_.PathName -match [regex]::Escape('{0}') }} | Format-List Name,DisplayName,State,StartMode,PathName" -f $leafPs)
    })|Out-Null

    foreach($service in ([string]$Finding.DriverServices -split ',')){
        $svc=$service.Trim()
        if($svc -match '^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{1,256}$'){
            $commands.Add([pscustomobject]@{
                Title=("Service-Status: {0}" -f $svc)
                Purpose='Service Control Manager read-only query.'
                Command=("sc.exe query ""{0}""" -f $svc)
            })|Out-Null
            $commands.Add([pscustomobject]@{
                Title=("Service-Konfiguration: {0}" -f $svc)
                Purpose='Zeigt Typ, Startmodus und Binary Path; verändert nichts.'
                Command=("sc.exe qc ""{0}""" -f $svc)
            })|Out-Null
        }
    }

    if(-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string]$Finding.PresentDevices)){
        $commands.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Title='Vom Scan beobachtete Device-Namen'
            Purpose='Informationsausgabe aus dem Scan; keine IOCTL-Kommunikation.'
            Command=("Write-Output '{0}'" -f ([string]$Finding.PresentDevices).Replace("'","''"))
        })|Out-Null
    }

    return $commands.ToArray()
}

function Get-DRSInvestigationConclusion {
    param($Finding)
    if([string]$Finding.Classification -eq 'Known'){
        if([bool]$Finding.Running -and [int]$Finding.CurrentExploitabilityScore -ge 70){
            return 'Exact/known vulnerability evidence is present and the driver is active. Current exposure is high-priority; validate vendor remediation and low-privilege reachability without executing a PoC.'
        }
        if(-not [bool]$Finding.Running){
            return 'Known vulnerability evidence is present, but the driver is not currently running. Potential-if-loaded risk is tracked separately; current privilege escalation is not proven.'
        }
        return 'Known vulnerability evidence is present. Exploitability still depends on reachability, primitive, OS/policy context and the affected version/hash evidence.'
    }
    if([string]$Finding.Classification -eq 'Suspicious'){
        return 'Heuristic indicators are present, but no exact public vulnerability match was established. Treat this as a manual review lead, not a vulnerability confirmation.'
    }
    return 'No exact vulnerability evidence was established for this inventory item.'
}

function New-DRSInvestigationRecords {
    param([array]$Findings,$Protection,$AppControl)

    foreach($f in @($Findings)){
        $match=@(Get-DRSMatchEvidence $f)
        $sources=@(Get-DRSSourceLinks $f)
        $commands=@(Get-DRSSafeValidationCommands $f)
        $cves=@(Get-DRSCves $f)

        [pscustomobject]@{
            FileName=[string]$f.FileName
            Path=[string]$f.Path
            SHA256=[string]$f.SHA256
            MatchEvidence=$match
            Sources=$sources
            SafeValidationCommands=$commands
            CVEList=$cves
            Conclusion=(Get-DRSInvestigationConclusion $f)
            SafetyNote='Read-only validation only. Do not load/start a known vulnerable driver or execute exploit/PoC code on production systems.'
        }
    }
}

function New-DRSInvestigationPages {
    param(
        [array]$Findings,
        [string]$Directory,
        [string]$ToolVersion,
        $Protection,
        $AppControl
    )

    if(-not (Test-Path $Directory)){New-Item -ItemType Directory $Directory -Force|Out-Null}

    foreach($f in @($Findings)){
        $hash=[string]$f.SHA256
        $short=$(if($hash.Length -ge 12){$hash.Substring(0,12)}else{$hash})
        $safeName=([regex]::Replace([string]$f.FileName,'[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]','_'))
        $fileName=("{0}-{1}.html" -f $safeName,$short)
        $relative=("investigations/{0}" -f $fileName)
        $out=Join-Path $Directory $fileName

        $evidenceRows=''
        foreach($e in @($f.MatchEvidence)){
            $evidenceRows+="<tr><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $e.Type)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $e.Source)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $e.Confidence)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $e.Evidence)</td></tr>"
        }

        $sourceRows=''
        foreach($s in @($f.InvestigationSources)){
            $u=ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $s.Url
            $sourceRows+="<tr><td><a target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' href='$u'>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $s.Name)</a></td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $s.Kind)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $s.Trust)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $s.Query)</td></tr>"
        }

        $exploitRows=''
        foreach($x in @((Get-DRSInvestigationProperty $f 'ExploitIntelMatches' @()))){
            $u=ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $x.Url
            $link=$(if($u){"<a target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' href='$u'>open research</a>"}else{''})
            $exploitRows+="<tr><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $x.CVE)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $x.Type)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $x.CVSS)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $x.Description)</td><td>$($x.SourceVerifiedFlag)</td><td>$link</td></tr>"
        }

        $commandHtml=''
        foreach($c in @($f.SafeValidationCommands)){
            $commandHtml+="<section class='cmd'><h3>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $c.Title)</h3><p>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $c.Purpose)</p><pre>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $c.Command)</pre></section>"
        }

        $html=@"
<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8">
<title>DriverRiskScout $ToolVersion - $(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.FileName)</title>
<style>
body{font-family:Segoe UI,Arial,sans-serif;margin:28px;background:#f4f6f9;color:#1f2937}
a{color:#175cd3}.muted{color:#667085}.cards{display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:16px 0}
.card{background:#fff;border-radius:10px;padding:15px 20px;box-shadow:0 1px 5px #0002;min-width:155px}
.card b{font-size:26px;display:block}.warnbox{background:#fff7ed;border:1px solid #fed7aa;padding:14px;border-radius:8px}
table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;background:#fff;margin:12px 0 28px}
th,td{padding:9px;border-bottom:1px solid #e5e7eb;text-align:left;font-size:13px;vertical-align:top}
th{background:#111827;color:white}.kv{display:grid;grid-template-columns:210px 1fr;gap:6px 16px;background:#fff;padding:16px;border-radius:8px}
pre{background:#111827;color:#f9fafb;padding:14px;border-radius:8px;overflow:auto;white-space:pre-wrap}
.cmd{background:#fff;padding:14px 16px;margin:12px 0;border-radius:8px}.small{font-size:12px;word-break:break-word}
</style></head><body>
<p><a href="../DriverRiskScout-v2-report.html">← Zurück zum Host-Report</a></p>
<h1>Vulnerability Investigation: $(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.FileName)</h1>
<p class="muted">DriverRiskScout $ToolVersion — evidence-driven, read-only investigation</p>

<div class="cards">
<div class="card"><b>$($f.CurrentExploitabilityScore)</b>Current exploitability<br>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.CurrentExploitabilityLevel)</div>
<div class="card"><b>$($f.PotentialIfLoadedScore)</b>Potential if loaded<br>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.PotentialIfLoadedLevel)</div>
<div class="card"><b>$($f.PrivilegeEscalationScore)</b>Privilege escalation score</div>
<div class="card"><b>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.VulnerabilityConfidence)</b>Vulnerability confidence</div>
<div class="card"><b>$((Get-DRSInvestigationProperty $f 'ResearchMaturityScore' 0))</b>Research maturity</div>
<div class="card"><b>$(@((Get-DRSInvestigationProperty $f 'ExploitIntelReferences' @())).Count)</b>Public research refs</div>
</div>

<div class="warnbox"><b>Safety boundary:</b> $(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.InvestigationSafetyNote)</div>

<h2>Local evidence</h2>
<div class="kv">
<div>Path</div><div class="small">$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.Path)</div>
<div>SHA256</div><div class="small"><code>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.SHA256)</code></div>
<div>Vendor / Version</div><div>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.Company) / $(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.FileVersion)</div>
<div>Signature</div><div>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.SignatureStatus) — $(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.Signer)</div>
<div>State / Running</div><div>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.State) / $($f.Running)</div>
<div>Driver service(s)</div><div>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.DriverServices)</div>
<div>Reachability</div><div>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.Reachability) — low privilege: $(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.LowPrivilegeAccess)</div>
<div>Device names</div><div>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.PresentDevices)</div>
<div>Broad device ACL</div><div>$($f.BroadDeviceAcl)</div>
<div>Primitive</div><div>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.Primitive)</div>
<div>Privilege assessment</div><div>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.PrivilegeEscalationAssessment)</div>
<div>CVE(s)</div><div>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml ((@(Get-DRSCves $f)) -join ', '))</div>
<div>Exploit intel types</div><div>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml ((@((Get-DRSInvestigationProperty $f 'ExploitIntelTypes' @()))) -join ', '))</div>
<div>Exploit intel max CVSS</div><div>$((Get-DRSInvestigationProperty $f 'ExploitIntelMaxCVSS' 0))</div>
<div>Research maturity</div><div>$((Get-DRSInvestigationProperty $f 'ResearchMaturityScore' 0)) / 100</div>
<div>HVCI</div><div>$($Protection.HVCIRegistryEnabled)</div>
<div>Vulnerable-driver blocklist</div><div>$($Protection.VulnerableDriverBlocklistEnabled)</div>
<div>App Control policies / enforced</div><div>$($AppControl.PolicyCount) / $($AppControl.EnforcedCount)</div>
</div>

<h2>Evidence chain</h2>
<table><tr><th>Match type</th><th>Source</th><th>Confidence</th><th>Evidence</th></tr>$evidenceRows</table>

<h2>Bundled exploit intelligence</h2>
<p class="muted">These records come from the supplied exploit-db.json. The source's Verified flag is displayed as source metadata; DriverRiskScout does not independently validate or execute the referenced PoC.</p>
<table><tr><th>CVE</th><th>Type</th><th>CVSS</th><th>Description</th><th>Source Verified flag</th><th>Reference</th></tr>$exploitRows</table>

<h2>External sources</h2>
<p class="muted">GitHub results are research leads. A repository or PoC mention does not itself prove that this endpoint is exploitable.</p>
<table><tr><th>Source</th><th>Type</th><th>Trust / purpose</th><th>Query</th></tr>$sourceRows</table>

<h2>Lab validation telemetry</h2>
<div class="kv">
<div>Validation readiness</div><div>$($f.LabValidationReadinessScore) / 100</div>
<div>Runtime status</div><div>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.RuntimeStatus)</div>
<div>Registered service state</div><div>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.RegisteredServiceState)</div>
<div>Code Integrity matches</div><div>$($f.MatchingCodeIntegrityEvents)</div>
<div>Driver install/service matches</div><div>$($f.MatchingDriverInstallEvents)</div>
<div>Sysmon driver-load matches</div><div>$($f.MatchingSysmonDriverLoadEvents)</div>
<div>Block evidence</div><div>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.BlockEvidence) ($(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.BlockEvidenceConfidence))</div>
</div>
<p class="muted">$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.SafeConclusion)</p>

<h2>Assessment</h2>
<p>$(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.InvestigationConclusion)</p>
<p><b>Why:</b> $(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.ExploitabilityWhy)</p>
<p><b>Recommended action:</b> $(ConvertTo-DRSInvestigationHtml $f.RecommendedAction)</p>

<h2>Safe validation commands</h2>
<p class="muted">Read-only commands only. They do not create/start a driver service and do not send IOCTLs.</p>
$commandHtml

</body></html>
"@
        Set-Content -LiteralPath $out -Value $html -Encoding UTF8
        $f|Add-Member -NotePropertyName InvestigationPage -NotePropertyValue $relative -Force
    }
}
```

`Agent/Modules/LabValidation.psm1`:

```psm1
Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0

function Get-DRSLabEventText {
    param($Event)
    $parts=@()
    foreach($name in @('Message','ImageLoaded','ServiceName','ImagePath','Driver','Path','ProviderName','Id')){
        try{
            $v=$Event.$name
            if($null -ne $v -and -not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string]$v)){$parts += [string]$v}
        }catch{}
    }
    return ($parts -join ' ')
}

function Get-DRSLabValidationAssessment {
    param(
        $Finding,
        $Protection,
        $AppControl,
        [array]$CodeIntegrityEvents,
        [array]$DriverInstallEvents,
        [array]$SysmonDriverEvents
    )

    $path=[string]$Finding.Path
    $name=[string]$Finding.FileName
    $services=@(([string]$Finding.DriverServices -split ',')|ForEach-Object{$_.Trim()}|Where-Object{$_})

    $matchPattern=[regex]::Escape($name)
    $ci=@($CodeIntegrityEvents|Where-Object{(Get-DRSLabEventText $_) -match $matchPattern})
    $install=@($DriverInstallEvents|Where-Object{(Get-DRSLabEventText $_) -match $matchPattern})
    $sysmon=@($SysmonDriverEvents|Where-Object{(Get-DRSLabEventText $_) -match $matchPattern})

    $isAdmin=$false
    try{
        $isAdmin=[Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal]::new(
            [Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent()
        ).IsInRole([Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)
    }catch{}

    $serviceState='Not observed'
    $serviceDetails=@()
    try{
        $drivers=@(Get-CimInstance Win32_SystemDriver -ErrorAction Stop|Where-Object{
            ([string]$_.PathName -match $matchPattern) -or ($services -contains [string]$_.Name)
        })
        if($drivers.Count){
            $serviceState=(@($drivers|ForEach-Object{"$($_.Name):$($_.State)/$($_.StartMode)"}) -join '; ')
            $serviceDetails=@($drivers|Select-Object Name,DisplayName,State,StartMode,PathName)
        }
    }catch{}

    $blockEvidence='Not observed'
    $blockConfidence='Low'
    if($ci.Count -gt 0){
        $blockEvidence='Code Integrity telemetry references this driver'
        $blockConfidence='Medium'
    }
    if([bool]$Finding.MicrosoftBlocklistExact -and [bool]$Protection.VulnerableDriverBlocklistEnabled){
        $blockEvidence='Exact Microsoft blocklist evidence plus local blocklist enabled'
        $blockConfidence='High'
    }

    $readiness=0
    $why=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
    if([string]$Finding.Classification -eq 'Known'){$readiness+=30;$why.Add('+30 exact/known vulnerability evidence')}
    if([bool]$Finding.Running){$readiness+=25;$why.Add('+25 driver already running')}
    elseif($serviceDetails.Count){$readiness+=10;$why.Add('+10 matching driver service is registered')}
    if(-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string]$Finding.PresentDevices)){$readiness+=15;$why.Add('+15 live device observed')}
    if([bool]$Finding.BroadDeviceAcl){$readiness+=15;$why.Add('+15 broad device ACL indicated')}
    if(-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string]$Finding.Primitive) -and [string]$Finding.Primitive -notmatch '^(Unknown|See intelligence source)$'){
        $readiness+=15;$why.Add('+15 concrete primitive evidence')
    }
    $readiness=[Math]::Min(100,$readiness)

    $status=if([bool]$Finding.Running){'Active driver - investigate current exposure'}
            elseif($ci.Count -gt 0){'Prior/blocked load-related telemetry observed'}
            elseif($serviceDetails.Count){'Registered but not currently running'}
            else{'No active/registered runtime state observed'}

    [pscustomobject]@{
        FileName=$name
        Path=$path
        LabValidationReadinessScore=$readiness
        RuntimeStatus=$status
        IsAdministratorSession=$isAdmin
        RegisteredServiceState=$serviceState
        MatchingCodeIntegrityEvents=$ci.Count
        MatchingDriverInstallEvents=$install.Count
        MatchingSysmonDriverLoadEvents=$sysmon.Count
        BlockEvidence=$blockEvidence
        BlockEvidenceConfidence=$blockConfidence
        HVCIEnabled=[bool]$Protection.HVCIRegistryEnabled
        VulnerableDriverBlocklistEnabled=[bool]$Protection.VulnerableDriverBlocklistEnabled
        EnforcedAppControlPolicies=[int]$AppControl.EnforcedCount
        Why=($why -join '; ')
        SafeConclusion='DriverRiskScout does not create/start the service or send IOCTLs. Use the observed runtime/policy telemetry to decide whether a separate isolated lab reproduction is warranted.'
        ProhibitedActions='No automatic sc.exe create/start; no driver loading; no DeviceIoControl/IOCTL execution; no PoC download/build/run.'
    }
}

function New-DRSLabValidationRecords {
    param(
        [array]$Findings,
        $Protection,
        $AppControl,
        [array]$CodeIntegrityEvents,
        [array]$DriverInstallEvents,
        [array]$SysmonDriverEvents
    )
    foreach($f in @($Findings)){
        Get-DRSLabValidationAssessment -Finding $f -Protection $Protection -AppControl $AppControl `
          -CodeIntegrityEvents $CodeIntegrityEvents -DriverInstallEvents $DriverInstallEvents -SysmonDriverEvents $SysmonDriverEvents
    }
}
```

`Agent/Modules/Provenance.psm1`:

```psm1
Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0

function Get-DRSDriverProvenanceIndex {
    $index=@{}
    try{
        $rows=@(Get-CimInstance Win32_PnPSignedDriver -ErrorAction Stop |
          Select-Object DeviceName,DeviceID,DeviceClass,DriverName,DriverVersion,DriverProviderName,InfName,DriverDate,Manufacturer,IsSigned,Signer)
        foreach($r in $rows){
            $leaf=''
            try{$leaf=[IO.Path]::GetFileName([string]$r.DriverName)}catch{}
            if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($leaf)){continue}
            $k=$leaf.ToLowerInvariant()
            if(-not $index.ContainsKey($k)){$index[$k]=New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList}
            [void]$index[$k].Add($r)
        }
    }catch{}
    return $index
}

function Add-DRSProvenance {
    param([array]$Inventory,[hashtable]$Index)
    foreach($i in $Inventory){
        $name=[string]$i.FileName
        $matches=@()
        if($name -and $Index.ContainsKey($name.ToLowerInvariant())){$matches=@($Index[$name.ToLowerInvariant()])}
        [pscustomobject]@{
            Path=[string]$i.Path
            FileName=$name
            PnPMatchCount=$matches.Count
            DeviceNames=(@($matches|Select-Object -ExpandProperty DeviceName -Unique) -join ' | ')
            DeviceClasses=(@($matches|Select-Object -ExpandProperty DeviceClass -Unique) -join ',')
            Providers=(@($matches|Select-Object -ExpandProperty DriverProviderName -Unique) -join ',')
            Manufacturers=(@($matches|Select-Object -ExpandProperty Manufacturer -Unique) -join ',')
            InfNames=(@($matches|Select-Object -ExpandProperty InfName -Unique) -join ',')
            PnPDriverVersions=(@($matches|Select-Object -ExpandProperty DriverVersion -Unique) -join ',')
            DeviceIds=(@($matches|Select-Object -ExpandProperty DeviceID -Unique) -join ' | ')
            CurrentlyBound=($matches.Count -gt 0)
        }
    }
}
```

```psm1
Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0

function ConvertTo-DRSHtml {
    param($x)
    if($null -eq $x){return ''}
    return [System.Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode([string]$x)
}

function New-DRSV2HtmlReport {
    param($Report,[string]$Path)
    $spRows=''
    foreach($p in @($Report.security_products.products|Where-Object Active)){
        $spRows+="<tr><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $p.Product)</td><td>$($p.Confidence)</td><td>$($p.Active)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $p.Evidence)</td></tr>"
    }
    $fRows=''
    foreach($f in @($Report.findings|Sort-Object RemediationScore -Descending)){
        $driverCell=ConvertTo-DRSHtml $f.FileName
        if(-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string]$f.InvestigationPage)){
            $href=ConvertTo-DRSHtml $f.InvestigationPage
            $driverCell="<a href='$href'>$driverCell</a>"
        }
        $fRows+="<tr><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $f.Priority)</td><td>$($f.RemediationScore)</td><td>$driverCell</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $f.Classification)</td><td>$($f.Running)</td><td><b>$($f.CurrentExploitabilityScore)</b><br>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $f.CurrentExploitabilityLevel)</td><td>$($f.PotentialIfLoadedScore)<br>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $f.PotentialIfLoadedLevel)</td><td>$($f.PrivilegeEscalationScore)</td><td>$($f.ResearchMaturityScore)</td><td>$($f.ExploitIntelMatchCount)</td><td>$($f.ExploitIntelMaxCVSS)</td><td>$($f.HasPublicPoCReference)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $f.Reachability)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $f.Primitive)</td><td class='small'>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $f.PrivilegeEscalationAssessment)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $f.PolicyAssessment)</td><td class='small'>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $f.ExploitabilityWhy)</td></tr>"
    }
    $leadRows=''
    foreach($l in @($Report.exploit_intel.inventory_leads|Select-Object -First 100)){
        $leadRows+="<tr><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $l.FileName)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $l.FileVersion)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $l.CVE)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $l.Type)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $l.CVSS)</td><td class='small'>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $l.Applicability)</td></tr>"
    }
    $pcRows=''
    foreach($c in @($Report.changes.protection_changes)){
        $pcRows+="<tr><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $c.Protection)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $c.Old)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $c.New)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $c.Severity)</td></tr>"
    }
    $dcRows=''
    foreach($c in @($Report.changes.driver_changes|Select-Object -First 200)){
        $dcRows+="<tr><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $c.Type)</td><td>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $c.Path)</td><td class='small'>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $c.Old)</td><td class='small'>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $c.New)</td></tr>"
    }
    $html=@"
<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>DriverRiskScout $($Report.tool.version)</title>

.ok{color:#087f5b}.warn{color:#b54708}.bad{color:#b42318}
</style></head><body>

<div class="card"><div class="big">$($Report.statistics.files)</div>Driver files</div>
<div class="card"><div class="big">$(@($Report.security_products.products|Where-Object Active).Count)</div>Active security products</div>

<p>HVCI: <b>$($Report.protection.HVCIRegistryEnabled)</b> &nbsp; VBS: <b>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $Report.protection.VBSRuntimeStatus)</b> &nbsp; Driver blocklist: <b>$($Report.protection.VulnerableDriverBlocklistEnabled)</b> &nbsp; Secure Boot: <b>$(ConvertTo-DRSHtml $Report.protection.SecureBoot)</b></p>
```

```psm1
Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0

function Add-DRSExplainableRisk {
    param([array]$Findings,$Protection,$AppControl,[array]$CodeIntegrityEvents,[hashtable]$ProvenanceByPath)

    foreach($f in $Findings){
        $ti=0;$ex=0;$active=0;$why=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
        if([string]$f.Classification -eq 'Known'){$ti+=70;$why.Add('+70 known vulnerable/blocked intelligence match')}
        if([string]$f.Verified -match 'TRUE|YES|1'){$ti+=15;$why.Add('+15 source marked verified')}
        if([bool]$f.MicrosoftBlocklistExact){$ti+=15;$why.Add('+15 exact Microsoft blocklist hash')}
        if([string]$f.ThreatProfileConfidence -match 'Exact'){$ti=[Math]::Min(100,$ti+20);$why.Add('+20 exact curated BYOVD profile')}

        if([string]$f.Path -match '\\System32\\drivers\\'){$ex+=20;$why.Add('+20 present in System32 drivers')}
        if([bool]$f.BroadDeviceAcl){$ex+=30;$why.Add('+30 broad device ACL')}
        if([string]$f.DeviceNames){$ex+=15;$why.Add('+15 device interface/string present')}
        if([string]$f.IoctlCandidates){$ex+=10;$why.Add('+10 private IOCTL candidates')}
        if([string]$f.DangerousImports){$ex+=15;$why.Add('+15 sensitive kernel imports')}
        if([string]$f.State -eq 'DriverStore only'){$ex+=5;$why.Add('+5 DriverStore presence only')}

        if([bool]$f.Running){$active+=55;$why.Add('+55 currently running')}
        if([bool]$f.RecentLoad){$active+=30;$why.Add('+30 recent driver-load telemetry')}
        if([bool]$f.RecentInstall){$active+=15;$why.Add('+15 recent driver installation telemetry')}

        if([bool]$Protection.HVCIRegistryEnabled){$active=[Math]::Max(0,$active-10);$why.Add('-10 HVCI enabled')}
        if([bool]$Protection.VulnerableDriverBlocklistEnabled){$active=[Math]::Max(0,$active-10);$why.Add('-10 vulnerable-driver blocklist enabled')}

        $policy=Get-DRSDriverPolicyAssessment -Finding $f -Protection $Protection -AppControl $AppControl -CodeIntegrityEvents $CodeIntegrityEvents
        $rem=[Math]::Round([Math]::Min(100,($ti*0.45)+($ex*0.30)+($active*0.25)))
        $priority=if($rem -ge 85){'Critical'}elseif($rem -ge 65){'High'}elseif($rem -ge 40){'Medium'}else{'Info'}

        [pscustomobject]@{
            FileName=[string]$f.FileName;Path=[string]$f.Path;SHA256=[string]$f.SHA256;Classification=[string]$f.Classification;
            ThreatIntelligenceScore=[Math]::Min(100,$ti);ExposureScore=[Math]::Min(100,$ex);ActiveRiskScore=[Math]::Min(100,$active);
            RemediationScore=$rem;Priority=$priority;WhyFlagged=($why -join '; ');
            PolicyAssessment=$policy.Status;PolicyConfidence=$policy.Confidence;PolicyReason=$policy.Reason;
            OriginalRemediationScore=[int]$f.RemediationScore;OriginalPriority=[string]$f.Priority;
            Running=[bool]$f.Running;RecentLoad=[bool]$f.RecentLoad;Company=[string]$f.Company;FileVersion=[string]$f.FileVersion;
            DriverServices=[string]$f.DriverServices;LOLDriversEntryId=[string]$f.LOLDriversEntryId;Category=[string]$f.Category;
            SignatureStatus=[string]$f.SignatureStatus;Signer=[string]$f.Signer;WHQLHint=[bool]$f.WHQLHint;DriverAgeYears=$f.DriverAgeYears;
            AdvisoryResources=[string]$f.AdvisoryResources;PatchAssessment=[string]$f.PatchAssessment;
            CVEs=[string]$f.CVEs;Verified=[string]$f.Verified;Usecase=[string]$f.Usecase;PrimitiveClass=[string]$f.PrimitiveClass;HeuristicCapabilities=[string]$f.HeuristicCapabilities;
            MicrosoftBlocklistExact=[bool]$f.MicrosoftBlocklistExact;MicrosoftVersionHint=[bool]$f.MicrosoftVersionHint;
            ThreatProfileConfidence=[string]$f.ThreatProfileConfidence;ThreatProfileProjects=[string]$f.ThreatProfileProjects;ThreatProfileIds=[string]$f.ThreatProfileIds;
            BroadDeviceAcl=[bool]$f.BroadDeviceAcl;DeviceNames=[string]$f.DeviceNames;PresentDevices=[string]$f.PresentDevices;
            IoctlCandidates=[string]$f.IoctlCandidates;DangerousImports=[string]$f.DangerousImports;State=[string]$f.State;RecentInstall=[bool]$f.RecentInstall;
            RecommendedAction=[string]$f.RecommendedAction
        }
    }
}
```

`Agent/Modules/SecurityProducts.psm1`:

```psm1
Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0

function Get-DRSInstalledApplications {
    $paths=@(
      'HKLM:\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall\*',
      'HKLM:\Software\WOW6432Node\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall\*'
    )
    $out=@()
    foreach($p in $paths){
        try{
            $out += @(Get-ItemProperty $p -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
                Where-Object DisplayName |
                Select-Object DisplayName,DisplayVersion,Publisher,InstallDate,InstallLocation)
        }catch{}
    }
    return @($out)
}

function Get-DRSSecurityProducts {
    param([string]$ProfilePath,[array]$DriverInventory=@())

    $profiles=@()
    if($ProfilePath -and (Test-Path $ProfilePath -PathType Leaf)){
        try{$profiles=@((Get-Content $ProfilePath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json -AsHashtable -Depth 20))}catch{}
    }

    $services=@();$processes=@();$apps=Get-DRSInstalledApplications;$sc=@()
    try{$services=@(Get-CimInstance Win32_Service -ErrorAction Stop)}catch{}
    try{$processes=@(Get-Process -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object ProcessName,Id,Path,Company)}catch{}
    try{$sc=@(Get-CimInstance -Namespace root\SecurityCenter2 -ClassName AntiVirusProduct -ErrorAction Stop)}catch{}

    $defender=$null
    try{
        $m=Get-MpComputerStatus -ErrorAction Stop
        $defender=[ordered]@{
            Product='Microsoft Defender Antivirus'
            Source='Get-MpComputerStatus'
            AMRunningMode=[string]$m.AMRunningMode
            AntivirusEnabled=[bool]$m.AntivirusEnabled
            AMServiceEnabled=[bool]$m.AMServiceEnabled
            RealTimeProtectionEnabled=[bool]$m.RealTimeProtectionEnabled
            BehaviorMonitorEnabled=[bool]$m.BehaviorMonitorEnabled
            IoavProtectionEnabled=[bool]$m.IoavProtectionEnabled
            NISEnabled=[bool]$m.NISEnabled
            IsTamperProtected=[bool]$m.IsTamperProtected
            EngineVersion=[string]$m.AMEngineVersion
            ProductVersion=[string]$m.AMProductVersion
            SignatureVersion=[string]$m.AntivirusSignatureVersion
            SignatureAge=[int]$m.AntivirusSignatureAge
            Active=([string]$m.AMRunningMode -match 'Normal|EDR Block')
        }
    }catch{}

    $results=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]

    foreach($p in $profiles){
        $name=[string]$p.Name
        $svcPatterns=@($p.Services)
        $procPatterns=@($p.Processes)
        $appPatterns=@($p.Applications)
        $publishers=@($p.Publishers)
        $driverPatterns=@($p.DriverPatterns)
        $matchedSvc=@($services | Where-Object {
            $n=[string]$_.Name;$d=[string]$_.DisplayName
            @($svcPatterns | Where-Object {$n -like $_ -or $d -like $_}).Count -gt 0
        })
        $matchedProc=@($processes | Where-Object {
            $n=[string]$_.ProcessName
            @($procPatterns | Where-Object {$n -like $_}).Count -gt 0
        })
        $matchedApps=@($apps | Where-Object {
            $dn=[string]$_.DisplayName;$pub=[string]$_.Publisher
            (@($appPatterns | Where-Object {$dn -like $_}).Count -gt 0) -or
            (@($publishers | Where-Object {$pub -like $_}).Count -gt 0)
        })
        $matchedDrivers=@($DriverInventory | Where-Object {
            $fn=[string]$_.FileName
            @($driverPatterns | Where-Object {$fn -like $_}).Count -gt 0
        })
        $matchedSC=@($sc | Where-Object {
            $dn=[string]$_.displayName
            ($dn -like "*$name*") -or (@($appPatterns | Where-Object {$dn -like $_}).Count -gt 0)
        })

        $evidence=0
        if($matchedSC.Count){$evidence+=35}
        if(@($matchedSvc|Where-Object State -eq 'Running').Count){$evidence+=30}
        if($matchedProc.Count){$evidence+=20}
        if($matchedApps.Count){$evidence+=15}
        if($matchedDrivers.Count){$evidence+=10}
        if($evidence -eq 0){continue}

        $results.Add([pscustomobject]@{
            Product=$name
            Confidence=[Math]::Min(100,$evidence)
            RegisteredWithSecurityCenter=($matchedSC.Count -gt 0)
            Installed=($matchedApps.Count -gt 0)
            RunningServices=(@($matchedSvc|Where-Object State -eq 'Running'|Select-Object -ExpandProperty Name) -join ',')
            AllMatchedServices=(@($matchedSvc|Select-Object -ExpandProperty Name) -join ',')
            RunningProcesses=(@($matchedProc|Select-Object -ExpandProperty ProcessName -Unique) -join ',')
            Versions=(@($matchedApps|Select-Object -ExpandProperty DisplayVersion -Unique) -join ',')
            Publishers=(@($matchedApps|Select-Object -ExpandProperty Publisher -Unique) -join ',')
            SecurityDrivers=(@($matchedDrivers|Select-Object -ExpandProperty FileName -Unique) -join ',')
            Active=((@($matchedSvc|Where-Object State -eq 'Running').Count -gt 0) -or $matchedProc.Count -gt 0 -or $matchedSC.Count -gt 0)
            Evidence="SecurityCenter=$($matchedSC.Count);RunningServices=$(@($matchedSvc|Where-Object State -eq 'Running').Count);Processes=$($matchedProc.Count);InstalledApps=$($matchedApps.Count);Drivers=$($matchedDrivers.Count)"
        }) | Out-Null
    }

    if($defender){
        $existing=@($results|Where-Object Product -eq 'Microsoft Defender Antivirus')
        if($existing.Count -eq 0){
            $results.Add([pscustomobject]@{
                Product='Microsoft Defender Antivirus';Confidence=100;RegisteredWithSecurityCenter=(@($sc|Where-Object displayName -match 'Defender').Count -gt 0);
                Installed=$true;RunningServices=$(if($defender.AMServiceEnabled){'WinDefend'}else{''});AllMatchedServices='WinDefend';
                RunningProcesses=$(if($defender.AMServiceEnabled){'MsMpEng'}else{''});SecurityDrivers=(@($DriverInventory|Where-Object { $_.FileName -in @('WdBoot.sys','WdFilter.sys','WdNisDrv.sys') }|Select-Object -ExpandProperty FileName -Unique) -join ',');Versions=$defender.ProductVersion;Publishers='Microsoft Corporation';
                Active=$defender.Active;Evidence="AMRunningMode=$($defender.AMRunningMode);AV=$($defender.AntivirusEnabled);Realtime=$($defender.RealTimeProtectionEnabled)"
            })|Out-Null
        }
    }

    return [pscustomobject]@{
        Products=@($results|Sort-Object -Property @{Expression='Confidence';Descending=$true},Product)
        Defender=$defender
        SecurityCenterProducts=@($sc|ForEach-Object{[pscustomobject]@{
            DisplayName=[string]$_.displayName
            ProductState=[string]$_.productState
            SignedProductExe=[string]$_.pathToSignedProductExe
            SignedReportingExe=[string]$_.pathToSignedReportingExe
        }})
    }
}
```

`Agent/Modules/Sigma.psm1`:

```psm1
Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0

function ConvertTo-DRSYamlScalar {
    param($Value)
    $s=[string]$Value
    return "'" + ($s -replace "'","''") + "'"
}

function Get-DRSSigmaProperty {
    param($Object,[string]$Name,$Default=$null)
    $p=$Object.PSObject.Properties[$Name]
    if($null -eq $p){return $Default}
    return $p.Value
}

function New-DRSSigmaRules {
    param(
        [AllowNull()][AllowEmptyCollection()][array]$Findings=@(),
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$OutputDirectory,
        [string]$ToolVersion='unknown'
    )

    $dir=Join-Path $OutputDirectory 'sigma-rules'
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dir -Force|Out-Null
    $generated=[System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()

    foreach($f in @($Findings|Where-Object {
        $_.Classification -eq 'Known' -or $_.Priority -in @('High','Critical') -or [int](Get-DRSSigmaProperty $_ 'ExploitIntelMatchCount' 0) -gt 0
    })){
        $driver=[string]$f.FileName
        if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($driver)){continue}
        $hash=[string]$f.SHA256
        $short=if($hash.Length -ge 16){$hash.Substring(0,16)}else{([guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N').Substring(0,16))}
        $base=[regex]::Replace([IO.Path]::GetFileNameWithoutExtension($driver),'[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]','_')
        $path=Join-Path $dir "$base-$short.yml"

        $refs=[System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
        foreach($cve in @((Get-DRSSigmaProperty $f 'ExploitIntelCves' @()))){
            if($cve){$refs.Add("https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/$cve")|Out-Null}
        }
        foreach($u in @((Get-DRSSigmaProperty $f 'ExploitIntelReferences' @()))){
            if($u -match '^https?://' -and -not $refs.Contains($u)){$refs.Add($u)|Out-Null}
        }
        if($refs.Count -eq 0){$refs.Add('https://www.loldrivers.io/')|Out-Null}

        $selectionHash=''
        $condition='selection_name'
        if($hash -match '^[A-Fa-f0-9]{64}$'){
            $selectionHash=@"
  selection_hash:
    Hashes|contains: $(ConvertTo-DRSYamlScalar ("SHA256=" + $hash.ToUpperInvariant()))
"@
            $condition='selection_name and selection_hash'
        }

        $refYaml=($refs|ForEach-Object{"  - " + (ConvertTo-DRSYamlScalar $_)}) -join "`n"
        $title="DriverRiskScout - Driver Load - $driver"
        $yaml=@"
title: $(ConvertTo-DRSYamlScalar $title)
id: $([guid]::NewGuid())
status: experimental
description: $(ConvertTo-DRSYamlScalar ("Detects a Sysmon driver-load event for a DriverRiskScout-prioritized driver. Current classification: " + [string]$f.Classification))
references:
$refYaml
author: $(ConvertTo-DRSYamlScalar ("DriverRiskScout " + $ToolVersion))
date: $(Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd')
tags:
  - attack.privilege-escalation
  - attack.t1068
logsource:
  product: windows
  service: sysmon
detection:
  selection_name:
    EventID: 6
    ImageLoaded|endswith: $(ConvertTo-DRSYamlScalar ("\" + $driver))
$selectionHash
  condition: $condition
falsepositives:
  - Legitimate vendor software or driver installation
level: $(if($f.Priority -eq 'Critical'){'critical'}elseif($f.Priority -eq 'High'){'high'}else{'medium'})
"@
        Set-Content -LiteralPath $path -Value $yaml -Encoding UTF8
        $generated.Add([pscustomobject]@{Name=[IO.Path]::GetFileName($path);Path=$path;Driver=$driver;SHA256=$hash})|Out-Null
    }

    [pscustomobject]@{
        RulesGenerated=$generated.Count
        OutputDirectory=$dir
        Rules=$generated.ToArray()
    }
}
```

`Agent/Modules/State.psm1`:

```psm1
Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0

function Read-DRSJsonFile {
    param([string]$Path,$Default)
    if(-not (Test-Path $Path -PathType Leaf)){return $Default}
    try{return (Get-Content $Path -Raw|ConvertFrom-Json -AsHashtable -Depth 50)}catch{return $Default}
}

function Write-DRSJsonFile {
    param([string]$Path,$Value)
    $parent=Split-Path $Path -Parent
    if($parent -and -not (Test-Path $parent)){New-Item -ItemType Directory $parent -Force|Out-Null}
    $Value|ConvertTo-Json -Depth 50|Set-Content $Path -Encoding UTF8
}

function Update-DRSFileHistory {
    param([array]$Inventory,[string]$HistoryPath,[bool]$SuppressInitialChangeEvents=$true)
    $now=(Get-Date).ToString('o')
    $hadState=Test-Path $HistoryPath -PathType Leaf
    $old=Read-DRSJsonFile $HistoryPath @{}
    if($old -isnot [System.Collections.IDictionary]){$old=@{}}
    $current=@{};$changes=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]

    foreach($i in $Inventory){
        $key=[string]$i.PathKey;if(-not $key){$key=([string]$i.Path).ToLowerInvariant()}
        $prev=$null;if(@($old.Keys) -contains $key){$prev=$old[$key]}
        $first=if($prev -and $prev.first_seen){[string]$prev.first_seen}else{$now}
        $record=[ordered]@{
            path=[string]$i.Path;sha256=[string]$i.SHA256;file_version=[string]$i.FileVersion;company=[string]$i.Company;
            state=[string]$i.State;first_seen=$first;last_seen=$now
        }
        $current[$key]=$record
        if(-not $prev){
            if($hadState -or -not $SuppressInitialChangeEvents){
                $changes.Add([pscustomobject]@{Type='driver_new';Path=$record.path;Old='';New=$record.sha256;Timestamp=$now})|Out-Null
            }
        } elseif(([string]$prev.sha256) -ne $record.sha256){
            $changes.Add([pscustomobject]@{Type='driver_hash_changed';Path=$record.path;Old=[string]$prev.sha256;New=$record.sha256;Timestamp=$now})|Out-Null
        } elseif(([string]$prev.state) -ne $record.state){
            $changes.Add([pscustomobject]@{Type='driver_state_changed';Path=$record.path;Old=[string]$prev.state;New=$record.state;Timestamp=$now})|Out-Null
        }
    }
    foreach($k in $old.Keys){
        if(-not $current.ContainsKey($k)){
            $p=$old[$k]
            $changes.Add([pscustomobject]@{Type='driver_removed';Path=[string]$p.path;Old=[string]$p.sha256;New='';Timestamp=$now})|Out-Null
        }
    }
    Write-DRSJsonFile $HistoryPath $current
    return [pscustomobject]@{History=$current;Changes=$changes.ToArray()}
}

function Compare-DRSProtectionState {
    param($Current,[string]$StatePath)
    $now=(Get-Date).ToString('o')
    $old=Read-DRSJsonFile $StatePath @{}
    $props=@(
      'HVCIRegistryEnabled','VBSRuntimeStatus','VulnerableDriverBlocklistEnabled','SecureBoot',
      'DefenderAntivirusEnabled','DefenderTamperProtection','VulnerableSignedDriverASRState'
    )
    $changes=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
    foreach($p in $props){
        $new=[string]$Current.$p
        $oldv=if(@($old.Keys) -contains $p){[string]$old[$p]}else{''}
        if($oldv -and $oldv -ne $new){
            $severity='Info'
            if($p -in @('HVCIRegistryEnabled','VulnerableDriverBlocklistEnabled','DefenderAntivirusEnabled','DefenderTamperProtection') -and $new -match 'False|Disabled|Not configured'){
                $severity='Critical'
            }
            $changes.Add([pscustomobject]@{Type='protection_changed';Protection=$p;Old=$oldv;New=$new;Severity=$severity;Timestamp=$now})|Out-Null
        }
    }
    $store=[ordered]@{};foreach($p in $props){$store[$p]=[string]$Current.$p}
    Write-DRSJsonFile $StatePath $store
    return $changes.ToArray()
}


function Compare-DRSSecurityProductState {
    param([array]$Products,[string]$StatePath,[bool]$SuppressInitialChangeEvents=$true)
    $now=(Get-Date).ToString('o')
    $hadState=Test-Path $StatePath -PathType Leaf
    $old=Read-DRSJsonFile $StatePath @{}
    if($old -isnot [System.Collections.IDictionary]){$old=@{}}
    $current=@{}
    foreach($p in @($Products)){
        if(-not [bool]$p.Active){continue}
        $current[[string]$p.Product]=[ordered]@{
            active=[bool]$p.Active;confidence=[int]$p.Confidence;versions=[string]$p.Versions;evidence=[string]$p.Evidence
        }
    }
    $changes=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
    foreach($name in $current.Keys){
        if(-not (@($old.Keys) -contains $name)){
            if($hadState -or -not $SuppressInitialChangeEvents){
                $changes.Add([pscustomobject]@{Type='security_product_added';Product=$name;Old='';New='Active';Severity='Info';Timestamp=$now})|Out-Null
            }
        }
    }
    foreach($name in $old.Keys){
        if(-not $current.ContainsKey($name)){
            $changes.Add([pscustomobject]@{Type='security_product_removed';Product=$name;Old='Active';New='Missing/inactive';Severity='High';Timestamp=$now})|Out-Null
        }
    }
    Write-DRSJsonFile $StatePath $current
    return $changes.ToArray()
}
```

`Agent/Modules/Timeline.psm1`:

```psm1
Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0

function New-DRSDriverTimeline {
    param([string]$OutputDirectory)
    $events=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
    $sources=@(
      @{File='sysmon-driver-load-events.csv';Type='driver_loaded';Time='TimeCreated'},
      @{File='codeintegrity-events.csv';Type='code_integrity';Time='TimeCreated'},
      @{File='driver-install-events.csv';Type='driver_installed';Time='TimeCreated'}
    )
    foreach($src in $sources){
        $path=Join-Path $OutputDirectory $src.File
        if(-not (Test-Path $path -PathType Leaf)){continue}
        foreach($r in @(Import-Csv $path)){
            $time='';$eventId='';$driver='';$sha256='';$message=''
            foreach($candidate in @($src.Time,'UtcTime','Date','Timestamp')){
                try{if($r.$candidate){$time=[string]$r.$candidate;break}}catch{}
            }
            try{$eventId=[string]$r.Id}catch{}
            if(-not $eventId){try{$eventId=[string]$r.EventId}catch{}}
            try{$driver=[string]$r.ImageLoaded}catch{}
            if(-not $driver){try{$driver=[string]$r.FilePath}catch{}}
            try{$sha256=[string]$r.SHA256}catch{}
            try{$message=[string]$r.Message}catch{}
            [void]$events.Add([pscustomobject]@{
                Time=$time
                Type=[string]$src.Type
                EventId=$eventId
                Driver=$driver
                SHA256=$sha256
                Message=$message
                SourceFile=[string]$src.File
            })
        }
    }
    return @($events|Sort-Object -Property @{Expression={try{[datetime]$_.Time}catch{[datetime]::MinValue}};Descending=$true})
}
Export-ModuleMember -Function *

```

`Agent/Modules/Wazuh.psm1`:

```psm1
Set-StrictMode -Version 2.0

function New-DRSWazuhEvents {
    param($ReportV2)
    $events=New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
    $events.Add([ordered]@{driver_risk_scout=[ordered]@{
        type='summary';computer=$ReportV2.host.computer_name;version=$ReportV2.tool.version;scan_id=$ReportV2.scan_id;
        files=$ReportV2.statistics.files;known=$ReportV2.statistics.known;suspicious=$ReportV2.statistics.suspicious;
        critical=$ReportV2.statistics.critical;high=$ReportV2.statistics.high;medium=$ReportV2.statistics.medium;
        security_products=(@($ReportV2.security_products.products|Where-Object active|ForEach-Object product) -join ',');
        timestamp=$ReportV2.created_at
    }})|Out-Null
    foreach($f in @($ReportV2.findings)){
        $events.Add([ordered]@{driver_risk_scout=[ordered]@{
            type='finding';computer=$ReportV2.host.computer_name;version=$ReportV2.tool.version;scan_id=$ReportV2.scan_id;
            priority=$f.Priority;score=$f.RemediationScore;classification=$f.Classification;driver=$f.FileName;path=$f.Path;
            sha256=$f.SHA256;running=$f.Running;recent_load=$f.RecentLoad;cves=$f.CVEs;
            policy_assessment=$f.PolicyAssessment;why_flagged=$f.WhyFlagged;microsoft_blocklist_exact=$f.MicrosoftBlocklistExact;threat_profile=$f.ThreatProfileProjects;threat_profile_confidence=$f.ThreatProfileConfidence;broad_device_acl=$f.BroadDeviceAcl;
            exploitability_score=$f.CurrentExploitabilityScore;exploitability_level=$f.CurrentExploitabilityLevel;potential_if_loaded_score=$f.PotentialIfLoadedScore;
            privilege_escalation_score=$f.PrivilegeEscalationScore;privilege_escalation_assessment=$f.PrivilegeEscalationAssessment;reachability=$f.Reachability;primitive=$f.Primitive;
            exploit_intel_matches=$f.ExploitIntelMatchCount;exploit_intel_cves=(@($f.ExploitIntelCves) -join ',');
            exploit_intel_max_cvss=$f.ExploitIntelMaxCVSS;research_maturity_score=$f.ResearchMaturityScore;
            public_poc_reference=$f.HasPublicPoCReference;exploit_intel_types=(@($f.ExploitIntelTypes) -join ',');
            timestamp=$ReportV2.created_at
        }})|Out-Null
    }
    foreach($f in @($ReportV2.findings)){
        if(([int]$f.MatchingCodeIntegrityEvents -gt 0) -or ([int]$f.MatchingDriverInstallEvents -gt 0) -or ([int]$f.MatchingSysmonDriverLoadEvents -gt 0)){
            $events.Add([ordered]@{driver_risk_scout=[ordered]@{
                type='lab_validation_observation';computer=$ReportV2.host.computer_name;scan_id=$ReportV2.scan_id;
                driver=$f.FileName;path=$f.Path;code_integrity_matches=$f.MatchingCodeIntegrityEvents;
                driver_install_matches=$f.MatchingDriverInstallEvents;sysmon_load_matches=$f.MatchingSysmonDriverLoadEvents;
                block_evidence=$f.BlockEvidence;block_confidence=$f.BlockEvidenceConfidence;timestamp=$ReportV2.created_at
            }})|Out-Null
        }
    }
    foreach($c in @($ReportV2.changes.driver_changes)){
        $events.Add([ordered]@{driver_risk_scout=[ordered]@{
            type=[string]$c.Type;computer=$ReportV2.host.computer_name;scan_id=$ReportV2.scan_id;path=$c.Path;old=$c.Old;new=$c.New;timestamp=$c.Timestamp
        }})|Out-Null
    }
    foreach($c in @($ReportV2.changes.protection_changes)){
        $events.Add([ordered]@{driver_risk_scout=[ordered]@{
            type='protection_changed';computer=$ReportV2.host.computer_name;scan_id=$ReportV2.scan_id;protection=$c.Protection;old=$c.Old;new=$c.New;severity=$c.Severity;timestamp=$c.Timestamp
        }})|Out-Null
    }

    foreach($c in @($ReportV2.changes.security_product_changes)){
        $events.Add([ordered]@{driver_risk_scout=[ordered]@{
            type=[string]$c.Type;computer=$ReportV2.host.computer_name;scan_id=$ReportV2.scan_id;
            security_product=$c.Product;old=$c.Old;new=$c.New;severity=$c.Severity;timestamp=$c.Timestamp
        }})|Out-Null
    }
    return $events.ToArray()
}

function Write-DRSWazuhNdjson {
    param([array]$Events,[string]$Path)
    $parent=Split-Path $Path -Parent
    if($parent -and -not (Test-Path $parent)){New-Item -ItemType Directory $parent -Force|Out-Null}
    $lines=@($Events|ForEach-Object{$_|ConvertTo-Json -Compress -Depth 20})
    Set-Content $Path $lines -Encoding UTF8
}

Export-ModuleMember -Function *
```

`Agent/Start-DriverRiskScout.cmd`:

```cmd
@echo off
setlocal EnableExtensions
cd /d "%~dp0"

where pwsh.exe >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [ERROR] PowerShell 7 ^(pwsh.exe^) wurde nicht gefunden.
    echo Bitte PowerShell 7 installieren und danach erneut starten.
    pause
    exit /b 90
)

if not exist "%~dp0config\config.json" (
    if exist "%~dp0config\config.example.json" (
        copy /Y "%~dp0config\config.example.json" "%~dp0config\config.json" >nul
    ) else (
        echo [ERROR] config\config.example.json wurde nicht gefunden.
        pause
        exit /b 90
    )
)

echo DriverRiskScout 2.3.5 - Self-Test...
echo.
pwsh.exe -NoLogo -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0Test-DriverRiskScout.ps1"
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo.
    echo [ERROR] Der DriverRiskScout Self-Test ist fehlgeschlagen.
    echo Der Scan wurde aus Sicherheitsgruenden nicht gestartet.
    pause
    exit /b 90
)

echo.
echo Starte DriverRiskScout 2.3.5 mit PowerShell 7...
echo.

pwsh.exe -NoLogo -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0DriverRiskScout.ps1" -ConfigPath "%~dp0config\config.json"

set "rc=%ERRORLEVEL%"
echo.
echo DriverRiskScout beendet. ExitCode=%rc%
pause
exit /b %rc%
```

`Agent/Test-DriverRiskScout.ps1`:

```ps1
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$failed=$false

if($PSVersionTable.PSVersion.Major -lt 7){
    Write-Host "PowerShell 7 required; detected $($PSVersionTable.PSVersion)" -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}

$files=@(
  "$PSScriptRoot\DriverRiskScout.ps1",
  "$PSScriptRoot\Core\DriverRiskScout-Core.ps1",
  "$PSScriptRoot\Install-Agent.ps1",
  "$PSScriptRoot\Uninstall-Agent.ps1"
)+@(Get-ChildItem "$PSScriptRoot\Modules" -Filter '*.psm1'|Select-Object -ExpandProperty FullName)

foreach($f in $files){
  $t=$null;$e=$null
  [System.Management.Automation.Language.Parser]::ParseFile($f,[ref]$t,[ref]$e)|Out-Null
  if(@($e).Count){
    $failed=$true
    Write-Host "Parser errors in $f" -ForegroundColor Red
    $e|ForEach-Object{Write-Host ("  Line {0}, column {1}: {2}" -f $_.Extent.StartLineNumber,$_.Extent.StartColumnNumber,$_.Message) -ForegroundColor Red}
  } else {
    Write-Host "Parser OK: $([IO.Path]::GetFileName($f))" -ForegroundColor Green
  }
}

foreach($j in @(
  "$PSScriptRoot\config\config.example.json",
  "$PSScriptRoot\profiles\known-byovd.json",
  "$PSScriptRoot\profiles\security-products.json",
  "$PSScriptRoot\profiles\exploitability-policy.json",
  "$PSScriptRoot\profiles\exploit-db.json",
  "$PSScriptRoot\config\config.imported-v2.3.0.json",
  "$PSScriptRoot\Core\profiles\known-byovd.json"
)){
  try{
    $o=Get-Content $j -Raw|ConvertFrom-Json -AsHashtable -Depth 50
    Write-Host "JSON OK: $([IO.Path]::GetFileName($j))" -ForegroundColor Green
  }catch{
    $failed=$true
    Write-Host "JSON FAILED: $j - $($_.Exception.Message)" -ForegroundColor Red
  }
}

# Regression fixture for LOLDrivers-style case-distinct JSON keys.
# Wrap pipeline output in @() because PowerShell enumerates a single-element JSON
# array and would otherwise assign the one hashtable directly to $fixture.
try{
  $fixture=@('[{"init":"lower","INIT":"upper","page":1,"PAGE":2}]'|ConvertFrom-Json -AsHashtable)
  if($fixture.Count -ne 1){throw "Expected one fixture record, got $($fixture.Count)."}
  $row=$fixture[0]
  if($null -eq $row){throw 'Fixture record is null.'}
  if(-not ($row -is [System.Collections.IDictionary])){throw "Fixture record has unexpected type: $($row.GetType().FullName)"}
  if($row['init'] -ne 'lower' -or $row['INIT'] -ne 'upper'){throw 'Case-distinct JSON keys were not preserved.'}
  if($row['page'] -ne 1 -or $row['PAGE'] -ne 2){throw 'Second case-distinct JSON key pair was not preserved.'}
  Write-Host 'JSON case-collision regression: OK' -ForegroundColor Green
}catch{
  $failed=$true
  Write-Host "JSON case-collision regression FAILED: $($_.Exception.Message)" -ForegroundColor Red
}

# Module/config smoke test without scanning.
try{
  Import-Module "$PSScriptRoot\Modules\Common.psm1" -Force -DisableNameChecking
  $cfg=Get-DRSConfig "$PSScriptRoot\config\config.example.json"
  if([int]$cfg.Performance.MaxParallelism -lt 1){throw 'Invalid MaxParallelism'}
  if(-not $cfg.State.Root){throw 'State.Root missing'}
  Write-Host 'Common/config smoke test: OK' -ForegroundColor Green
}catch{
  $failed=$true
  Write-Host "Common/config smoke test FAILED: $($_.Exception.Message)" -ForegroundColor Red
}


# PowerShell 7.6 generic-list regression guard.
try{
  $genericList=[System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
  $genericList.Add([pscustomobject]@{Value=1})|Out-Null
  $genericArray=$genericList.ToArray()
  if($genericArray.Count -ne 1 -or $genericArray[0].Value -ne 1){throw 'Generic List[object] conversion failed'}
  Write-Host 'Generic List[object] regression: OK' -ForegroundColor Green
}catch{
  $failed=$true
  Write-Host "Generic List[object] regression FAILED: $($_.Exception.Message)" -ForegroundColor Red
}

# Exploit Intelligence hashtable-access regression.
try{
  Import-Module "$PSScriptRoot\Modules\ExploitIntel.psm1" -Force -DisableNameChecking
  $rawRecord=@(
    Get-Content "$PSScriptRoot\profiles\exploit-db.json" -Raw |
      ConvertFrom-Json -AsHashtable -Depth 30
  ) | Select-Object -First 1

  $rawCve=Get-DRSExploitProperty $rawRecord 'CVE' ''
  $rawDrivers=@(Get-DRSExploitProperty $rawRecord 'AffectedDrivers' @())

  if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string]$rawCve)){throw 'Raw hashtable CVE lookup returned empty'}
  if($rawDrivers.Count -lt 1){throw 'Raw hashtable AffectedDrivers lookup returned empty'}

  $imported=@(Import-DRSExploitDatabase -Path "$PSScriptRoot\profiles\exploit-db.json")
  if($imported.Count -lt 1){throw 'Exploit database import returned zero records'}
  $afdEntry=@($imported|Where-Object { @($_.AffectedDrivers) -contains 'afd.sys' }|Select-Object -First 1)
  if($afdEntry.Count -ne 1){throw 'Imported exploit DB does not contain afd.sys mapping'}
  if($afdEntry[0].CVE -ne 'CVE-2023-21768'){throw "afd.sys maps to unexpected CVE: $($afdEntry[0].CVE)"}

  Write-Host 'Exploit Intelligence hashtable regression: OK' -ForegroundColor Green
}catch{
  $failed=$true
  Write-Host "Exploit Intelligence hashtable regression FAILED: $($_.Exception.Message)" -ForegroundColor Red
}

# PowerShell automatic $Matches collision regression.
try{
  Import-Module "$PSScriptRoot\Modules\ExploitIntel.psm1" -Force -DisableNameChecking
  $researchRecords=@(
    [pscustomobject]@{
      CVE='CVE-2023-21768'
      Url='https://example.invalid/research'
      Type='Local-Privilege-Escalation'
      CVSS=7.8
      SourceVerifiedFlag=$true
    }
  )
  $score=Get-DRSResearchMaturityScore -ResearchRecords $researchRecords

  # Trigger PowerShell's automatic regex $Matches variable deliberately.
  $null=('Local-Privilege-Escalation' -match 'Privilege')
  if($Matches -isnot [System.Collections.IDictionary]){
    throw 'PowerShell automatic $Matches variable was not created as expected'
  }

  # The research array must remain independent of the automatic $Matches variable.
  if($researchRecords.Count -ne 1 -or $researchRecords[0].CVE -ne 'CVE-2023-21768'){
    throw 'Research records were corrupted by automatic $Matches variable'
  }
  if($score -le 0){throw 'Research maturity score regression returned zero'}

  Write-Host 'Automatic $Matches collision regression: OK' -ForegroundColor Green
}catch{
  $failed=$true
  Write-Host "Automatic `$Matches collision regression FAILED: $($_.Exception.Message)" -ForegroundColor Red
}

# Zero-findings / clean-host regression.
try{
  Import-Module "$PSScriptRoot\Modules\ExploitIntel.psm1" -Force -DisableNameChecking
  Import-Module "$PSScriptRoot\Modules\Sigma.psm1" -Force -DisableNameChecking

  $emptyIntel=@(Add-DRSExploitIntel -Findings @() -DatabasePath "$PSScriptRoot\profiles\exploit-db.json" -CacheRoot "$PSScriptRoot\state-test" -EnableOnline:$false -NoNetwork)
  if($emptyIntel.Count -ne 0){throw "Expected zero exploit-intel results for zero findings, got $($emptyIntel.Count)"}

  $emptyLeads=@(Get-DRSExploitIntelInventoryLeads -Inventory @() -DatabasePath "$PSScriptRoot\profiles\exploit-db.json")
  if($emptyLeads.Count -ne 0){throw "Expected zero research leads for zero inventory, got $($emptyLeads.Count)"}

  $emptySigma=New-DRSSigmaRules -Findings @() -OutputDirectory "$PSScriptRoot\sigma-empty-test" -ToolVersion 'self-test'
  if($emptySigma.RulesGenerated -ne 0){throw "Expected zero Sigma rules for zero findings, got $($emptySigma.RulesGenerated)"}

  Remove-Item "$PSScriptRoot\state-test" -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Remove-Item "$PSScriptRoot\sigma-empty-test" -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Write-Host 'Zero-findings clean-host regression: OK' -ForegroundColor Green
}catch{
  $failed=$true
  Remove-Item "$PSScriptRoot\state-test" -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Remove-Item "$PSScriptRoot\sigma-empty-test" -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Write-Host "Zero-findings clean-host regression FAILED: $($_.Exception.Message)" -ForegroundColor Red
  if($_.ScriptStackTrace){Write-Host ("  Stack: " + $_.ScriptStackTrace) -ForegroundColor DarkYellow}
}

# Exploit Intelligence smoke test; local database only, no network.
try{
  Import-Module "$PSScriptRoot\Modules\ExploitIntel.psm1" -Force -DisableNameChecking
  $sample=[pscustomobject]@{
    FileName='afd.sys';Path='C:\Windows\System32\drivers\afd.sys';SHA256=('b'*64);
    CVEs='';CVEList=@()
  }
  $intel=@(Add-DRSExploitIntel -Findings @($sample) -DatabasePath "$PSScriptRoot\profiles\exploit-db.json" -CacheRoot "$PSScriptRoot\state-test" -EnableOnline:$false -NoNetwork)
  if($intel.Count -ne 1){throw "Expected one exploit-intel result, got $($intel.Count)"}
  $intelRow=$intel[0]
  if($null -eq $intelRow){throw 'Exploit-intel result is null'}
  $intelProps=@($intelRow.PSObject.Properties.Name)
  foreach($required in @('MatchCount','CVEs','ResearchMaturityScore')){
    if($intelProps -notcontains $required){throw "Exploit-intel result missing property '$required'. Properties: $($intelProps -join ', ')"}
  }
  if([int]$intelRow.MatchCount -lt 1){throw 'Expected afd.sys to match bundled exploit DB'}
  if(@($intelRow.CVEs) -notcontains 'CVE-2023-21768'){throw 'Expected CVE-2023-21768 for afd.sys'}
  if([int]$intelRow.ResearchMaturityScore -le 0){throw 'Expected positive ResearchMaturityScore'}
  $leads=@(Get-DRSExploitIntelInventoryLeads -Inventory @([pscustomobject]@{
    FileName='afd.sys';Path='C:\Windows\System32\drivers\afd.sys';SHA256=('b'*64);
    Company='Microsoft';FileVersion='test';State='Running';Running=$true
  }) -DatabasePath "$PSScriptRoot\profiles\exploit-db.json")
  if($leads.Count -lt 1 -or $leads[0].CVE -ne 'CVE-2023-21768'){throw 'Inventory research lead mapping failed'}
  Remove-Item "$PSScriptRoot\state-test" -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Write-Host 'Exploit Intelligence smoke test: OK' -ForegroundColor Green
}catch{
  $failed=$true
  Remove-Item "$PSScriptRoot\state-test" -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Write-Host "Exploit Intelligence smoke test FAILED: $($_.Exception.Message)" -ForegroundColor Red
  if($_.InvocationInfo){
    Write-Host ("  At: " + $_.InvocationInfo.PositionMessage) -ForegroundColor DarkYellow
  }
  if($_.ScriptStackTrace){
    Write-Host ("  Stack: " + $_.ScriptStackTrace) -ForegroundColor DarkYellow
  }
}

# Sigma generator smoke test; produces only a local YAML detection rule.
try{
  Import-Module "$PSScriptRoot\Modules\Sigma.psm1" -Force -DisableNameChecking
  $sample=[pscustomobject]@{
    FileName='afd.sys';SHA256=('b'*64);Classification='Known';Priority='High';
    ExploitIntelMatchCount=1;ExploitIntelCves=@('CVE-2023-21768');
    ExploitIntelReferences=@('https://example.invalid/research')
  }
  $sigma=New-DRSSigmaRules -Findings @($sample) -OutputDirectory "$PSScriptRoot\sigma-test" -ToolVersion 'self-test'
  if($sigma.RulesGenerated -ne 1){throw 'Expected one Sigma rule'}
  $rule=Get-Content $sigma.Rules[0].Path -Raw
  if($rule -notmatch 'EventID: 6'){throw 'Sigma EventID 6 missing'}
  if($rule -notmatch 'afd\.sys'){throw 'Sigma driver filename missing'}
  Remove-Item "$PSScriptRoot\sigma-test" -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Write-Host 'Sigma generator smoke test: OK' -ForegroundColor Green
}catch{
  $failed=$true
  Remove-Item "$PSScriptRoot\sigma-test" -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Write-Host "Sigma generator smoke test FAILED: $($_.Exception.Message)" -ForegroundColor Red
}

# Investigation module smoke test; no network or driver interaction.
try{
  Import-Module "$PSScriptRoot\Modules\Investigation.psm1" -Force -DisableNameChecking
  $sample=[pscustomobject]@{
    FileName='sample.sys';Path='C:\Windows\System32\drivers\sample.sys';SHA256=('a'*64);
    Classification='Known';LOLDriversEntryId='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000';
    MicrosoftBlocklistExact=$false;MicrosoftVersionHint=$false;ThreatProfileConfidence='';
    ThreatProfileProjects='';AdvisoryResources='';CVEs='CVE-2024-12345';DriverServices='SampleSvc';
    Running=$false;CurrentExploitabilityScore=30;Usecase='';PresentDevices='';BroadDeviceAcl=$false;
    CVEList=@('CVE-2024-12345');ExploitIntelCves=@('CVE-2024-12345');ExploitIntelMatches=@();
    ExploitIntelTypes=@();ExploitIntelReferences=@();ResearchMaturityScore=0;ExploitIntelMaxCVSS=0
  }
  $links=@(Get-DRSSourceLinks $sample)
  $cmds=@(Get-DRSSafeValidationCommands $sample)
  if(@($links|Where-Object Name -like 'GitHub*').Count -lt 1){throw 'GitHub source link missing'}
  if(@($cmds|Where-Object Command -match 'sc\.exe query').Count -ne 1){throw 'Safe sc.exe query command missing'}
  if(@($cmds|Where-Object Command -match 'sc\.exe (create|start)').Count -ne 0){throw 'Unsafe service command generated'}
  $cimCmd=@($cmds|Where-Object Title -eq 'Kernel-Service nach Dateiname suchen'|Select-Object -First 1).Command
  if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace([string]$cimCmd)){throw 'CIM validation command missing'}
  if($cimCmd -notmatch 'Where-Object \{ .*PathName.* \}'){throw 'CIM validation command formatting failed'}
  Write-Host 'Investigation module smoke test: OK' -ForegroundColor Green
}catch{
  $failed=$true
  Write-Host "Investigation module smoke test FAILED: $($_.Exception.Message)" -ForegroundColor Red
}

Write-Host "PowerShell: $($PSVersionTable.PSVersion)"
Write-Host 'This self-test does not run a driver scan or contact the Collector.'
if($failed){exit 1}else{exit 0}
```

`Agent/Uninstall-Agent.ps1`:

```ps1
[CmdletBinding(SupportsShouldProcess)]
param(
  [string]$InstallPath='C:\Program Files\DriverRiskScout',
  [string]$DataPath='C:\ProgramData\DriverRiskScout',
  [switch]$RemoveData,
  [switch]$RemoveApiKey
)
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
if(-not ([Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal]::new([Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent())).IsInRole([Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)){
  throw 'Run from an elevated PowerShell session.'
}
if($PSCmdlet.ShouldProcess('DriverRiskScout','Uninstall')){
  Unregister-ScheduledTask -TaskName 'DriverRiskScout Daily' -Confirm:$false -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  if(Test-Path $InstallPath){Remove-Item $InstallPath -Recurse -Force}
  if($RemoveData -and (Test-Path $DataPath)){Remove-Item $DataPath -Recurse -Force}
  if($RemoveApiKey){[Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable('DRS_COLLECTOR_API_KEY',$null,'Machine')}
  Write-Host 'DriverRiskScout agent uninstalled.' -ForegroundColor Green
}
```

```json
    "EnableOnlineCertificateRevocation": true,
    "EnableExploitIntel": true,

    "ExploitDbPath": "profiles\\exploit-db.json",
    "GenerateSigmaRules": true
  },
  "Output": {
    "Root": "./reports",
    "KeepReportsDays": 30,
    "IncludeFullInventoryInAgentPayload": false,
    "Html": true,
    "Json": true,
    "Csv": true
  },
  "State": {

    "CacheRoot": "./state/cache",
    "SpoolRoot": "./state/spool",
```

`Agent/rules/DriverRiskScout.yar`:

```yar
/* DriverRiskScout defensive YARA heuristics.
   These rules identify review-worthy Windows kernel-driver characteristics.
   A match is NOT proof of a vulnerability or exploitability. */

rule DRS_Process_Termination_APIs
{
    meta:
        description = "Kernel driver imports/strings associated with process termination"
        purpose = "defensive triage"
    strings:
        $a1 = "ZwTerminateProcess" ascii wide
        $a2 = "PsLookupProcessByProcessId" ascii wide
        $a3 = "ObOpenObjectByPointer" ascii wide
    condition:
        uint16(0) == 0x5A4D and 2 of ($a*)
}

rule DRS_Kernel_Physical_Memory_APIs
{
    meta:
        description = "Kernel driver strings associated with kernel/physical memory mapping"
        purpose = "defensive triage"
    strings:
        $m1 = "MmMapIoSpace" ascii wide
        $m2 = "MmMapIoSpaceEx" ascii wide
        $m3 = "MmMapLockedPagesSpecifyCache" ascii wide
        $m4 = "MmCopyMemory" ascii wide
        $m5 = "MmMapLockedPages" ascii wide
    condition:
        uint16(0) == 0x5A4D and 2 of ($m*)
}

rule DRS_Device_Exposure_APIs
{
    meta:
        description = "Kernel driver exposes a device/symbolic-link interface"
        purpose = "defensive triage"
    strings:
        $d1 = "IoCreateDevice" ascii wide
        $d2 = "IoCreateDeviceSecure" ascii wide
        $d3 = "IoCreateSymbolicLink" ascii wide
        $d4 = "\\DosDevices\\" ascii wide
        $d5 = "\\Device\\" ascii wide
    condition:
        uint16(0) == 0x5A4D and (($d1 or $d2) and 1 of ($d3,$d4,$d5))
}

rule DRS_User_Buffer_Handling_APIs
{
    meta:
        description = "Kernel driver contains user-buffer validation/mapping APIs"
        purpose = "defensive triage"
    strings:
        $b1 = "ProbeForRead" ascii wide
        $b2 = "ProbeForWrite" ascii wide
        $b3 = "MmProbeAndLockPages" ascii wide
        $b4 = "MmGetSystemAddressForMdlSafe" ascii wide
    condition:
        uint16(0) == 0x5A4D and 2 of ($b*)
}

```

`BUILD-VALIDATION.txt`:

```txt
DriverRiskScout 2.3.5 - BUILD VALIDATION
================================================

PASS  ExploitIntel accepts zero findings
PASS  ExploitIntel inventory correlation accepts zero inventory
PASS  Main orchestration skips only finding enrichment when findings=0
PASS  Full inventory research correlation still executes when findings=0
PASS  Sigma generator accepts zero findings and returns zero rules
PASS  Empty research/investigation JSON uses valid array serialization
PASS  Native zero-findings clean-host regression added
PASS  PowerShell 7.6 fixes from 2.3.1-2.3.4 retained
PASS  JSON validation
PASS  Wazuh XML validation
PASS  Python compile: Server/app.py
PASS  Collector pytest: [32m.[0m[32m                                                                        [100%][0m | [32m[32m[1m1 passed[0m[32m in 1.00s[0m[0m

Root cause:
A clean scan can legitimately produce an empty prioritized-findings collection.
Add-DRSExploitIntel declared Findings as a Mandatory array without AllowEmptyCollection,
so PowerShell rejected the call before the function body ran.

Native Windows validation:
    .\Test-DriverRiskScout.ps1

Expected additional line:
    Zero-findings clean-host regression: OK

```

`CHANGELOG.md`:

```md
# Changelog

## 2.3.5
- Fixed clean-host scans with zero prioritized findings.
- Exploit Intelligence now accepts an empty findings collection and returns zero results instead of terminating the scan.
- Full-inventory research-lead correlation still runs when prioritized findings are empty.
- Sigma generation now accepts an empty findings collection and returns zero generated rules.
- Empty investigations/research-lead JSON outputs are serialized as valid JSON arrays (`[]`).
- Added native zero-findings/zero-inventory/zero-Sigma regression tests.
- No vulnerability scoring or classification semantics changed.

## 2.3.4
- Fixed collision with PowerShell's automatic `$Matches` regex variable.
- Renamed the Exploit Intel local `$matches` collection to `$intelRecords`.
- Renamed Get-DRSResearchMaturityScore parameter from `$Matches` to `$ResearchRecords`.
- Prevents regex `-match` operations from replacing the exploit-intel collection with a Hashtable.
- Added native regression test that deliberately creates automatic `$Matches` and verifies research data remains intact.
- Added stack/location diagnostics to Exploit Intelligence smoke-test failures.
- No scoring or vulnerability-classification semantics changed.

## 2.3.3
- Fixed ExploitIntel lookup for JSON records produced by ConvertFrom-Json -AsHashtable.
- Get-DRSExploitProperty now supports IDictionary / OrderedHashtable input.
- Dictionary keys are resolved case-sensitively first to preserve case-colliding JSON keys.
- Added an ambiguity-safe case-insensitive fallback only when exactly one key matches.
- Added native regression tests for raw exploit-db CVE/AffectedDrivers lookup and afd.sys -> CVE-2023-21768 import mapping.
- No exploitability scoring or classification semantics changed.
```
