`Server/Dockerfile`:

```
FROM python:3.13-slim
WORKDIR /app
COPY requirements.txt requirements.lock.txt ./
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.lock.txt
COPY app.py run.py ./
RUN mkdir -p /data/wazuh && useradd --system --uid 10001 --home /nonexistent --shell /usr/sbin/nologin drs && chown -R drs:drs /data /app
USER drs
EXPOSE 8088
CMD ["python","run.py"]

```

`Server/app.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

import gzip
import hashlib
import hmac
import json
import os
import re
import secrets
import sqlite3
import threading
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Header, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, JSONResponse, Response
from fastapi.security import HTTPBasic, HTTPBasicCredentials
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict

APP_VERSION = "2.3.5"
DATA_DIR = Path(os.getenv("DRS_DATA_DIR", "/data"))
DB_PATH = Path(os.getenv("DRS_DB_PATH", str(DATA_DIR / "drs.sqlite3")))
WAZUH_PATH = Path(os.getenv("DRS_WAZUH_PATH", str(DATA_DIR / "wazuh" / "driver-risk-scout.ndjson")))
INGEST_API_KEY = os.getenv("DRS_INGEST_API_KEY", "")
ADMIN_USER = os.getenv("DRS_ADMIN_USER", "admin")
ADMIN_PASSWORD = os.getenv("DRS_ADMIN_PASSWORD", "")
RETENTION_DAYS = int(os.getenv("DRS_RETENTION_DAYS", "90"))
MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES = int(os.getenv("DRS_MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES", str(25 * 1024 * 1024)))
ONLINE_ENRICHMENT = os.getenv("DRS_ONLINE_ENRICHMENT", "false").strip().lower() in {"1","true","yes","on"}
ENRICHMENT_TTL_HOURS = int(os.getenv("DRS_ENRICHMENT_TTL_HOURS", "12"))
GITHUB_TOKEN = os.getenv("DRS_GITHUB_TOKEN", "").strip()
NVD_API_KEY = os.getenv("DRS_NVD_API_KEY", "").strip()
ONLINE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = int(os.getenv("DRS_ONLINE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS", "8"))

DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
DB_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
WAZUH_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

security = HTTPBasic(auto_error=False)
db_lock = threading.Lock()


def utcnow() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def db() -> sqlite3.Connection:
    c = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH, timeout=30)
    c.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    c.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
    c.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
    return c


def init_db() -> None:
    with db_lock, db() as c:
        c.executescript(
            """
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hosts (
              host_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
              computer_name TEXT NOT NULL,
              agent_id TEXT DEFAULT '',
              domain TEXT DEFAULT '',
              site TEXT DEFAULT '',
              tags_json TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
              first_seen TEXT NOT NULL,
              last_seen TEXT NOT NULL,
              os_caption TEXT,
              build_number TEXT,
              tool_version TEXT,
              critical INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
              high INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
              medium INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
              known INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
              suspicious INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
              security_products_json TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
              protection_json TEXT DEFAULT '{}',
              latest_snapshot_id INTEGER
            );
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS snapshots (
              id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
              host_id TEXT NOT NULL,
              received_at TEXT NOT NULL,
              scan_time TEXT,
              scan_id TEXT,
              payload_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
              payload_gzip BLOB NOT NULL,
              FOREIGN KEY(host_id) REFERENCES hosts(host_id)
            );
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_snapshots_host_time ON snapshots(host_id, received_at DESC);
            CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_snapshots_host_scan ON snapshots(host_id, scan_id) WHERE scan_id IS NOT NULL AND scan_id <> '';
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS findings (
              id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
              snapshot_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
              host_id TEXT NOT NULL,
              received_at TEXT NOT NULL,
              priority TEXT,
              classification TEXT,
              driver TEXT,
              path TEXT,
              sha256 TEXT,
              running INTEGER,
              score INTEGER,
              policy_assessment TEXT,
              data_json TEXT NOT NULL,
              FOREIGN KEY(snapshot_id) REFERENCES snapshots(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
            );
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_findings_host ON findings(host_id, received_at DESC);
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_findings_sha ON findings(sha256);
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS driver_inventory (
              id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
              snapshot_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
              host_id TEXT NOT NULL,
              received_at TEXT NOT NULL,
              file_name TEXT,
              path TEXT,
              sha256 TEXT,
              state TEXT,
              company TEXT,
              file_version TEXT,
              running INTEGER,
              data_json TEXT NOT NULL,
              FOREIGN KEY(snapshot_id) REFERENCES snapshots(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
            );
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inventory_sha ON driver_inventory(sha256);
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inventory_name ON driver_inventory(file_name);
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inventory_host ON driver_inventory(host_id, received_at DESC);
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS changes (
              id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
              snapshot_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
              host_id TEXT NOT NULL,
              received_at TEXT NOT NULL,
              change_type TEXT,
              severity TEXT,
              data_json TEXT NOT NULL,
              FOREIGN KEY(snapshot_id) REFERENCES snapshots(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
            );
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_changes_host ON changes(host_id, received_at DESC);
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS enrichment_cache (
              evidence_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
              created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
              data_json TEXT NOT NULL
            );
            """
        )
        # Lightweight forward-compatible migrations for existing 2.x SQLite files.
        existing = {row[1] for row in c.execute("PRAGMA table_info(hosts)").fetchall()}
        for column, ddl in {
            "agent_id": "TEXT DEFAULT ''",
            "domain": "TEXT DEFAULT ''",
            "site": "TEXT DEFAULT ''",
            "tags_json": "TEXT DEFAULT '[]'",
        }.items():
            if column not in existing:
                c.execute(f"ALTER TABLE hosts ADD COLUMN {column} {ddl}")


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(_: FastAPI):
    init_db()
    yield


app = FastAPI(title="DriverRiskScout Collector", version=APP_VERSION, lifespan=lifespan)


class IngestPayload(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="allow")
    schema_version: str
    report: dict[str, Any]
    wazuh_events: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    inventory: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None
    provenance: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None


def require_ingest_key(x_drs_api_key: str | None = Header(default=None)) -> None:
    if not INGEST_API_KEY:
        raise HTTPException(503, "Collector ingest API key is not configured.")
    if not x_drs_api_key or not hmac.compare_digest(x_drs_api_key, INGEST_API_KEY):
        raise HTTPException(401, "Invalid ingest API key.")


def require_admin(credentials: HTTPBasicCredentials | None = Depends(security)) -> str:
    if not ADMIN_PASSWORD:
        raise HTTPException(503, "Dashboard administrator password is not configured.")
    if credentials is None:
        raise HTTPException(401, "Authentication required.", headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Basic"})
    user_ok = hmac.compare_digest(credentials.username.encode(), ADMIN_USER.encode())
    pass_ok = hmac.compare_digest(credentials.password.encode(), ADMIN_PASSWORD.encode())
    if not (user_ok and pass_ok):
        raise HTTPException(401, "Invalid credentials.", headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Basic"})
    return credentials.username


def host_id_for(name: str, agent_id: str = '') -> str:
    stable = agent_id.strip().lower() if agent_id else name.strip().lower()
    return hashlib.sha256(stable.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()[:32]


def append_wazuh(events: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    if not events:
        return
    with WAZUH_PATH.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for event in events:
            f.write(json.dumps(event, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")



def parse_iso(value: str) -> datetime | None:
    try:
        return datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    except Exception:
        return None


def fetch_json(url: str, headers: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> Any:
    req_headers = {
        "User-Agent": f"DriverRiskScout-Collector/{APP_VERSION}",
        "Accept": "application/json",
    }
    if headers:
        req_headers.update(headers)
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=req_headers, method="GET")
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=ONLINE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS) as response:
            raw = response.read()
            return json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        body = ""
        try:
            body = exc.read(1024).decode("utf-8", "replace")
        except Exception:
            pass
        raise RuntimeError(f"HTTP {exc.code} for {url}: {body[:300]}") from exc
    except urllib.error.URLError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Network error for {url}: {exc.reason}") from exc


def extract_cves(finding: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    candidates: list[str] = []
    raw = finding.get("CVEList")
    if isinstance(raw, list):
        candidates.extend(str(x) for x in raw)
    candidates.extend(re.split(r"[,;|\s]+", str(finding.get("CVEs") or "")))
    out: list[str] = []
    for value in candidates:
        v = value.strip().upper()
        if re.fullmatch(r"CVE-\d{4}-\d{4,}", v) and v not in out:
            out.append(v)
    return out


def evidence_cache_key(finding: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    material = "|".join([
        str(finding.get("SHA256") or "").lower(),
        str(finding.get("FileName") or "").lower(),
        ",".join(extract_cves(finding)),
    ])
    return hashlib.sha256(material.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def github_headers() -> dict[str, str]:
    headers = {
        "Accept": "application/vnd.github+json",
        "X-GitHub-Api-Version": "2022-11-28",
    }
    if GITHUB_TOKEN:
        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {GITHUB_TOKEN}"
    return headers


def github_get(path: str, params: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
    query = urllib.parse.urlencode(params)
    return fetch_json(f"https://api.github.com{path}?{query}", headers=github_headers())


def add_unique_result(target: list[dict[str, Any]], item: dict[str, Any], key: str = "url") -> None:
    value = str(item.get(key) or "")
    if not value:
        target.append(item)
        return
    if any(str(existing.get(key) or "") == value for existing in target):
        return
    target.append(item)


def fetch_github_enrichment(finding: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    name = str(finding.get("FileName") or "").strip()
    sha = str(finding.get("SHA256") or "").strip()
    cves = extract_cves(finding)

    result: dict[str, Any] = {
        "token_configured": bool(GITHUB_TOKEN),
        "repositories": [],
        "issues": [],
        "code": [],
        "advisories": [],
        "errors": [],
    }

    research_queries: list[str] = []
    if name:
        research_queries.append(f'"{name}"')
    research_queries.extend(cves[:2])

    for query in research_queries[:3]:
        try:
            data = github_get("/search/repositories", {"q": query, "sort": "updated", "order": "desc", "per_page": 5})
            for item in data.get("items", [])[:5]:
                add_unique_result(result["repositories"], {
                    "query": query,
                    "name": item.get("full_name"),
                    "description": item.get("description"),
                    "url": item.get("html_url"),
                    "stars": item.get("stargazers_count"),
                    "updated_at": item.get("updated_at"),
                })
        except Exception as exc:
            result["errors"].append(f"repository search {query}: {exc}")

        try:
            data = github_get("/search/issues", {"q": f"{query} in:title,body", "sort": "updated", "order": "desc", "per_page": 5})
            for item in data.get("items", [])[:5]:
                repo_url = str(item.get("repository_url") or "")
                add_unique_result(result["issues"], {
                    "query": query,
                    "title": item.get("title"),
                    "url": item.get("html_url"),
                    "state": item.get("state"),
                    "updated_at": item.get("updated_at"),
                    "repository_api_url": repo_url,
                })
        except Exception as exc:
            result["errors"].append(f"issue search {query}: {exc}")

    # GitHub code search is the strongest way to find exact hashes/filenames in public research
    # repositories, but GitHub may require authentication and applies stricter rate limits.
    if GITHUB_TOKEN:
        code_queries: list[str] = []
        if sha:
            code_queries.append(f'"{sha}"')
        if name:
            code_queries.append(f'"{name}"')
        for query in code_queries[:2]:
            try:
                data = github_get("/search/code", {"q": query, "per_page": 5})
                for item in data.get("items", [])[:5]:
                    repo = item.get("repository") or {}
                    add_unique_result(result["code"], {
                        "query": query,
                        "name": item.get("name"),
                        "path": item.get("path"),
                        "repository": repo.get("full_name"),
                        "url": item.get("html_url"),
                    })
            except Exception as exc:
                result["errors"].append(f"code search {query}: {exc}")
        # Metasploit-specific public research lookup; references only, never downloaded/executed.
        metasploit_queries = []
        if name:
            metasploit_queries.append(f'"{name}" repo:rapid7/metasploit-framework')
        metasploit_queries.extend(f'{cve} repo:rapid7/metasploit-framework' for cve in cves[:2])
        for query in metasploit_queries[:3]:
            try:
                data = github_get("/search/code", {"q": query, "per_page": 5})
                for item in data.get("items", [])[:5]:
                    repo = item.get("repository") or {}
                    add_unique_result(result["code"], {
                        "query": query,
                        "name": item.get("name"),
                        "path": item.get("path"),
                        "repository": repo.get("full_name"),
                        "url": item.get("html_url"),
                    })
            except Exception as exc:
                result["errors"].append(f"Metasploit code search {query}: {exc}")
    else:
        result["errors"].append("GitHub code search skipped: DRS_GITHUB_TOKEN is not configured.")

    for cve in cves[:10]:
        try:
            data = github_get("/advisories", {"cve_id": cve, "per_page": 10})
            for item in (data if isinstance(data, list) else []):
                add_unique_result(result["advisories"], {
                    "cve": cve,
                    "ghsa_id": item.get("ghsa_id"),
                    "summary": item.get("summary"),
                    "severity": item.get("severity"),
                    "url": item.get("html_url"),
                    "published_at": item.get("published_at"),
                })
        except Exception as exc:
            result["errors"].append(f"advisory lookup {cve}: {exc}")

    return result


def nvd_metric(cve: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    metrics = cve.get("metrics") or {}
    order = ["cvssMetricV40", "cvssMetricV31", "cvssMetricV30", "cvssMetricV2"]
    for key in order:
        values = metrics.get(key) or []
        if not values:
            continue
        # Prefer NVD primary where available.
        metric = next((x for x in values if str(x.get("type") or "").lower() == "primary"), values[0])
        data = metric.get("cvssData") or {}
        return {
            "version": data.get("version"),
            "score": data.get("baseScore"),
            "severity": data.get("baseSeverity") or metric.get("baseSeverity"),
            "vector": data.get("vectorString"),
        }
    return {}


def fetch_nvd_enrichment(finding: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    cves = extract_cves(finding)
    result: dict[str, Any] = {"records": [], "errors": []}
    if not cves:
        return result
    headers: dict[str, str] = {}
    if NVD_API_KEY:
        headers["apiKey"] = NVD_API_KEY
    try:
        params = urllib.parse.urlencode({"cveIds": ",".join(cves[:100])})
        data = fetch_json(f"https://services.nvd.nist.gov/rest/json/cves/2.0?{params}", headers=headers)
        for wrapper in data.get("vulnerabilities", []):
            cve = wrapper.get("cve") or {}
            descriptions = cve.get("descriptions") or []
            description = next((x.get("value") for x in descriptions if x.get("lang") == "en"), "")
            weaknesses: list[str] = []
            for block in cve.get("weaknesses") or []:
                for desc in block.get("description") or []:
                    value = str(desc.get("value") or "")
                    if value and value not in weaknesses:
                        weaknesses.append(value)
            result["records"].append({
                "cve": cve.get("id"),
                "status": cve.get("vulnStatus"),
                "published": cve.get("published"),
                "last_modified": cve.get("lastModified"),
                "description": description,
                "cvss": nvd_metric(cve),
                "weaknesses": weaknesses,
                "kev": bool(cve.get("cisaExploitAdd")),
                "cisa_exploit_add": cve.get("cisaExploitAdd"),
                "cisa_action_due": cve.get("cisaActionDue"),
                "cisa_required_action": cve.get("cisaRequiredAction"),
                "cisa_vulnerability_name": cve.get("cisaVulnerabilityName"),
                "url": f"https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/{urllib.parse.quote(str(cve.get('id') or ''))}",
            })
    except Exception as exc:
        result["errors"].append(str(exc))
    return result


def get_online_enrichment(finding: dict[str, Any], refresh: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
    key = evidence_cache_key(finding)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    if not refresh:
        with db() as c:
            row = c.execute("SELECT created_at,data_json FROM enrichment_cache WHERE evidence_key=?", (key,)).fetchone()
            if row:
                created = parse_iso(str(row["created_at"]))
                if created and now - created < timedelta(hours=max(1, ENRICHMENT_TTL_HOURS)):
                    try:
                        data = json.loads(row["data_json"])
                        data["cache"] = "hit"
                        return data
                    except Exception:
                        pass

    if not ONLINE_ENRICHMENT:
        return {
            "enabled": False,
            "cache": "none",
            "message": "Online enrichment is disabled. Set DRS_ONLINE_ENRICHMENT=true on the Collector to enable GitHub/NVD lookups.",
        }

    data: dict[str, Any] = {
        "enabled": True,
        "cache": "refresh",
        "retrieved_at": utcnow(),
        "github": fetch_github_enrichment(finding),
        "nvd": fetch_nvd_enrichment(finding),
    }
    with db_lock, db() as c:
        c.execute(
            """INSERT INTO enrichment_cache(evidence_key,created_at,data_json) VALUES(?,?,?)
               ON CONFLICT(evidence_key) DO UPDATE SET created_at=excluded.created_at,data_json=excluded.data_json""",
            (key, utcnow(), json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False)),
        )
    return data


def cleanup_retention(c: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    cutoff = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=max(1, RETENTION_DAYS))).isoformat()
    c.execute("DELETE FROM snapshots WHERE received_at < ?", (cutoff,))


@app.get("/api/v1/health")
def health() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"status": "ok", "version": APP_VERSION, "time": utcnow()}


@app.post("/api/v1/ingest", dependencies=[Depends(require_ingest_key)])
async def ingest(request: Request) -> dict[str, Any]:
    raw = await request.body()
    if len(raw) > MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES:
        raise HTTPException(413, f"Payload exceeds {MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES} bytes.")
    try:
        payload = IngestPayload.model_validate_json(raw)
    except Exception as exc:
        raise HTTPException(400, f"Invalid DriverRiskScout payload: {exc}") from exc

    report = payload.report
    host = report.get("host") or {}
    stats = report.get("statistics") or {}
    tool = report.get("tool") or {}
    name = str(host.get("computer_name") or "").strip()
    if not name:
        raise HTTPException(400, "report.host.computer_name is required.")
    agent_id = str(host.get('agent_id') or '').strip()
    hid = host_id_for(name, agent_id)
    received = utcnow()
    digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
    compressed = gzip.compress(raw, compresslevel=6)
    security_products = ((report.get("security_products") or {}).get("products") or [])
    domain = str(host.get('domain') or '')
    site = str(host.get('site') or '')
    tags = host.get('tags') or []
    protection = report.get("protection") or {}

    scan_id = str(report.get("scan_id") or "")
    with db_lock, db() as c:
        if scan_id:
            existing_snapshot = c.execute("SELECT id,payload_sha256 FROM snapshots WHERE host_id=? AND scan_id=?", (hid, scan_id)).fetchone()
            if existing_snapshot:
                return {"accepted": True, "duplicate": True, "host_id": hid, "snapshot_id": existing_snapshot["id"], "sha256": existing_snapshot["payload_sha256"]}
        current = c.execute("SELECT first_seen FROM hosts WHERE host_id=?", (hid,)).fetchone()
        first_seen = current["first_seen"] if current else received
        c.execute(
            """INSERT INTO hosts(host_id,computer_name,agent_id,domain,site,tags_json,first_seen,last_seen,os_caption,build_number,tool_version,
               critical,high,medium,known,suspicious,security_products_json,protection_json)
               VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
               ON CONFLICT(host_id) DO UPDATE SET
               computer_name=excluded.computer_name,agent_id=excluded.agent_id,domain=excluded.domain,site=excluded.site,tags_json=excluded.tags_json,last_seen=excluded.last_seen,os_caption=excluded.os_caption,
               build_number=excluded.build_number,tool_version=excluded.tool_version,critical=excluded.critical,
               high=excluded.high,medium=excluded.medium,known=excluded.known,suspicious=excluded.suspicious,
               security_products_json=excluded.security_products_json,protection_json=excluded.protection_json""",
            (
                hid, name, agent_id, domain, site, json.dumps(tags, ensure_ascii=False), first_seen, received, str(host.get("os_caption","")), str(host.get("build_number","")),
                str(tool.get("version","")), int(stats.get("critical",0)), int(stats.get("high",0)),
                int(stats.get("medium",0)), int(stats.get("known",0)), int(stats.get("suspicious",0)),
                json.dumps(security_products, ensure_ascii=False), json.dumps(protection, ensure_ascii=False),
            ),
        )
        cur = c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO snapshots(host_id,received_at,scan_time,scan_id,payload_sha256,payload_gzip) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (hid, received, str(report.get("created_at","")), scan_id, digest, compressed),
        )
        sid = cur.lastrowid
        c.execute("UPDATE hosts SET latest_snapshot_id=? WHERE host_id=?", (sid, hid))

        for f in report.get("findings") or []:
            c.execute(
                """INSERT INTO findings(snapshot_id,host_id,received_at,priority,classification,driver,path,sha256,running,score,policy_assessment,data_json)
                   VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)""",
                (
                    sid,hid,received,str(f.get("Priority","")),str(f.get("Classification","")),
                    str(f.get("FileName","")),str(f.get("Path","")),str(f.get("SHA256","")),
                    1 if f.get("Running") else 0,int(f.get("RemediationScore",0)),
                    str(f.get("PolicyAssessment","")),json.dumps(f,ensure_ascii=False)
                ),
            )
        for item in (payload.inventory or []):
            c.execute(
                """INSERT INTO driver_inventory(snapshot_id,host_id,received_at,file_name,path,sha256,state,company,file_version,running,data_json)
                   VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)""",
                (
                    sid,hid,received,str(item.get("FileName","")),str(item.get("Path","")),str(item.get("SHA256","")),
                    str(item.get("State","")),str(item.get("Company","")),str(item.get("FileVersion","")),
                    1 if str(item.get("Running","")).lower()=="true" or item.get("Running") is True else 0,
                    json.dumps(item,ensure_ascii=False)
                ),
            )
        changes = report.get("changes") or {}
        for ch in (changes.get("driver_changes") or []):
            c.execute(
                "INSERT INTO changes(snapshot_id,host_id,received_at,change_type,severity,data_json) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                (sid,hid,received,str(ch.get("Type","driver_change")),"",json.dumps(ch,ensure_ascii=False)),
            )
        for ch in (changes.get("protection_changes") or []):
            c.execute(
                "INSERT INTO changes(snapshot_id,host_id,received_at,change_type,severity,data_json) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                (sid,hid,received,"protection_changed",str(ch.get("Severity","")),json.dumps(ch,ensure_ascii=False)),
            )
        cleanup_retention(c)

    append_wazuh(payload.wazuh_events)
    return {"accepted": True, "host_id": hid, "snapshot_id": sid, "sha256": digest}


@app.get("/api/v1/hosts")
def api_hosts(_: str = Depends(require_admin)) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    with db() as c:
        rows = c.execute("SELECT * FROM hosts ORDER BY critical DESC, high DESC, known DESC, computer_name").fetchall()
        return [dict(r) for r in rows]


@app.get("/api/v1/hosts/{host_id}")
def api_host(host_id: str, _: str = Depends(require_admin)) -> dict[str, Any]:
    with db() as c:
        h = c.execute("SELECT * FROM hosts WHERE host_id=?", (host_id,)).fetchone()
        if not h:
            raise HTTPException(404, "Host not found.")
        findings = c.execute("SELECT * FROM findings WHERE host_id=? ORDER BY received_at DESC,score DESC LIMIT 500",(host_id,)).fetchall()
        changes = c.execute("SELECT * FROM changes WHERE host_id=? ORDER BY received_at DESC LIMIT 500",(host_id,)).fetchall()
        snapshots = c.execute("SELECT id,received_at,scan_time,scan_id,payload_sha256 FROM snapshots WHERE host_id=? ORDER BY received_at DESC LIMIT 100",(host_id,)).fetchall()
        return {"host":dict(h),"findings":[dict(x) for x in findings],"changes":[dict(x) for x in changes],"snapshots":[dict(x) for x in snapshots]}


@app.get("/api/v1/snapshots/{snapshot_id}")
def api_snapshot(snapshot_id: int, _: str = Depends(require_admin)) -> Response:
    with db() as c:
        row = c.execute("SELECT payload_gzip FROM snapshots WHERE id=?", (snapshot_id,)).fetchone()
        if not row:
            raise HTTPException(404, "Snapshot not found.")
        raw = gzip.decompress(row["payload_gzip"])
        return Response(raw, media_type="application/json")



@app.get("/api/v1/drivers")
def api_drivers(
    sha256: str = "",
    name: str = "",
    host_id: str = "",
    _: str = Depends(require_admin),
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    where=[];args=[]
    if sha256:
        where.append("LOWER(sha256)=LOWER(?)");args.append(sha256.strip())
    if name:
        where.append("LOWER(file_name) LIKE LOWER(?)");args.append(f"%{name.strip()}%")
    if host_id:
        where.append("host_id=?");args.append(host_id)
    sql="""SELECT di.*,h.computer_name,h.site FROM driver_inventory di
           JOIN hosts h ON h.host_id=di.host_id"""
    latest_clause = "di.snapshot_id=h.latest_snapshot_id"
    if where:
        sql += " WHERE " + latest_clause + " AND " + " AND ".join(where)
    else:
        sql += " WHERE " + latest_clause
    sql += " ORDER BY di.received_at DESC LIMIT 2000"
    with db() as c:
        return [dict(x) for x in c.execute(sql,args).fetchall()]


@app.get("/drivers", response_class=HTMLResponse)
def driver_search_page(
    request: Request,
    name: str = "",
    sha256: str = "",
    _: str = Depends(require_admin),
) -> str:
    rows=[]
    if name or sha256:
        where=[];args=[]
        if sha256:
            where.append("LOWER(di.sha256)=LOWER(?)");args.append(sha256.strip())
        if name:
            where.append("LOWER(di.file_name) LIKE LOWER(?)");args.append(f"%{name.strip()}%")
        sql="""SELECT di.*,h.computer_name,h.site FROM driver_inventory di
               JOIN hosts h ON h.host_id=di.host_id WHERE di.snapshot_id=h.latest_snapshot_id AND """+" AND ".join(where)+" ORDER BY di.received_at DESC LIMIT 500"
        with db() as c:
            rows=c.execute(sql,args).fetchall()
    body=""
    for x in rows:
        body += f"<tr><td>{esc(x['computer_name'])}</td><td>{esc(x['site'])}</td><td>{esc(x['file_name'])}</td><td class='small'>{esc(x['path'])}</td><td class='small'>{esc(x['sha256'])}</td><td>{esc(x['state'])}</td><td>{bool(x['running'])}</td><td>{esc(x['company'])}</td><td>{esc(x['file_version'])}</td><td>{esc(x['received_at'])}</td></tr>"
    return f"""<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Driver Search</title>{CSS}</head><body>
    <p><a href="/">← Overview</a></p><h1>Fleet Driver Search</h1>
    <form method="get"><label>Name <input name="name" value="{esc(name)}"></label>
    <label> SHA-256 <input name="sha256" size="70" value="{esc(sha256)}"></label>
    <button type="submit">Search</button></form>
    <table><tr><th>Host</th><th>Site</th><th>Driver</th><th>Path</th><th>SHA256</th><th>State</th><th>Running</th><th>Vendor</th><th>Version</th><th>Seen</th></tr>{body}</table>
    </body></html>"""



@app.get("/api/v1/findings/{finding_id}")
def api_finding(
    finding_id: int,
    refresh: bool = False,
    _: str = Depends(require_admin),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    with db() as c:
        row = c.execute(
            """SELECT f.*,h.computer_name,h.site,h.os_caption,h.build_number
               FROM findings f JOIN hosts h ON h.host_id=f.host_id WHERE f.id=?""",
            (finding_id,),
        ).fetchone()
        if not row:
            raise HTTPException(404, "Finding not found.")
    finding = finding_extra(row["data_json"])
    return {
        "finding": dict(row),
        "evidence": finding,
        "online_enrichment": get_online_enrichment(finding, refresh=refresh),
    }


def html_source_rows(sources: Any) -> str:
    rows = ""
    for source in sources if isinstance(sources, list) else []:
        url = esc(source.get("Url") or source.get("url") or "")
        name = esc(source.get("Name") or source.get("name") or "Source")
        kind = esc(source.get("Kind") or source.get("kind") or "")
        trust = esc(source.get("Trust") or source.get("trust") or "")
        query = esc(source.get("Query") or source.get("query") or "")
        link = f'<a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="{url}">{name}</a>' if url else name
        rows += f"<tr><td>{link}</td><td>{kind}</td><td>{trust}</td><td class='small'>{query}</td></tr>"
    return rows


def html_evidence_rows(evidence: Any) -> str:
    rows = ""
    for item in evidence if isinstance(evidence, list) else []:
        rows += (
            f"<tr><td>{esc(item.get('Type',''))}</td><td>{esc(item.get('Source',''))}</td>"
            f"<td>{esc(item.get('Confidence',''))}</td><td>{esc(item.get('Evidence',''))}</td></tr>"
        )
    return rows


def html_exploit_intel(matches: Any) -> str:
    rows = ""
    for item in matches if isinstance(matches, list) else []:
        url = esc(item.get("Url") or item.get("url") or "")
        link = f"<a target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' href='{url}'>open research</a>" if url else ""
        rows += (
            f"<tr><td>{esc(item.get('CVE',''))}</td><td>{esc(item.get('Type',''))}</td>"
            f"<td>{esc(item.get('CVSS',''))}</td><td>{esc(item.get('SourceVerifiedFlag',''))}</td>"
            f"<td class='small'>{esc(item.get('Description',''))}</td>"
            f"<td class='small'>{esc(item.get('Applicability','Affected version/build not established'))}</td><td>{link}</td></tr>"
        )
    if not rows:
        return "<p class='muted'>No bundled exploit-intelligence mapping for this finding.</p>"
    return (
        "<p class='muted'>Bundled records are research mappings. The source Verified flag is source metadata, "
        "not independent DriverRiskScout validation. Filename mappings do not prove the current version/build is vulnerable.</p>"
        "<table><tr><th>CVE</th><th>Type</th><th>CVSS</th><th>Source Verified</th>"
        "<th>Description</th><th>Applicability</th><th>Reference</th></tr>" + rows + "</table>"
    )


def html_commands(commands: Any) -> str:
    body = ""
    for item in commands if isinstance(commands, list) else []:
        body += (
            f"<section class='command'><h3>{esc(item.get('Title',''))}</h3>"
            f"<p>{esc(item.get('Purpose',''))}</p><pre>{esc(item.get('Command',''))}</pre></section>"
        )
    return body


def html_github(online: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    github = online.get("github") or {}
    if not github:
        return f"<p class='muted'>{esc(online.get('message','No GitHub data available.'))}</p>"
    parts = [
        f"<p>GitHub token configured: <b>{bool(github.get('token_configured'))}</b>. "
        "Repositories/issues/advisories are research leads; they do not prove endpoint exploitability.</p>"
    ]
    repos = github.get("repositories") or []
    if repos:
        rows = ""
        for item in repos:
            rows += (
                f"<tr><td><a target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' href='{esc(item.get('url'))}'>{esc(item.get('name'))}</a></td>"
                f"<td>{esc(item.get('query'))}</td><td>{esc(item.get('stars'))}</td>"
                f"<td>{esc(item.get('updated_at'))}</td><td class='small'>{esc(item.get('description'))}</td></tr>"
            )
        parts.append(f"<h3>Repositories</h3><table><tr><th>Repository</th><th>Query</th><th>Stars</th><th>Updated</th><th>Description</th></tr>{rows}</table>")
    issues = github.get("issues") or []
    if issues:
        rows = ""
        for item in issues:
            rows += (
                f"<tr><td><a target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' href='{esc(item.get('url'))}'>{esc(item.get('title'))}</a></td>"
                f"<td>{esc(item.get('query'))}</td><td>{esc(item.get('state'))}</td><td>{esc(item.get('updated_at'))}</td></tr>"
            )
        parts.append(f"<h3>Issues / pull-request search</h3><table><tr><th>Title</th><th>Query</th><th>State</th><th>Updated</th></tr>{rows}</table>")
    code = github.get("code") or []
    if code:
        rows = ""
        for item in code:
            rows += (
                f"<tr><td><a target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' href='{esc(item.get('url'))}'>{esc(item.get('repository'))}/{esc(item.get('path'))}</a></td>"
                f"<td>{esc(item.get('query'))}</td></tr>"
            )
        parts.append(f"<h3>Exact code search</h3><table><tr><th>Match</th><th>Query</th></tr>{rows}</table>")
    advisories = github.get("advisories") or []
    if advisories:
        rows = ""
        for item in advisories:
            rows += (
                f"<tr><td><a target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' href='{esc(item.get('url'))}'>{esc(item.get('ghsa_id'))}</a></td>"
                f"<td>{esc(item.get('cve'))}</td><td>{esc(item.get('severity'))}</td><td class='small'>{esc(item.get('summary'))}</td></tr>"
            )
        parts.append(f"<h3>GitHub Advisory Database</h3><table><tr><th>GHSA</th><th>CVE</th><th>Severity</th><th>Summary</th></tr>{rows}</table>")
    errors = github.get("errors") or []
    if errors:
        parts.append("<details><summary>GitHub lookup notes/errors</summary><pre>" + esc("\n".join(str(x) for x in errors)) + "</pre></details>")
    if not any((repos, issues, code, advisories)):
        parts.append("<p class='muted'>No live GitHub API matches were returned for the current filename/hash/CVE queries.</p>")
    return "".join(parts)


def html_nvd(online: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    nvd = online.get("nvd") or {}
    records = nvd.get("records") or []
    if not records:
        errors = nvd.get("errors") or []
        return "<p class='muted'>" + esc("; ".join(str(x) for x in errors) or "No NVD/CVE records returned.") + "</p>"
    rows = ""
    for item in records:
        cvss = item.get("cvss") or {}
        kev = "YES" if item.get("kev") else "NO"
        cisa = ""
        if item.get("kev"):
            cisa = f"Added {item.get('cisa_exploit_add') or ''}; due {item.get('cisa_action_due') or ''}; {item.get('cisa_required_action') or ''}"
        rows += (
            f"<tr><td><a target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' href='{esc(item.get('url'))}'>{esc(item.get('cve'))}</a></td>"
            f"<td>{esc(cvss.get('score'))} {esc(cvss.get('severity'))}</td><td>{kev}</td>"
            f"<td>{esc(', '.join(item.get('weaknesses') or []))}</td><td class='small'>{esc(item.get('description'))}</td>"
            f"<td class='small'>{esc(cisa)}</td></tr>"
        )
    return f"<table><tr><th>CVE</th><th>CVSS</th><th>CISA KEV</th><th>CWE</th><th>Description</th><th>KEV details</th></tr>{rows}</table>"


@app.get("/finding/{finding_id}", response_class=HTMLResponse)
def finding_page(
    finding_id: int,
    refresh: bool = False,
    _: str = Depends(require_admin),
) -> str:
    with db() as c:
        row = c.execute(
            """SELECT f.*,h.computer_name,h.site,h.os_caption,h.build_number
               FROM findings f JOIN hosts h ON h.host_id=f.host_id WHERE f.id=?""",
            (finding_id,),
        ).fetchone()
        if not row:
            raise HTTPException(404, "Finding not found.")

    finding = finding_extra(row["data_json"])
    online = get_online_enrichment(finding, refresh=refresh)

    evidence_rows = html_evidence_rows(finding.get("MatchEvidence"))
    source_rows = html_source_rows(finding.get("InvestigationSources"))
    command_html = html_commands(finding.get("SafeValidationCommands"))
    github_html = html_github(online)
    nvd_html = html_nvd(online)
    exploit_intel_html = html_exploit_intel(finding.get("ExploitIntelMatches"))

    return f"""<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>{esc(row['driver'])} Investigation</title>{CSS}</head><body>
    <p><a href="/host/{esc(row['host_id'])}">← {esc(row['computer_name'])}</a> &nbsp; <a href="/">Overview</a></p>
    <h1>Vulnerability Investigation: {esc(row['driver'])}</h1>
    <p class="muted">{esc(row['computer_name'])} / {esc(row['site'])} — {esc(row['os_caption'])} build {esc(row['build_number'])}</p>

    <div class="cards">
      <div class="card"><b>{esc(finding.get('CurrentExploitabilityScore',''))}</b><span>Current exploitability<br>{esc(finding.get('CurrentExploitabilityLevel',''))}</span></div>
      <div class="card"><b>{esc(finding.get('PotentialIfLoadedScore',''))}</b><span>Potential if loaded<br>{esc(finding.get('PotentialIfLoadedLevel',''))}</span></div>
      <div class="card"><b>{esc(finding.get('PrivilegeEscalationScore',''))}</b><span>Privilege escalation</span></div>
      <div class="card"><b>{esc(finding.get('VulnerabilityConfidence',''))}</b><span>Vulnerability confidence</span></div>
      <div class="card"><b>{esc(finding.get('ResearchMaturityScore',0))}</b><span>Research maturity</span></div>
      <div class="card"><b>{esc(finding.get('ExploitIntelMatchCount',0))}</b><span>Exploit-intel mappings</span></div>
    </div>

    <div class="warning"><b>Read-only investigation:</b> {esc(finding.get('InvestigationSafetyNote') or 'No exploit execution, driver loading or IOCTL attack is performed.')}</div>

    <h2>Local evidence</h2>
    <div class="kv">
      <div>Path</div><div class="small">{esc(finding.get('Path'))}</div>
      <div>SHA256</div><div class="small"><code>{esc(finding.get('SHA256'))}</code></div>
      <div>Vendor / version</div><div>{esc(finding.get('Company'))} / {esc(finding.get('FileVersion'))}</div>
      <div>Match class</div><div>{esc(finding.get('Classification'))} — {esc(finding.get('PatchAssessment'))}</div>
      <div>State / running</div><div>{esc(finding.get('State'))} / {esc(finding.get('Running'))}</div>
      <div>Driver service(s)</div><div>{esc(finding.get('DriverServices'))}</div>
      <div>Reachability</div><div>{esc(finding.get('Reachability'))}; low privilege access: {esc(finding.get('LowPrivilegeAccess'))}</div>
      <div>Observed device(s)</div><div>{esc(finding.get('PresentDevices'))}</div>
      <div>Broad device ACL</div><div>{esc(finding.get('BroadDeviceAcl'))}</div>
      <div>Primitive</div><div>{esc(finding.get('Primitive'))}</div>
      <div>Privilege assessment</div><div>{esc(finding.get('PrivilegeEscalationAssessment'))}</div>
      <div>CVE(s)</div><div>{esc(', '.join(finding.get('CVEList') or []))}</div>
      <div>Exploit-intel type(s)</div><div>{esc(', '.join(finding.get('ExploitIntelTypes') or []))}</div>
      <div>Exploit-intel max CVSS</div><div>{esc(finding.get('ExploitIntelMaxCVSS',0))}</div>
      <div>Public PoC/research ref</div><div>{esc(finding.get('HasPublicPoCReference',False))}</div>
      <div>Research maturity</div><div>{esc(finding.get('ResearchMaturityScore',0))} / 100</div>
      <div>Policy assessment</div><div>{esc(finding.get('PolicyAssessment'))} — {esc(finding.get('PolicyReason'))}</div>
    </div>

    <h2>Lab validation telemetry</h2>
    <div class="kv">
      <div>Validation readiness</div><div>{esc(finding.get('LabValidationReadinessScore',''))} / 100</div>
      <div>Runtime status</div><div>{esc(finding.get('RuntimeStatus',''))}</div>
      <div>Registered service state</div><div>{esc(finding.get('RegisteredServiceState',''))}</div>
      <div>Code Integrity matches</div><div>{esc(finding.get('MatchingCodeIntegrityEvents',''))}</div>
      <div>Driver install/service matches</div><div>{esc(finding.get('MatchingDriverInstallEvents',''))}</div>
      <div>Sysmon driver-load matches</div><div>{esc(finding.get('MatchingSysmonDriverLoadEvents',''))}</div>
      <div>Block evidence</div><div>{esc(finding.get('BlockEvidence',''))} ({esc(finding.get('BlockEvidenceConfidence',''))})</div>
    </div>
    <p class="muted">{esc(finding.get('SafeConclusion',''))}</p>

    <h2>Evidence chain</h2>
    <table><tr><th>Match type</th><th>Source</th><th>Confidence</th><th>Evidence</th></tr>{evidence_rows}</table>

    <h2>Bundled exploit intelligence</h2>
    {exploit_intel_html}

    <h2>Source links</h2>
    <p class="muted">Exact hash evidence outranks filename-only or heuristic hits. GitHub research is secondary evidence and is never used alone to declare a driver exploitable.</p>
    <table><tr><th>Source</th><th>Type</th><th>Trust / purpose</th><th>Query</th></tr>{source_rows}</table>

    <h2>Live GitHub research</h2>
    <p><a href="/finding/{finding_id}?refresh=true">Refresh online sources</a> &nbsp; Cache: {esc(online.get('cache','none'))}</p>
    {github_html}

    <h2>NVD / CISA KEV enrichment</h2>
    {nvd_html}

    <h2>Assessment</h2>
    <p>{esc(finding.get('InvestigationConclusion'))}</p>
    <p><b>Exploitability rationale:</b> {esc(finding.get('ExploitabilityWhy'))}</p>
    <p><b>Recommended remediation:</b> {esc(finding.get('RecommendedAction'))}</p>

    <h2>Safe validation commands</h2>
    <p class="muted">These commands only inspect file/service state. No sc.exe create/start, no DeviceIoControl and no PoC execution are generated.</p>
    {command_html}
    </body></html>"""


def finding_extra(data_json: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        return json.loads(data_json or "{}")
    except Exception:
        return {}


def esc(value: Any) -> str:
    import html
    return html.escape(str(value if value is not None else ""))


def badge(priority: str) -> str:
    p=priority.lower()
    cls="crit" if p=="critical" else "high" if p=="high" else "med" if p=="medium" else "ok"
    return f'<span class="badge {cls}">{esc(priority)}</span>'


@app.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
def dashboard(_: str = Depends(require_admin)) -> str:
    with db() as c:
        hosts = c.execute("SELECT * FROM hosts ORDER BY critical DESC, high DESC, known DESC, computer_name").fetchall()
        counts = c.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) hosts,SUM(critical) critical,SUM(high) high,SUM(medium) medium,SUM(known) known FROM hosts").fetchone()
        latest_findings = c.execute(
            """SELECT f.host_id,f.data_json FROM findings f
               JOIN hosts h ON h.host_id=f.host_id
               WHERE f.snapshot_id=h.latest_snapshot_id"""
        ).fetchall()

    host_risk: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
    for item in latest_findings:
        extra=finding_extra(item["data_json"])
        bucket=host_risk.setdefault(item["host_id"], {"exploitability":0,"privesc":0,"research":0,"poc_refs":0})
        try: bucket["exploitability"]=max(bucket["exploitability"],int(extra.get("CurrentExploitabilityScore") or 0))
        except Exception: pass
        try: bucket["privesc"]=max(bucket["privesc"],int(extra.get("PrivilegeEscalationScore") or 0))
        except Exception: pass
        try: bucket["research"]=max(bucket["research"],int(extra.get("ResearchMaturityScore") or 0))
        except Exception: pass
        try:
            if extra.get("HasPublicPoCReference"):
                bucket["poc_refs"] += 1
        except Exception:
            pass

    rows=""
    high_exploit_hosts=sum(1 for v in host_risk.values() if v["exploitability"] >= 70)
    high_privesc_hosts=sum(1 for v in host_risk.values() if v["privesc"] >= 70)
    for h in hosts:
        active=[]
        try:
            for p in json.loads(h["security_products_json"] or "[]"):
                if p.get("Active") or p.get("active"):
                    active.append(p.get("Product") or p.get("product"))
        except Exception:
            pass
        risk=host_risk.get(h["host_id"], {"exploitability":0,"privesc":0,"research":0,"poc_refs":0})
        rows += f"""<tr><td><a href="/host/{esc(h['host_id'])}">{esc(h['computer_name'])}</a></td>
        <td>{esc(h['site'])}</td><td>{esc(h['os_caption'])} {esc(h['build_number'])}</td><td>{esc(h['last_seen'])}</td>
        <td>{h['critical']}</td><td>{h['high']}</td><td>{h['medium']}</td><td>{h['known']}</td>
        <td><b>{risk['exploitability']}</b></td><td><b>{risk['privesc']}</b></td>
        <td><b>{risk['research']}</b></td><td>{risk['poc_refs']}</td>
        <td>{esc(', '.join(active))}</td></tr>"""
    return f"""<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>DriverRiskScout Collector</title>{CSS}</head><body>
    <h1>DriverRiskScout Collector <span class="muted">v{APP_VERSION}</span></h1>
    <div class="cards"><div class="card"><b>{counts['hosts'] or 0}</b><span>Hosts</span></div>
    <div class="card crit"><b>{counts['critical'] or 0}</b><span>Critical</span></div>
    <div class="card high"><b>{counts['high'] or 0}</b><span>High</span></div>
    <div class="card med"><b>{counts['medium'] or 0}</b><span>Medium</span></div>
    <div class="card"><b>{counts['known'] or 0}</b><span>Known</span></div>
    <div class="card high"><b>{high_exploit_hosts}</b><span>Hosts Exploitability ≥70</span></div>
    <div class="card high"><b>{high_privesc_hosts}</b><span>Hosts PrivEsc ≥70</span></div></div>
    <p><a href="/drivers">Fleet Driver Search</a></p><h2>Endpoints</h2><table><tr><th>Host</th><th>Site</th><th>OS</th><th>Last seen</th><th>Critical</th><th>High</th><th>Medium</th><th>Known</th><th>Max Exploitability</th><th>Max PrivEsc</th><th>Max Research</th><th>PoC refs</th><th>Active AV/EDR</th></tr>{rows}</table>
    </body></html>"""


@app.get("/host/{host_id}", response_class=HTMLResponse)
def host_page(host_id: str, _: str = Depends(require_admin)) -> str:
    with db() as c:
        h = c.execute("SELECT * FROM hosts WHERE host_id=?", (host_id,)).fetchone()
        if not h:
            raise HTTPException(404,"Host not found.")
        f = c.execute("SELECT * FROM findings WHERE host_id=? ORDER BY received_at DESC,score DESC LIMIT 100",(host_id,)).fetchall()
        ch = c.execute("SELECT * FROM changes WHERE host_id=? ORDER BY received_at DESC LIMIT 100",(host_id,)).fetchall()
        research_leads=[]
        if h["latest_snapshot_id"]:
            snap=c.execute("SELECT payload_gzip FROM snapshots WHERE id=?",(h["latest_snapshot_id"],)).fetchone()
            if snap:
                try:
                    payload=json.loads(gzip.decompress(snap["payload_gzip"]).decode("utf-8"))
                    research_leads=(((payload.get("report") or {}).get("exploit_intel") or {}).get("inventory_leads") or [])
                except Exception:
                    research_leads=[]
    frows=""
    for x in f:
        extra=finding_extra(x['data_json'])
        frows += f"<tr><td>{esc(x['received_at'])}</td><td>{badge(x['priority'] or '')}</td><td><a href='/finding/{x['id']}'>{esc(x['driver'])}</a></td><td>{esc(x['classification'])}</td><td>{x['score']}</td><td>{bool(x['running'])}</td><td><b>{esc(extra.get('CurrentExploitabilityScore',''))}</b> {esc(extra.get('CurrentExploitabilityLevel',''))}</td><td>{esc(extra.get('PotentialIfLoadedScore',''))}</td><td>{esc(extra.get('PrivilegeEscalationScore',''))}</td><td>{esc(extra.get('ResearchMaturityScore',''))}</td><td>{esc(extra.get('ExploitIntelMaxCVSS',''))}</td><td>{esc(extra.get('HasPublicPoCReference',''))}</td><td>{esc(extra.get('Reachability',''))}</td><td class='small'>{esc(extra.get('PrivilegeEscalationAssessment',''))}</td><td class='small'>{esc(x['policy_assessment'])}</td></tr>"
    leadrows=""
    for lead in research_leads[:200]:
        leadrows += (
            f"<tr><td>{esc(lead.get('FileName',''))}</td><td>{esc(lead.get('FileVersion',''))}</td>"
            f"<td>{esc(lead.get('CVE',''))}</td><td>{esc(lead.get('Type',''))}</td><td>{esc(lead.get('CVSS',''))}</td>"
            f"<td class='small'>{esc(lead.get('Applicability',''))}</td></tr>"
        )
    crows=""
    for x in ch:
        crows += f"<tr><td>{esc(x['received_at'])}</td><td>{esc(x['change_type'])}</td><td>{esc(x['severity'])}</td><td class='small'>{esc(x['data_json'])}</td></tr>"
    return f"""<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>{esc(h['computer_name'])}</title>{CSS}</head><body>
    <p><a href="/">← Overview</a></p><h1>{esc(h['computer_name'])}</h1>
    <p class="muted">{esc(h['os_caption'])} build {esc(h['build_number'])} — last seen {esc(h['last_seen'])}</p>
    <h2>Latest protection</h2><pre>{esc(json.dumps(json.loads(h['protection_json'] or '{}'),indent=2,ensure_ascii=False))}</pre>
    <h2>Latest security products</h2><pre>{esc(json.dumps(json.loads(h['security_products_json'] or '[]'),indent=2,ensure_ascii=False))}</pre>
    <h2>Findings history</h2><table><tr><th>Received</th><th>Priority</th><th>Driver</th><th>Class</th><th>Remediation</th><th>Running</th><th>Exploitability</th><th>Potential if loaded</th><th>PrivEsc</th><th>Research</th><th>Max CVSS</th><th>PoC ref</th><th>Reachability</th><th>Privilege assessment</th><th>Policy</th></tr>{frows}</table>
    <h2>Inventory research leads</h2>
    <p class="muted">Research-only filename/CVE mappings from the supplied database; affected installed version/build is not proven by these rows.</p>
    <table><tr><th>Driver</th><th>Version</th><th>CVE</th><th>Type</th><th>CVSS</th><th>Applicability</th></tr>{leadrows}</table>
    <h2>Changes</h2><table><tr><th>Received</th><th>Type</th><th>Severity</th><th>Data</th></tr>{crows}</table></body></html>"""


CSS = """<style>

`Server/driver-riskscout-collector.service.example`:

```example
[Unit]
Description=DriverRiskScout Collector
After=network-online.target
Wants=network-online.target

[Service]
Type=simple
User=drs
Group=drs
WorkingDirectory=/opt/DriverRiskScout/Server
EnvironmentFile=/etc/driver-riskscout-collector.env
ExecStart=/opt/DriverRiskScout/venv/bin/python /opt/DriverRiskScout/Server/run.py
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=5
NoNewPrivileges=true
PrivateTmp=true
ProtectSystem=strict
ProtectHome=true
ReadWritePaths=/var/lib/driver-riskscout

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target

```

`Server/run.py`:

```py
import os
import uvicorn

if __name__ == "__main__":
    uvicorn.run(
        "app:app",
        host=os.getenv("DRS_BIND", "0.0.0.0"),
        port=int(os.getenv("DRS_PORT", "8088")),
        workers=int(os.getenv("DRS_WORKERS", "1")),
        proxy_headers=True,
    )

`Server/test_server.py`:

```py
import importlib, os
from pathlib import Path

def test_ingest_and_dashboard(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("DRS_DATA_DIR", str(tmp_path))
    monkeypatch.setenv("DRS_DB_PATH", str(tmp_path/"test.sqlite3"))
    monkeypatch.setenv("DRS_WAZUH_PATH", str(tmp_path/"wazuh.ndjson"))
    monkeypatch.setenv("DRS_INGEST_API_KEY", "test-ingest")
    monkeypatch.setenv("DRS_ADMIN_USER", "admin")
    monkeypatch.setenv("DRS_ADMIN_PASSWORD", "test-admin")
    monkeypatch.setenv("DRS_ONLINE_ENRICHMENT", "false")

    import app as appmod
    appmod = importlib.reload(appmod)
    appmod.init_db()
    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
    client=TestClient(appmod.app)

    payload={
      "schema_version":"2.3",
      "report":{
        "scan_id":"scan-1","created_at":"2026-08-09T13:00:00+02:00",
        "tool":{"name":"DriverRiskScout","version":"2.3.0","mode":"Agent"},
        "host":{"computer_name":"PC01","os_caption":"Windows 11 Pro","build_number":"26200"},
        "statistics":{"files":900,"known":1,"suspicious":0,"critical":0,"high":0,"medium":1},
        "protection":{"HVCIRegistryEnabled":True},
        "security_products":{"products":[{"Product":"Microsoft Defender Antivirus","Active":True}]},
        "exploit_intel":{
          "enabled":True,
          "inventory_leads":[{
            "FileName":"afd.sys","FileVersion":"test","CVE":"CVE-2023-21768",
            "Type":"Local-Privilege-Escalation","CVSS":7.8,
            "Applicability":"Research lead only: filename mapping matched; affected version/build is not established"
          }]
        },
        "findings":[{
          "Priority":"Medium",
          "Classification":"Known",
          "FileName":"IOMap64.sys",
          "Path":"C:\\Windows\\System32\\drivers\\IOMap64.sys",
          "SHA256":"e"*64,
          "Running":False,
          "State":"File only",
          "RemediationScore":44,
          "PolicyAssessment":"Unknown",
          "CurrentExploitabilityScore":30,
          "CurrentExploitabilityLevel":"Guarded",
          "PotentialIfLoadedScore":60,
          "PotentialIfLoadedLevel":"Elevated",
          "PrivilegeEscalationScore":20,
          "VulnerabilityConfidence":"Very High",
          "Reachability":"Not currently active",
          "LowPrivilegeAccess":"Not established",
          "Primitive":"See intelligence source",
          "PrivilegeEscalationAssessment":"Known vulnerable sample, but privilege-escalation primitive is not established by current metadata",
          "CVEList":["CVE-2024-41498"],
          "CVEs":"CVE-2024-41498",
          "DriverServices":"IOMap64",
          "PatchAssessment":"Exact known-vulnerable sample hash present",
          "InvestigationConclusion":"Known vulnerability evidence is present, but the driver is not currently running.",
          "InvestigationSafetyNote":"Read-only validation only.",
          "ExploitIntelMatchCount":1,
          "ExploitIntelCves":["CVE-2024-41498"],
          "ExploitIntelTypes":["Local-Privilege-Escalation"],
          "ExploitIntelMaxCVSS":7.8,
          "HasPublicPoCReference":True,
          "ResearchMaturityScore":85,
          "ExploitIntelMatches":[{
            "CVE":"CVE-2024-41498",
            "Type":"Local-Privilege-Escalation",
            "CVSS":7.8,
            "SourceVerifiedFlag":True,
            "Description":"Test research mapping",
            "Applicability":"Affected driver filename mapping only; affected version/build is not provided by this database",
            "Url":"https://github.com/example/research"
          }],
          "MatchEvidence":[{"Type":"Exact SHA256","Source":"LOLDrivers","Confidence":"Very High","Evidence":"Exact hash"}],
          "InvestigationSources":[{"Name":"GitHub - exact SHA256 search","Kind":"Research","Trust":"Secondary","Url":"https://github.com/search?q=test&type=code","Query":"hash"}],
          "SafeValidationCommands":[{"Title":"Hash","Purpose":"Verify hash","Command":"Get-FileHash C:\\Windows\\System32\\drivers\\IOMap64.sys -Algorithm SHA256"}]
        }],
        "changes":{"driver_changes":[],"protection_changes":[]}
      },
      "wazuh_events":[{"driver_risk_scout":{"type":"summary","computer":"PC01"}}],
      "inventory":[{"FileName":"IOMap64.sys","Path":"C:\\Windows\\System32\\drivers\\IOMap64.sys","SHA256":"e"*64,"State":"File only","Company":"ASUS","FileVersion":"2.50","Running":False}]
    }
    r=client.post("/api/v1/ingest",headers={"X-DRS-API-Key":"test-ingest"},json=payload)
    assert r.status_code==200, r.text
    r2=client.post("/api/v1/ingest",headers={"X-DRS-API-Key":"test-ingest"},json=payload)
    assert r2.status_code==200
    assert r2.json().get("duplicate") is True
    r=client.get("/api/v1/hosts",auth=("admin","test-admin"))
    assert r.status_code==200
    assert r.json()[0]["computer_name"]=="PC01"
    r=client.get("/",auth=("admin","test-admin"))
    assert r.status_code==200
    assert "PC01" in r.text
    r=client.get("/api/v1/drivers?name=IOMap64",auth=("admin","test-admin"))
    assert r.status_code==200
    assert r.json()[0]["file_name"]=="IOMap64.sys"

    host=client.get("/api/v1/hosts",auth=("admin","test-admin")).json()[0]
    hp=client.get(f"/host/{host['host_id']}",auth=("admin","test-admin"))
    assert hp.status_code==200
    assert "Inventory research leads" in hp.text
    assert "CVE-2023-21768" in hp.text

    host=client.get("/api/v1/hosts",auth=("admin","test-admin")).json()[0]
    details=client.get(f"/api/v1/hosts/{host['host_id']}",auth=("admin","test-admin")).json()
    finding_id=details["findings"][0]["id"]
    r=client.get(f"/finding/{finding_id}",auth=("admin","test-admin"))
    assert r.status_code==200
    assert "Vulnerability Investigation" in r.text
    assert "GitHub - exact SHA256 search" in r.text
    assert "Safe validation commands" in r.text
    assert "Bundled exploit intelligence" in r.text
    assert "Test research mapping" in r.text
    assert "Research maturity" in r.text
    r=client.get(f"/api/v1/findings/{finding_id}",auth=("admin","test-admin"))
    assert r.status_code==200
    assert r.json()["online_enrichment"]["enabled"] is False

```

`THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md`:

```md
# Third-party notices and external data

DriverRiskScout can consume or correlate public/security-vendor data at runtime, including LOLDrivers, Microsoft vulnerable-driver block rules and optional NVD data. Those data sources retain their own terms and ownership.

The Collector uses Python packages installed from `Server/requirements.lock.txt`, including FastAPI, Uvicorn and Pydantic; their respective package licenses apply.

The curated BYOVD detection profiles contain detection metadata (for example hashes, file/device indicators and public source links) derived from public defensive/research references. DriverRiskScout does not bundle or execute the offensive binaries/projects referenced by those profiles.

```

`VERSION`:

```
2.3.5

`Wazuh/111_driver_risk_scout_v2_rules.xml`:

```xml
<group name="driver_risk_scout,windows,vulnerability_detection,">
  <rule id="111000" level="0">
    <decoded_as>json</decoded_as>
    <field name="driver_risk_scout.type">.+</field>
    <description>DriverRiskScout v2 JSON event grouping.</description>
    <options>no_full_log</options>
  </rule>

  <rule id="111010" level="3">
    <if_sid>111000</if_sid>

    <group>driver_inventory,</group>
  </rule>

  </rule>

  <rule id="111030" level="5">

  <rule id="111031" level="9">

    <group>driver_risk,exploit_intel,research_reference,</group>
  </rule>

</group>

`Wazuh/README-WAZUH.md`:

```md
# Wazuh Integration

DriverRiskScout schreibt JSON Lines und benötigt keinen eigenen Decoder, solange Wazuh den eingebauten JSON-Decoder verwendet.

## Variante A – jeder Windows-Agent sendet direkt an Wazuh

Auf dem Windows-Agent den Inhalt von `agent-localfile.xml` in `<ossec_config>` einfügen.

Quelle:

```text
C:\ProgramData\DriverRiskScout\wazuh-driver-findings.ndjson
```

Danach Wazuh-Agent neu starten.

## Variante B – zentraler DriverRiskScout Collector

Der Collector schreibt:

```text
/data/wazuh/driver-risk-scout.ndjson
```

Liegt der Wazuh Manager auf demselben Host, kann diese Datei mit `collector-localfile.xml` überwacht werden.

Bei getrennten Hosts die Datei über eine geeignete, authentisierte Log-Pipeline an Wazuh übertragen.

## Manager Rules

Kopieren:

```text
111_driver_risk_scout_v2_rules.xml
```

nach:

```text
/var/ossec/etc/rules/
```

Wazuh Manager neu starten.

## Regeln testen

```bash
/var/ossec/bin/wazuh-logtest
```

Eine Zeile aus `sample-events.ndjson` einfügen.

## Relevante Rule IDs

- 111010 Scan Summary
- 111020 Medium
- 111021 High
- 111022 Critical
- 111023 Known + Running
- 111024 Microsoft Blocklist Exact
- 111030 New Driver
- 111031 Driver Hash Changed
- 111040 Protection Changed
- 111041 Critical Protection Regression
- 111050 Security Product Added
- 111051 Security Product Removed

## Hinweis zum ersten Lauf

Der erste First-Seen-Lauf erzeugt erwartungsgemäß viele `driver_new`-Events. Vor produktiver Alerting-Eskalation sollte eine Baseline erstellt werden.


## Exploitability Rules (2.1)

- 111026: Current Exploitability Level High/Critical
- 111027: privilege-escalation-relevanter Primitive aus Threat Intelligence

Neue Felder:
- driver_risk_scout.exploitability_score
- driver_risk_scout.exploitability_level
- driver_risk_scout.potential_if_loaded_score
- driver_risk_scout.privilege_escalation_score
- driver_risk_scout.privilege_escalation_assessment
- driver_risk_scout.reachability
- driver_risk_scout.primitive

## Exploit Intelligence 2.3

- `111029`: a public exploit/research reference exists for a finding.

This is intentionally a moderate research signal and does not mean the endpoint is exploitable.

```

`Wazuh/agent-localfile.xml`:

```xml
<!-- Add inside <ossec_config> on the Windows Wazuh agent. -->
<localfile>
  <location>C:\ProgramData\DriverRiskScout\wazuh-driver-findings.ndjson</location>
  <log_format>json</log_format>
  <label key="@source">driver_risk_scout</label>
</localfile>

```

`Wazuh/collector-localfile.xml`:

```xml
<!-- Optional: if DriverRiskScout Collector and Wazuh Manager share the same Linux host,
     monitor the Collector's central Wazuh NDJSON output. Adapt path to the bind mount. -->
<localfile>
  <location>/opt/DriverRiskScout/server-data/wazuh/driver-risk-scout.ndjson</location>
  <log_format>json</log_format>
  <label key="@source">driver_risk_scout_collector</label>
</localfile>

```

name: driver-riskscout

services:
  driver-riskscout-collector:
    build: ./Server
    container_name: driver-riskscout-collector

    environment:
      DRS_DATA_DIR: /data
      DRS_INGEST_API_KEY: ${DRS_INGEST_API_KEY}

      DRS_ADMIN_PASSWORD: ${DRS_ADMIN_PASSWORD}
      DRS_RETENTION_DAYS: ${DRS_RETENTION_DAYS:-90}
      DRS_MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES: ${DRS_MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES:-26214400}
      DRS_ONLINE_ENRICHMENT: ${DRS_ONLINE_ENRICHMENT:-false}
      DRS_ENRICHMENT_TTL_HOURS: ${DRS_ENRICHMENT_TTL_HOURS:-12}
      DRS_ONLINE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS: ${DRS_ONLINE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:-8}
      DRS_GITHUB_TOKEN: ${DRS_GITHUB_TOKEN:-}
      DRS_NVD_API_KEY: ${DRS_NVD_API_KEY:-}
    volumes:
      - drs_data:/data
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:8088:8088"
    security_opt:
      - no-new-privileges:true
    cap_drop:
      - ALL
    tmpfs:
      - /tmp:size=64m,mode=1777
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "python", "-c", "import urllib.request; urllib.request.urlopen('http://127.0.0.1:8088/api/v1/health', timeout=3).read()"]
      interval: 30s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 3
      start_period: 10s

volumes:
  drs_data:

```
